================================================================================
===== services/apps/catalog/src/catalog.service.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Injectable, Logger, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { In, Repository } from 'typeorm';
import { badRequest, conflict, notFound } from '@autos/common';
import { MOCK_CONSTANTS, MOCK_DEPOT_SCORES, MOCK_DEPOTS, MOCK_SUPPLIERS, MOCK_VEHICLES } from './data/mock-catalog';
import {
  DEFAULT_MAINTENANCE_KM,
  DEFAULT_MIN_DRIVER_AGE,
  Depot,
  MaintenanceLog,
  Supplier,
  Vehicle,
  VehicleStatus,
} from './entities/catalog.entities';

const VEHICLE_FIELDS = [
  'vehicle_id', 'make', 'model', 'car_type', 'transmission', 'doors', 'bag_capacity', 'seats',
  'price_per_day', 'supplier_id', 'depot_id', 'image_url', 'status', 'min_driver_age',
] as const;
const DEPOT_FIELDS = ['depot_id', 'name', 'city_id', 'airport', 'score'] as const;

/** Mensajes del contrato catalog.proto (proto3 no tiene nulos: vacío/0 = no definido). */
export function toVehicleMsg(v: Vehicle) {
  return {
    id: v.id,
    vehicle_id: v.vehicle_id,
    make: v.make,
    model: v.model,
    car_type: v.car_type,
    transmission: v.transmission,
    doors: v.doors,
    bag_capacity: v.bag_capacity,
    seats: v.seats,
    price_per_day: v.price_per_day,
    supplier_id: v.supplier_id,
    depot_id: v.depot_id,
    image_url: v.image_url ?? '',
    status: v.status,
    min_driver_age: v.min_driver_age ?? DEFAULT_MIN_DRIVER_AGE,
    odometer_km: v.odometer_km ?? 0,
    next_maintenance_km: v.next_maintenance_km ?? DEFAULT_MAINTENANCE_KM,
    available_from: v.available_from ? new Date(v.available_from).toISOString() : '',
  };
}

export function toDepotMsg(d: Depot) {
  return {
    id: d.id,
    depot_id: d.depot_id,
    name: d.name,
    city_id: d.city_id,
    airport: d.airport ?? '',
    score: d.score ?? 0,
  };
}

@Injectable()
export class CatalogService implements OnModuleInit {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(CatalogService.name);

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Vehicle) private readonly vehicles: Repository<Vehicle>,
    @InjectRepository(Depot) private readonly depots: Repository<Depot>,
    @InjectRepository(Supplier) private readonly suppliers: Repository<Supplier>,
    @InjectRepository(MaintenanceLog) private readonly logbook: Repository<MaintenanceLog>,
  ) {}

  // ── Siembra idempotente (por id público, no pisa ediciones del admin) ─────
  async onModuleInit() {
    const knownSuppliers = new Set((await this.suppliers.find()).map((s) => s.supplier_id));
    const newSuppliers = MOCK_SUPPLIERS.filter((s) => !knownSuppliers.has(s.supplier_id));
    if (newSuppliers.length) await this.suppliers.save(newSuppliers.map((s) => this.suppliers.create(s)));

    const knownDepots = new Set((await this.depots.find()).map((d) => d.depot_id));
    const newDepots = MOCK_DEPOTS.filter((d) => !knownDepots.has(d.depot_id));
    if (newDepots.length) {
      await this.depots.save(
        newDepots.map((d) =>
          this.depots.create({ ...d, airport: d.airport ?? null, score: MOCK_DEPOT_SCORES[d.depot_id] ?? 0 }),
        ),
      );
    }

    const knownVehicles = new Set((await this.vehicles.find({ select: { vehicle_id: true } })).map((v) => v.vehicle_id));
    const newVehicles = MOCK_VEHICLES.filter((v) => !knownVehicles.has(v.vehicle_id));
    if (newVehicles.length) {
      await this.vehicles.save(
        newVehicles.map((v, i) =>
          this.vehicles.create({
            ...v,
            image_url: `/cars/${v.vehicle_id}.jpg`,
            min_driver_age: DEFAULT_MIN_DRIVER_AGE,
            odometer_km: 1500 + ((i * 1873) % 8200), // flota de demostración con kilometrajes variados
            next_maintenance_km: DEFAULT_MAINTENANCE_KM,
          }),
        ),
      );
    }
    // Autos de una base anterior al contrato v2 (odómetro en 0): se les da un kilometraje de demostración.
    const untracked = await this.vehicles.find({ where: { odometer_km: 0 }, order: { vehicle_id: 'ASC' } });
    if (untracked.length) {
      untracked.forEach((v, i) => (v.odometer_km = 1500 + ((i * 1873) % 8200)));
      await this.vehicles.save(untracked);
    }
    this.logger.log(
      `Catálogo sembrado: +${newVehicles.length} autos, +${newDepots.length} agencias, +${newSuppliers.length} proveedores`,
    );
  }

  // ── Consulta ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /**
   * Fin del cooldown de limpieza: los autos EN_LIMPIEZA cuyo plazo venció vuelven a DISPONIBLE. Se evalúa al
   * consultar (sin temporizadores), así sobrevive a reinicios del servicio.
   */
  private async expireCooldowns() {
    await this.vehicles
      .createQueryBuilder()
      .update(Vehicle)
      .set({ status: VehicleStatus.AVAILABLE, available_from: null })
      .where('status = :s AND available_from <= now()', { s: VehicleStatus.CLEANING })
      .execute();
  }

  async search(req: { pickup_location?: { airport?: string; city_id?: number }; car_types?: string[]; transmission?: string[]; limit?: number }) {
    await this.expireCooldowns();
    const where: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    if (req.car_types?.length) where.car_type = In(req.car_types);
    if (req.transmission?.length) where.transmission = In(req.transmission);

    // La agencia de recogida manda: solo se ofrecen autos de la ciudad/aeropuerto elegido.
    const loc = req.pickup_location;
    if (loc?.airport || loc?.city_id) {
      const all = await this.depots.find();
      where.depot_id = In(
        all
          .filter((d) =>
            loc.airport ? d.airport?.toUpperCase() === loc.airport.toUpperCase() : d.city_id === loc.city_id,
          )
          .map((d) => d.depot_id),
      );
    }
    const found = await this.vehicles.find({ where, take: req.limit || 100 });
    return { vehicles: found.map(toVehicleMsg) };
  }

  async getVehicles(req: { vehicle_ids?: string[] }) {
    await this.expireCooldowns();
    const found = req.vehicle_ids?.length
      ? await this.vehicles.find({ where: { vehicle_id: In(req.vehicle_ids) } })
      : await this.vehicles.find();
    return { vehicles: found.map(toVehicleMsg) };
  }

  async listDepots() {
    return { depots: (await this.depots.find()).map(toDepotMsg) };
  }

  async listSuppliers(req: { supplier_ids?: number[] }) {
    const found = req.supplier_ids?.length
      ? await this.suppliers.find({ where: { supplier_id: In(req.supplier_ids) } })
      : await this.suppliers.find();
    return { suppliers: found.map((s) => ({ supplier_id: s.supplier_id, name: s.name })) };
  }

  getConstants(req: { constants?: string[] }) {
    const keys = req.constants?.length ? req.constants : Object.keys(MOCK_CONSTANTS);
    const data: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const k of keys) data[k] = MOCK_CONSTANTS[k];
    return { data_json: JSON.stringify(data) };
  }

  // ── Disponibilidad ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  /** Reserva atómica: solo una petición puede pasar de AVAILABLE a RESERVED. */
  async reserveVehicle(req: { vehicle_id: string }) {
    await this.expireCooldowns();
    const r = await this.vehicles.update(
      { vehicle_id: req.vehicle_id, status: VehicleStatus.AVAILABLE },
      { status: VehicleStatus.RESERVED },
    );
    return { reserved: !!r.affected };
  }

  async releaseVehicle(req: { vehicle_id: string }) {
    await this.vehicles.update({ vehicle_id: req.vehicle_id }, { status: VehicleStatus.AVAILABLE, available_from: null });
  }

  /**
   * Transición atómica de la máquina de estados: solo ocurre si el auto está en alguno de `from_statuses`
   * (vacío = cualquiera). CLEANING fija el fin del cooldown; el resto lo borra.
   */
  async transitionVehicle(req: { vehicle_id: string; from_statuses?: string[]; to_status: string; odometer_km?: number; available_from?: string }) {
    if (!(req.to_status in VehicleStatus)) throw badRequest(`to_status inválido: "${req.to_status}".`);
    await this.expireCooldowns();
    const patch: Partial<Vehicle> = {
      status: req.to_status as VehicleStatus,
      available_from: req.to_status === VehicleStatus.CLEANING && req.available_from ? new Date(req.available_from) : null,
    };
    if (req.odometer_km) patch.odometer_km = req.odometer_km;
    const r = await this.vehicles.update(
      { vehicle_id: req.vehicle_id, ...(req.from_statuses?.length ? { status: In(req.from_statuses) } : {}) },
      patch,
    );
    const v = await this.vehicles.findOneBy({ vehicle_id: req.vehicle_id });
    if (!v) throw notFound('Vehículo no encontrado', `Vehículo "${req.vehicle_id}" no encontrado`);
    return { changed: !!r.affected, vehicle: toVehicleMsg(v) };
  }

  /**
   * Registro técnico (rol MANTENIMIENTO/ADMIN): actualiza el odómetro, evalúa el umbral preventivo y asienta
   * la bitácora. Un auto que supera el umbral sale de circulación (EN_MANTENIMIENTO) hasta que el técnico
   * confirme que el mantenimiento se realizó.
   */
  async recordMaintenance(req: {
    vehicle_id: string; technician_id: string; odometer_km: number; fuel_level: number; body_condition?: string;
    tires_condition?: string; notes?: string; maintenance_done?: boolean; new_next_maintenance_km?: number;
  }) {
    const v = await this.vehicles.findOneBy({ vehicle_id: req.vehicle_id });
    if (!v) throw notFound('Vehículo no encontrado', `Vehículo "${req.vehicle_id}" no encontrado`);

    v.odometer_km = req.odometer_km;
    if (req.maintenance_done) v.next_maintenance_km = req.new_next_maintenance_km || req.odometer_km + DEFAULT_MAINTENANCE_KM;
    const alert = v.odometer_km >= v.next_maintenance_km;
    if (alert && v.status === VehicleStatus.AVAILABLE) v.status = VehicleStatus.MAINTENANCE;
    if (!alert && v.status === VehicleStatus.MAINTENANCE) v.status = VehicleStatus.AVAILABLE;
    await this.vehicles.save(v);

    const log = await this.logbook.save(
      this.logbook.create({
        vehicle_id: v.vehicle_id,
        technician_id: req.technician_id,
        odometer_km: req.odometer_km,
        fuel_level: req.fuel_level,
        body_condition: req.body_condition || null,
        tires_condition: req.tires_condition || null,
        notes: req.notes || null,
        maintenance_done: !!req.maintenance_done,
        alert,
      }),
    );
    return {
      vehicle: toVehicleMsg(v),
      alert,
      log_id: `BIT-AUTO-${String(log.id).padStart(2, '0')}`,
      timestamp: log.created_at.toISOString(),
    };
  }

  // ── Administración: vehículos ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  async adminListVehicles() {
    await this.expireCooldowns();
    return { vehicles: (await this.vehicles.find({ order: { make: 'ASC' } })).map(toVehicleMsg) };
  }

  async adminCreateVehicle(input: any) {
    if (await this.vehicles.findOneBy({ vehicle_id: input.vehicle_id })) {
      throw conflict(`Ya existe un vehículo con vehicle_id "${input.vehicle_id}".`);
    }
    const saved = await this.vehicles.save(this.vehicles.create(this.vehicleFromInput(input, VEHICLE_FIELDS as any)));
    return toVehicleMsg(saved);
  }

  async adminUpdateVehicle(req: { id: string; patch: any; fields: string[] }) {
    const v = await this.vehicles.findOneBy({ id: req.id });
    if (!v) throw notFound('Vehículo no encontrado', `Vehículo "${req.id}" no encontrado`);
    Object.assign(v, this.vehicleFromInput(req.patch, req.fields));
    return toVehicleMsg(await this.vehicles.save(v));
  }

  async adminSetVehicleStatus(req: { id: string; status: string }) {
    if (!(req.status in VehicleStatus)) throw badRequest(`status debe ser uno de: ${Object.keys(VehicleStatus).join(', ')}.`);
    const v = await this.vehicles.findOneBy({ id: req.id });
    if (!v) throw notFound('Vehículo no encontrado', `Vehículo "${req.id}" no encontrado`);
    v.status = req.status as VehicleStatus;
    if (v.status !== VehicleStatus.CLEANING) v.available_from = null;
    return toVehicleMsg(await this.vehicles.save(v));
  }

  async adminDeleteVehicle(req: { id: string }) {
    const r = await this.vehicles.delete(req.id);
    if (!r.affected) throw notFound('Vehículo no encontrado', `Vehículo "${req.id}" no encontrado`);
  }

  // ── Administración: agencias ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  async adminListDepots() {
    return { depots: (await this.depots.find({ order: { name: 'ASC' } })).map(toDepotMsg) };
  }

  async adminCreateDepot(input: any) {
    if (await this.depots.findOneBy({ depot_id: input.depot_id })) {
      throw conflict(`Ya existe una agencia con depot_id ${input.depot_id}.`);
    }
    const saved = await this.depots.save(this.depots.create(this.depotFromInput(input, DEPOT_FIELDS as any)));
    return toDepotMsg(saved);
  }

  async adminUpdateDepot(req: { id: string; patch: any; fields: string[] }) {
    const d = await this.depots.findOneBy({ id: req.id });
    if (!d) throw notFound('Agencia no encontrada', `Depot "${req.id}" no encontrado`);
    Object.assign(d, this.depotFromInput(req.patch, req.fields));
    return toDepotMsg(await this.depots.save(d));
  }

  async adminDeleteDepot(req: { id: string }) {
    const r = await this.depots.delete(req.id);
    if (!r.affected) throw notFound('Agencia no encontrada', `Depot "${req.id}" no encontrado`);
  }

  // ── helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  private vehicleFromInput(input: any, fields: readonly string[]) {
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const f of fields) {
      if (!(VEHICLE_FIELDS as readonly string[]).includes(f)) continue;
      let val = input[f];
      if (f === 'image_url') val = val || null;
      if (f === 'min_driver_age') val = val || null; // 0 = por defecto
      if (f === 'status') val = val || VehicleStatus.AVAILABLE;
      out[f] = val;
    }
    return out;
  }

  private depotFromInput(input: any, fields: readonly string[]) {
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const f of fields) {
      if (!(DEPOT_FIELDS as readonly string[]).includes(f)) continue;
      out[f] = f === 'airport' ? input[f] || null : input[f];
    }
    return out;
  }
}

export const MOCK_CONSTANTS: Record<string, unknown> = {
  fuel_policies: ['FULL_TO_FULL', 'FULL_TO_EMPTY', 'PREPAID'],
  fuel_types: ['GASOLINA', 'DIESEL', 'HIBRIDO', 'ELECTRICO'],
  transmission: ['Manual', 'Automatica'],
  payment_timings: ['PAGO_ANTICIPADO', 'PAGO_EN_DEPOSITO'],
  depot_services: ['ENTREGA_A_DOMICILIO', 'SILLA_BEBE', 'GPS', 'CONDUCTOR_ADICIONAL'],

================================================================================
===== services/apps/catalog/src/entities/catalog.entities.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Column, CreateDateColumn, Entity, PrimaryGeneratedColumn } from 'typeorm';
import { ValueTransformer } from 'typeorm';

class NumericTransformer implements ValueTransformer {
  to(data: number | null): number | null {
    return data;
  }
  from(data: string | null): number | null {
    return data ? parseFloat(data) : null;
  }
}

/** Máquina de estados de 6 fases (contrato v2 · Cátedra RDA2 §5). El gateway los traduce a DISPONIBLE, RESERVADO, … */
export enum VehicleStatus {
  AVAILABLE = 'AVAILABLE',
  RESERVED = 'RESERVED',
  READY_FOR_DELIVERY = 'READY_FOR_DELIVERY',
  DELIVERED = 'DELIVERED',
  CLEANING = 'CLEANING',
  MAINTENANCE = 'MAINTENANCE',
}

/** Cooldown obligatorio de limpieza tras la devolución. */
export const CLEANING_COOLDOWN_MINUTES = 120;
export const DEFAULT_MAINTENANCE_KM = 10000;

export const DEFAULT_MIN_DRIVER_AGE = 18; // mayoría de edad en Ecuador

@Entity('vehicles')
export class Vehicle {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 50, unique: true })
  vehicle_id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100 })
  make: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100 })
  model: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 50 })
  car_type: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 50 })
  transmission: string;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  doors: number;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  bag_capacity: number;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  seats: number;

  @Column('numeric', { precision: 10, scale: 2, transformer: new NumericTransformer() })
  price_per_day: number;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  supplier_id: number;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  depot_id: number;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 300, nullable: true })
  image_url: string | null;

  // null = valor por defecto (18)
  @Column({ type: 'int', nullable: true })
  min_driver_age: number | null;

  // RESERVED = tiene una reserva activa; solo el admin (o cancelar la orden) lo devuelve a AVAILABLE.
  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 20, default: VehicleStatus.AVAILABLE })
  status: VehicleStatus;

  @Column({ type: 'int', default: 0 })
  odometer_km: number;

  @Column({ type: 'int', default: DEFAULT_MAINTENANCE_KM })
  next_maintenance_km: number;

  // Fin del cooldown de limpieza: hasta entonces el auto está EN_LIMPIEZA y no aparece en las búsquedas.
  @Column({ type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true })
  available_from: Date | null;
}

/** Bitácora de mantenimiento (rol técnico). */
@Entity('maintenance_log')
export class MaintenanceLog {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn()
  id: number;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 50 })
  vehicle_id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100 })
  technician_id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  odometer_km: number;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  fuel_level: number;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 300, nullable: true })
  body_condition: string | null;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100, nullable: true })
  tires_condition: string | null;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 500, nullable: true })
  notes: string | null;

  @Column({ type: 'boolean', default: false })
  maintenance_done: boolean;

  @Column({ type: 'boolean', default: false })
  alert: boolean;

  @CreateDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' })
  created_at: Date;
}

@Entity('depots')
export class Depot {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'int', unique: true })
  depot_id: number;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 200 })
  name: string;

  @Column({ type: 'int' })
  city_id: number;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 10, nullable: true })
  airport: string | null;

  @Column('numeric', { precision: 3, scale: 2, transformer: new NumericTransformer(), default: 0 })
  score: number;
}

@Entity('suppliers')
export class Supplier {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn()
  id: number;

  @Column({ type: 'int', unique: true })
  supplier_id: number;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100 })
  name: string;
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/backends.ts =====

import { Inject, Injectable, Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClientGrpc, ClientsModule } from '@nestjs/microservices';
import { GrpcCaller, grpcClientOptions } from '@autos/common';

/** Direcciones de los microservicios (variables de entorno; por defecto, desarrollo local). */
const urls = {
  catalog: () => process.env.CATALOG_GRPC_URL ?? '127.0.0.1:50051',
  booking: () => process.env.BOOKING_GRPC_URL ?? '127.0.0.1:50052',
  payments: () => process.env.PAYMENTS_GRPC_URL ?? '127.0.0.1:50053',
  identity: () => process.env.IDENTITY_GRPC_URL ?? '127.0.0.1:50054',
};

/** Acceso tipado a los cuatro microservicios por gRPC (con correlation id y errores ProblemDetails). */
@Injectable()
export class Backends {
  readonly catalog: GrpcCaller;
  readonly booking: GrpcCaller;
  readonly payments: GrpcCaller;
  readonly identity: GrpcCaller;

  constructor(
    @Inject('CATALOG_CLIENT') catalog: ClientGrpc,
    @Inject('BOOKING_CLIENT') booking: ClientGrpc,
    @Inject('PAYMENTS_CLIENT') payments: ClientGrpc,
    @Inject('IDENTITY_CLIENT') identity: ClientGrpc,
  ) {
    this.catalog = new GrpcCaller(catalog, 'CatalogService');
    this.booking = new GrpcCaller(booking, 'BookingService');
    this.payments = new GrpcCaller(payments, 'PaymentsService');
    this.identity = new GrpcCaller(identity, 'IdentityService');
  }
}

@Module({
  imports: [
    ClientsModule.register([
      { name: 'CATALOG_CLIENT', ...grpcClientOptions('autos.catalog.v1', 'autos/catalog/v1/catalog.proto', urls.catalog()) },
      { name: 'BOOKING_CLIENT', ...grpcClientOptions('autos.booking.v1', 'autos/booking/v1/booking.proto', urls.booking()) },
      { name: 'PAYMENTS_CLIENT', ...grpcClientOptions('autos.payments.v1', 'autos/payments/v1/payments.proto', urls.payments()) },
      { name: 'IDENTITY_CLIENT', ...grpcClientOptions('autos.identity.v1', 'autos/identity/v1/identity.proto', urls.identity()) },
    ]),
  ],
  providers: [Backends],
  exports: [Backends],
})
export class BackendsModule {}

================================================================================
===== services/apps/gateway/src/common/auth.ts =====
================================================================================
import {
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  HttpException,
  HttpStatus,
  Injectable,
  SetMetadata,
  applyDecorators,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { ApiBearerAuth } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { JwtTokens, Principal, Role, unauthorized } from '@autos/common';

/**
 * Seguridad del gateway (JWT + roles).
 *
 * El gateway valida el JWT (HS256) de cada petición y deja el `principal` en `req.principal`.
 * Las rutas marcadas con @Roles(...) se protegen según estas variables de entorno:
 *   AUTH_ENFORCE_ADMIN=true     → /admin/** exige un JWT de ADMIN (401 sin token, 403 con otro rol)
 *   AUTH_ENFORCE_CUSTOMER=true  → /auth/me y /me/orders exigen un JWT de CLIENTE
 * Por defecto están apagadas para que Swagger se pueda probar sin tokens (decisión del equipo);
 * activarlas no requiere cambios de código. Cuando están activas, Swagger muestra el candado.
 */
const flag = (name: string) => /^(1|true)$/i.test(process.env[name] ?? '');
export const enforceAdmin = () => flag('AUTH_ENFORCE_ADMIN');
export const enforceCustomer = () => flag('AUTH_ENFORCE_CUSTOMER');

export const ROLES_KEY = 'roles';

/** Restringe una ruta a uno o más roles. Aplica el candado de Swagger solo si la protección está activa. */
export function Roles(...roles: Role[]) {
  const enforced = roles.includes('ADMIN') ? enforceAdmin() : enforceCustomer();
  return applyDecorators(SetMetadata(ROLES_KEY, roles), ...(enforced ? [ApiBearerAuth()] : []));
}

declare module 'express-serve-static-core' {
  interface Request {
    principal?: Principal | null;
  }
}

@Injectable()
export class AuthGuard implements CanActivate {
  private readonly tokens = new JwtTokens();

  constructor(private readonly reflector: Reflector) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    // Los resolvers GraphQL no usan @Roles; su petición vive en el contexto de Apollo, no en el HTTP de Nest.
    if (context.getType<string>() !== 'http') return true;
    const req = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const header: string | undefined = req.headers?.authorization;
    const token = header?.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : undefined;
    const principal = this.tokens.verify(token);
    req.principal = principal;

    const roles = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<Role[] | undefined>(ROLES_KEY, [
      context.getHandler(),
      context.getClass(),
    ]);
    if (!roles?.length) return true;

    const enforced = roles.includes('ADMIN') ? enforceAdmin() : enforceCustomer();
    if (!enforced) return true;

    if (!principal) throw unauthorized('Falta un token JWT válido (Authorization: Bearer <token>).');
    if (!roles.includes(principal.role)) {
      throw new HttpException(
        {
          type: 'https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/forbidden',
          title: 'Prohibido',
          status: HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN,
          detail: `Esta ruta requiere el rol ${roles.join(' o ')}; el token es de ${principal.role}.`,
          code: 'FORBIDDEN',
        },
        HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN,
      );
    }
    return true;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Contrato REST v2: seguridad BearerAuth (JWT) o ApiKeyAuth (X-API-Key), roles CLIENTE | ADMIN | MANTENIMIENTO
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type V2Role = 'CLIENTE' | 'ADMIN' | 'MANTENIMIENTO';
export const V2_ROLES_KEY = 'v2roles';

/** Restringe una ruta v2 a ciertos roles (RBAC RDA2 §5). Sin argumentos solo exige autenticación. */
export const V2Roles = (...roles: V2Role[]) => SetMetadata(V2_ROLES_KEY, roles);

export const enforceV2 = () => flag('AUTH_ENFORCE_V2');

/**
 * Guard de las rutas protegidas del contrato v2.
 *  · Autenticación: JWT válido (Authorization: Bearer) o clave M2M (X-API-Key = BOOKING_API_KEY).
 *  · Rol: el del JWT (ADMIN→ADMIN, CUSTOMER→CLIENTE) o, con clave M2M, el de la cabecera X-Role.
 * Con AUTH_ENFORCE_V2=true exige 401 sin credenciales y 403 con rol insuficiente. Con la bandera apagada
 * (por defecto, para probar en Swagger sin tokens) solo rechaza 403 si se envía explícitamente un X-Role
 * que no corresponde a la ruta.
 */
@Injectable()
export class V2AccessGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(private readonly reflector: Reflector) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    if (context.getType<string>() !== 'http') return true;
    const req = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const roles = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<V2Role[] | undefined>(V2_ROLES_KEY, [context.getHandler(), context.getClass()]) ?? [];

    const expectedKey = process.env.BOOKING_API_KEY;
    const apiKeyOk = !!expectedKey && req.headers['x-api-key'] === expectedKey;
    const principal: Principal | null | undefined = req.principal;
    const authenticated = apiKeyOk || !!principal;

    const headerRole = String(req.headers['x-role'] ?? '').toUpperCase() as V2Role | '';
    const role: V2Role | '' =
      apiKeyOk && headerRole ? headerRole : principal ? (principal.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'ADMIN' : 'CLIENTE') : headerRole;

    const forbidden = () =>
      new HttpException(
        {
          type: 'https://booking.epn.edu.ec/errors/forbidden',
          title: 'Acceso restringido por rol',
          status: HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN,
          detail: `Esta operación requiere el rol ${roles.join(' o ')}${role ? `; el rol recibido es ${role}` : ''}.`,
          code: 'FORBIDDEN',
        },
        HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN,
      );

    if (!enforceV2()) {
      if (roles.length && headerRole && !roles.includes(headerRole)) throw forbidden();
      return true;
    }
    if (!authenticated) throw unauthorized('Falta un JWT válido (Authorization: Bearer) o una clave de API (X-API-Key).');
    if (roles.length && !roles.includes(role as V2Role)) throw forbidden();
    return true;
  }
}

================================================================================
===== services/apps/gateway/src/common/idempotency.ts =====
================================================================================
import {
  CallHandler,
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  HttpException,
  HttpStatus,
  Injectable,
  NestInterceptor,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Observable, of, tap } from 'rxjs';
import { TtlCache } from '@autos/common';

const UUID_REGEX = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;

function bad(title: string, detail: string, type: string) {
  return new HttpException(
    { type: `https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/${type}`, title, status: HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, detail, code: 'VALIDATION_FAILED' },
    HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
  );
}

/** Exige la cabecera Idempotency-Key (UUID v4) en las operaciones transaccionales. */
@Injectable()
export class IdempotencyKeyGuard implements CanActivate {
  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const key = context.switchToHttp().getRequest().headers['idempotency-key'] as string | undefined;
    if (!key || !key.trim()) {
      throw bad(
        'Idempotency-Key header is required',
        'All transactional endpoints require an Idempotency-Key header (UUID v4) to prevent duplicate operations such as double charges.',
        'missing-idempotency-key',
      );
    }
    if (!UUID_REGEX.test(key)) {
      throw bad('Invalid Idempotency-Key format', `The Idempotency-Key header must be a valid UUID v4. Received: "${key}".`, 'invalid-idempotency-key');
    }
    return true;
  }
}

/**
 * Idempotencia real: si llega otra vez la misma Idempotency-Key para la misma operación, se devuelve la
 * respuesta guardada en vez de ejecutarla de nuevo (un doble clic no duplica la reserva ni el cobro).
 * Solo se guardan las respuestas exitosas; las fallidas pueden reintentarse con la misma clave.
 */
@Injectable()
export class IdempotencyInterceptor implements NestInterceptor {
  private readonly cache = new TtlCache();
  private static readonly TTL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

  intercept(context: ExecutionContext, next: CallHandler): Observable<any> {
    const http = context.switchToHttp();
    const req = http.getRequest();
    const res = http.getResponse();
    const key = req.headers['idempotency-key'] as string | undefined;
    if (!key) return next.handle();

    const cacheKey = `${req.method}:${req.route?.path ?? req.path}:${JSON.stringify(req.params ?? {})}:${key}`;
    const fingerprint = JSON.stringify(req.body ?? null);
    const hit = this.cache.get<{ body: unknown; fingerprint: string }>(cacheKey);
    if (hit) {
      // Misma clave con otra petición distinta: no es un reintento, es un uso indebido de la clave.
      if (hit.fingerprint !== fingerprint) {
        throw new HttpException(
          {
            type: 'https://booking.epn.edu.ec/errors/idempotency-conflict',
            title: 'Conflicto de idempotencia',
            status: HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
            detail: 'La Idempotency-Key ya se usó con un cuerpo distinto. Genera una clave nueva (UUID v4) para una operación diferente.',
            code: 'IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT',
          },
          HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
        );
      }
      res.setHeader('Idempotent-Replayed', 'true');
      return of(hit.body);
    }
    return next.handle().pipe(tap((body) => this.cache.set(cacheKey, { body, fingerprint }, IdempotencyInterceptor.TTL_MS)));
  }
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/common/mappers.ts =====
================================================================================
/**
 * Mapeadores gRPC → REST. El contrato REST (autos-openapi.yaml) no cambia: el gateway traduce los mensajes
 * .proto (sin nulos) a las formas JSON que ya consumen el frontend y los demás grupos.
 */

const orNull = <T>(v: T | '' | 0 | undefined | null): T | null => (v === '' || v === 0 || v === undefined || v === null ? null : (v as T));

export function normalizeLocation(loc: any) {
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (loc?.airport) out.airport = loc.airport;
  if (loc?.city_id) out.city_id = loc.city_id;
  return out;
}

export function normalizeRoute(route: any) {
  if (!route) return null;
  return {
    pickup: { datetime: route.pickup?.datetime ?? '', location: normalizeLocation(route.pickup?.location) },
    dropoff: { datetime: route.dropoff?.datetime ?? '', location: normalizeLocation(route.dropoff?.location) },
  };
}

/** Vehículo tal como lo muestran /details y las órdenes (snapshot). */
export function vehicleSnapshot(v: any) {
  if (!v) return null;
  return { ...v, image_url: orNull(v.image_url) };
}

export function vehicleToDetails(v: any) {
  return {
    vehicle_id: v.vehicle_id,
    make: v.make,
    model: v.model,
    doors: v.doors,
    bag_capacity: v.bag_capacity,
    seats: v.seats,
    image_url: orNull(v.image_url),
    status: v.status,
    min_driver_age: v.min_driver_age,
    depot_id: v.depot_id,
  };
}

export function vehicleToAdmin(v: any) {
  return {
    id: v.id,
    vehicle_id: v.vehicle_id,
    make: v.make,
    model: v.model,
    car_type: v.car_type,
    transmission: v.transmission,
    doors: v.doors,
    bag_capacity: v.bag_capacity,
    seats: v.seats,
    price_per_day: v.price_per_day,
    supplier_id: v.supplier_id,
    depot_id: v.depot_id,
    image_url: orNull(v.image_url),
    status: v.status,
    min_driver_age: v.min_driver_age,
  };
}

export function depotToAdmin(d: any) {
  return { id: d.id, depot_id: d.depot_id, name: d.name, city_id: d.city_id, airport: orNull(d.airport), score: d.score };
}

export function orderToDetail(o: any) {
  const links: Record<string, string> = { self: `/api/v1/orders/${o.order_id}` };
  if (o.status === 'CONFIRMED') {
    links.modify = `/api/v1/orders/${o.order_id}/modify`;
    links.cancel = `/api/v1/orders/${o.order_id}/cancel`;
  }
  return {
    order_id: o.order_id,
    locator: o.locator,
    status: o.status,
    vehicle_details: vehicleSnapshot(o.vehicle),
    route_details: normalizeRoute(o.route),
    extras: o.extras ?? [],
    total_price: o.total_price,
    currency: o.currency,
    creation_date: o.creation_date,
    _links: links,
  };
}

/** Forma "cruda" que consumen el panel Admin y "Mis reservas". */
export function orderToRaw(o: any) {
  return {
    id: o.order_id,
    locator: o.locator,
    status: o.status,
    vehicle_details: vehicleSnapshot(o.vehicle),
    route_details: normalizeRoute(o.route),
    driver_details: o.driver_details ?? null,
    extras: o.extras ?? [],
    total_price: o.total_price,
    currency: o.currency,
    payment_reference: orNull(o.payment_reference),
    customer_id: orNull(o.customer_id),
    creation_date: o.creation_date,
  };
}

export function profileToRest(p: any) {
  return { id: p.id, email: p.email, first_name: p.first_name, last_name: p.last_name, phone_number: orNull(p.phone_number) };
}

export function userToRest(u: any) {
  return {
    id: orNull(u.id),
    role: u.role,
    login: u.login,
    first_name: orNull(u.first_name),
    last_name: orNull(u.last_name),
    phone_number: orNull(u.phone_number),
    created_at: orNull(u.created_at),
    protected: u.protected,
  };
}

export function webhookToRest(w: any) {
  return { id: w.id, url: w.url, events: w.events, secret: orNull(w.secret) };
}

/** Máscara de actualización: campos presentes en el DTO de un PUT parcial. */
export function presentFields(dto: object): string[] {
  return Object.entries(dto)
    .filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)
    .map(([k]) => k);
}

================================================================================
===== services/apps/gateway/src/common/problem-details.filter.ts =====
================================================================================
import { ArgumentsHost, Catch, ExceptionFilter, HttpException, HttpStatus, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { CORRELATION_HEADER, currentCorrelationId } from '@autos/common';

/**
 * Traduce cualquier error al formato ProblemDetails (application/problem+json) del contrato.
 * Los errores que vienen de un microservicio ya traen su ProblemDetails (con `code`) y pasan tal cual.
 */
@Catch()
export class ProblemDetailsFilter implements ExceptionFilter {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('ProblemDetails');

  catch(exception: unknown, host: ArgumentsHost): void {
    if (host.getType() !== 'http') throw exception;
    const ctx = host.switchToHttp();
    const response = ctx.getResponse<Response>();
    const request = ctx.getRequest<Request>();
    const isV2 = !request.originalUrl?.startsWith('/api/v1');
    // Todo ProblemDetails lleva instance, correlation_id y timestamp (trazabilidad, contrato v2 y RFC 7807).
    const enrich = (p: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
      ...p,
      instance: p.instance ?? request.originalUrl?.split('?')[0],
      correlation_id: p.correlation_id ?? currentCorrelationId() ?? response.getHeader(CORRELATION_HEADER),
      timestamp: p.timestamp ?? new Date().toISOString(),
    });

    if (!(exception instanceof HttpException)) {
      this.logger.error((exception as Error)?.stack ?? String(exception));
      response
        .status(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
        .header('Content-Type', 'application/problem+json')
        .send(
          enrich({
            type: 'https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/internal',
            title: 'Error interno',
            status: 500,
            detail: 'Ocurrió un error inesperado.',
            code: 'INTERNAL',
          }),
        );
      return;
    }

    const status = exception.getStatus();
    const body = exception.getResponse();

    if (typeof body === 'object' && body !== null && 'code' in body) {
      response.status(status).header('Content-Type', 'application/problem+json').send(enrich(body as Record<string, unknown>));
      return;
    }

    const isValidationError =
      status === HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST &&
      typeof body === 'object' &&
      body !== null &&
      Array.isArray((body as { message?: unknown }).message);

    const problem = isValidationError
      ? {
          type: 'https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/validation-failed',
          title: 'Petición inválida',
          status,
          detail: 'Uno o más campos no cumplen con el contrato.',
          code: 'VALIDATION_FAILED',
          [isV2 ? 'invalid_params' : 'invalidParams']: (body as { message: string[] }).message.map((msg) => ({ name: msg.split(' ')[0], reason: msg })),
        }
      : {
          type: `https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/${status}`,
          title:
            (typeof body === 'object' && body && 'error' in body ? String((body as { error: unknown }).error) : exception.message) ||
            'Error',
          status,
          detail: exception.message,
          code: status === 401 ? 'UNAUTHORIZED' : status === 404 ? 'NOT_FOUND' : status === 409 ? 'CONFLICT' : 'VALIDATION_FAILED',
        };

    response.status(status).header('Content-Type', 'application/problem+json').send(enrich(problem));
  }
}

import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional, PartialType } from '@nestjs/swagger';

export class CreateDepotAdminDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 400 })
  @IsInt()
  depot_id: number;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'Terminal Terrestre Ambato' })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(2)
  name: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 4 })
  @IsInt()
  city_id: number;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 'UIO' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  airport?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 4.5, minimum: 0, maximum: 5 })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsNumber()
  @Min(0)
  @Max(5)
  score?: number;
}

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ format: 'uuid' })
  id: string;

===== services/apps/gateway/src/dto/admin/user-admin.dto.ts =====
================================================================================
import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { IsIn, IsOptional, IsString, Matches, MinLength } from 'class-validator';

export const USER_ROLES = ['ADMIN', 'CUSTOMER'] as const;
export type UserRole = (typeof USER_ROLES)[number];

export class CreateUserAdminDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    enum: USER_ROLES,
    description:
      'ADMIN: entra al panel de administración (/admin/auth/login con username). CUSTOMER: cliente que puede reservar (/auth/login con email).',
  })
  @IsIn(USER_ROLES)
  role: UserRole;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    example: 'maria.admin',
    description: 'Obligatorio si role=ADMIN. 3–60 caracteres: letras, números, punto, guion o guion bajo.',
  })
  @IsOptional()
  @Matches(/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{3,60}$/, {
    message: 'username debe tener 3–60 caracteres: letras, números, punto, guion o guion bajo',
  })
  username?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 'cliente@example.com', description: 'Obligatorio si role=CUSTOMER.' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  email?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'claveSegura123', minLength: 6 })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(6)
  password: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 'María', description: 'Obligatorio si role=CUSTOMER.' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  first_name?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 'Pérez', description: 'Obligatorio si role=CUSTOMER.' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  last_name?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: '0991234567' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  phone_number?: string;
}

export class UserAdminResponseDto {
  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid', nullable: true, description: 'null en el administrador de entorno.' })
  id: string | null;

  @ApiProperty({ enum: USER_ROLES })
  role: UserRole;

  @ApiProperty({ description: 'username (ADMIN) o email (CUSTOMER) con el que inicia sesión.' })
  login: string;

  @ApiProperty({ nullable: true })
  first_name: string | null;

  @ApiProperty({ nullable: true })
  last_name: string | null;

  @ApiProperty({ nullable: true })
  phone_number: string | null;

  @ApiProperty({ nullable: true })
  created_at: string | null;

  @ApiProperty({ description: 'true = cuenta de arranque (variables de entorno); no se puede eliminar.' })
  protected: boolean;
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/dto/admin/vehicle-admin.dto.ts =====
================================================================================
import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional, PartialType } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { VehicleStatus } from '../enums';
import { IsIn, IsInt, IsNumber, IsOptional, IsPositive, IsString, Min, MinLength } from 'class-validator';

const CAR_TYPES = ['Compacto', 'Sedan', 'SUV'];
const TRANSMISSIONS = ['Manual', 'Automatica'];
const VEHICLE_STATUSES = ['AVAILABLE', 'RESERVED'];

export class CreateVehicleAdminDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 'veh-009' })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(2)
  vehicle_id: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'Mazda' })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(1)
  make: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: '2' })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(1)
  model: string;

  @ApiProperty({ enum: CAR_TYPES })
  @IsIn(CAR_TYPES)
  car_type: string;

  @ApiProperty({ enum: TRANSMISSIONS })
  @IsIn(TRANSMISSIONS)
  transmission: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  @IsInt()
  @Min(2)
  doors: number;

  @ApiProperty()
  @IsInt()
  @Min(0)
  bag_capacity: number;

  @ApiProperty()
  @IsInt()
  @Min(1)
  seats: number;

  @ApiProperty()
  @IsNumber()
  @IsPositive()
  price_per_day: number;

  @ApiProperty()
  @IsInt()
  supplier_id: number;

  @ApiProperty()
  @IsInt()
  depot_id: number;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: '/cars/veh-009.jpg' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  image_url?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    enum: VEHICLE_STATUSES,
    description: 'RESERVED bloquea el auto para nuevas reservas hasta que el admin lo ponga AVAILABLE.',
  })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsIn(VEHICLE_STATUSES)
  status?: VehicleStatus;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 21, description: 'Edad mínima del conductor (por defecto 18).' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  @Min(18)
  min_driver_age?: number;
}

export class UpdateVehicleAdminDto extends PartialType(CreateVehicleAdminDto) {}

export class VehicleAdminResponseDto extends CreateVehicleAdminDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ format: 'uuid' })
  id: string;
}

import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Type } from 'class-transformer';
import {
  IsDateString,
  IsInt,
  IsNotEmpty,
  IsOptional,
  IsString,
  Matches,
  Max,
  Min,
  ValidateNested,
} from 'class-validator';

export class BookerDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 'ec', description: 'Código ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 del país del comprador.' })
  @IsString()
  @Matches(/^[a-z]{2}$/)
  country: string;
}

export class DriverDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 25, description: 'Edad del conductor.' })
  @IsInt()
  @Min(18)
  @Max(99)
  age: number;
}

export class LocationPointDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 'UIO', description: 'Código IATA de aeropuerto, si aplica.' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  airport?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ example: 1, description: 'ID interno de la ciudad.' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  city_id?: number;

  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  coordinates?: { latitude: number; longitude: number };
}

class RouteEndpointDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: '2026-10-01T10:00:00Z' })
  @IsDateString()
  datetime: string;

  @ApiProperty({ type: LocationPointDto })
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => LocationPointDto)
  location: LocationPointDto;
}

export class RouteDto {
  @ApiProperty({ type: RouteEndpointDto })
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => RouteEndpointDto)
  pickup: RouteEndpointDto;

  @ApiProperty({ type: RouteEndpointDto })
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => RouteEndpointDto)
  dropoff: RouteEndpointDto;
}

export class DriverDetailsDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 'Ana' })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  first_name: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'Pérez' })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  last_name: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'ana.perez@example.com' })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  email: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: '+593999999999' })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  phone_number: string;
}

export class MetadataDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  total_results?: number;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ nullable: true })
  @IsOptional()
  next_page?: string | null;

===== services/apps/gateway/src/dto/autos/payment.dto.ts =====
================================================================================
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Type } from 'class-transformer';
import { IsInt, IsNotEmpty, IsString, Matches, Max, Min, MinLength, ValidateNested } from 'class-validator';

export class CardDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 'Angel Loor' })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(3)
  holder_name: string;

  @ApiProperty({
    example: '4242424242424242',
    description:
      'Tarjeta de PRUEBA (simulación). 4242424242424242 = aprobada · 4000000000000002 = rechazada por fondos insuficientes. Debe pasar el algoritmo de Luhn.',
  })
  @Matches(/^\d{13,19}$/, { message: 'number debe tener entre 13 y 19 dígitos, sin espacios' })
  number: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 12, minimum: 1, maximum: 12 })
  @IsInt()
  @Min(1)
  @Max(12)
  expiry_month: number;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 2030 })
  @IsInt()
  @Min(2000)
  @Max(2100)
  expiry_year: number;

  @ApiProperty({ example: '123' })
  @Matches(/^\d{3,4}$/, { message: 'cvv debe tener 3 o 4 dígitos' })
  cvv: string;
}

export class PaymentRequestDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    description:
      'order_preview_id devuelto por /orders/preview. El monto se toma de ahí, no lo envía el cliente.',
  })
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  order_preview_id: string;

  @ApiProperty({ type: CardDto })
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => CardDto)
  card: CardDto;
}

export class PaymentResponseDto {
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Úsalo como payment_reference en /orders/create.' })
  payment_reference: string;

  @ApiProperty({ enum: ['APPROVED', 'DECLINED', 'REFUNDED'] })
  status: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  amount: number;

  @ApiProperty()
  currency: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'VISA' })
  card_brand: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: '4242' })
  card_last4: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  created_at: string;
}

import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';

export enum ProblemCode {
  VALIDATION_FAILED = 'VALIDATION_FAILED',
  CAR_NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE = 'CAR_NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE',
  PRICE_CHANGED = 'PRICE_CHANGED',
  DEPOT_CLOSED = 'DEPOT_CLOSED',
  DRIVER_AGE_RESTRICTION = 'DRIVER_AGE_RESTRICTION',
  BOOKING_NOT_CONFIRMED = 'BOOKING_NOT_CONFIRMED',
  CANCELLATION_NOT_ALLOWED = 'CANCELLATION_NOT_ALLOWED',
  RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED = 'RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED',
  PAYMENT_REFERENCE_INVALID = 'PAYMENT_REFERENCE_INVALID',
  PAYMENT_NOT_AUTHORIZED = 'PAYMENT_NOT_AUTHORIZED',
  PAYMENT_DECLINED = 'PAYMENT_DECLINED',
  PAYMENT_REQUIRED = 'PAYMENT_REQUIRED',
}

class InvalidParamDto {
  @ApiProperty()
  name: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  reason: string;
}

export class ProblemDetailsDto {
  @ApiProperty()
  type: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  title: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  status: number;

  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  detail?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ enum: ProblemCode })
  code: ProblemCode;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ type: [InvalidParamDto] })

}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/dto/autos/search.dto.ts =====

import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Type } from 'class-transformer';
import {
  IsArray,
  IsInt,
  IsOptional,
  IsString,
  Matches,
  Max,
  Min,
  ValidateNested,
} from 'class-validator';
import { BookerDto, DriverDto, RouteDto } from './common.dto';

class CarSearchFiltersDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional({ type: [String] })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsArray()
  @IsString({ each: true })
  car_types?: string[];

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ type: [String] })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsArray()
  @IsString({ each: true })
  transmission?: string[];
}

export class CarSearchRequestDto {
  @ApiProperty({ type: BookerDto })
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => BookerDto)
  booker: BookerDto;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'USD' })
  @IsString()
  @Matches(/^[A-Z]{3}$/)
  currency: string;

  @ApiProperty({ type: DriverDto })
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => DriverDto)
  driver: DriverDto;

  @ApiProperty({ type: RouteDto })
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => RouteDto)
  route: RouteDto;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ type: CarSearchFiltersDto })
  @IsOptional()
  @ValidateNested()
  @Type(() => CarSearchFiltersDto)
  filters?: CarSearchFiltersDto;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ default: 100, minimum: 10, maximum: 500 })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsInt()
  @Min(10)
  @Max(500)
  maximum_results?: number = 100;

  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  language?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()

  @ApiProperty()

  @ApiProperty()

  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Extra no contractual: edad mínima del conductor para este auto.' })
  min_driver_age: number;

  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Extra no contractual: agencia (depot) donde está el auto.' })

}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/dto/autos/webhook.dto.ts =====
================================================================================
import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { IsArray, IsIn, IsNotEmpty, IsOptional, IsString, IsUrl } from 'class-validator';
import { WebhookEvent } from '../enums';

const EVENT_VALUES = Object.values(WebhookEvent);

export class WebhookSubscriptionDto {
  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid' })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  id?: string;

  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uri' })
  @IsUrl({ require_tld: false })
  @IsNotEmpty()
  url: string;

  @ApiProperty({ enum: EVENT_VALUES, isArray: true })
  @IsArray()
  @IsIn(EVENT_VALUES, { each: true })
  events: WebhookEvent[];

  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  @IsOptional()
  @IsString()
  secret?: string;
}

export class WebhookPayloadDto {
  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid' })
  eventId: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  eventType: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  timestamp: string;

  @ApiProperty()
  resourceId: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ description: "Identificador de correlación de la petición que originó el evento (trazabilidad)." })
  correlationId?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional()
  data?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/gateway.module.ts =====

import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { APP_GUARD } from '@nestjs/core';
import { ApolloFederationDriver, ApolloFederationDriverConfig } from '@nestjs/apollo';
import { GraphQLModule } from '@nestjs/graphql';
import { join } from 'path';
import { protoDir } from '@autos/common';
import { BackendsModule } from './backends';
import { AuthGuard, V2AccessGuard } from './common/auth';
import { GRAPHQL_PROVIDERS } from './graphql/autos.resolvers';
import { AccountsController } from './rest/accounts.controller';
import { AdminController } from './rest/admin.controller';
import { AutosController } from './rest/autos.controller';
import { HealthController } from './rest/health.controller';
import { AutosV2Controller } from './v2/autos-v2.controller';

/** Subgrafo federado: el esquema es el contrato versionado de contracts/graphql/autos.graphql (schema-first). */
const graphqlSchema = join(protoDir(), '..', 'graphql', 'autos.graphql').replace(/\\/g, '/');

@Module({
  imports: [
    BackendsModule,
    GraphQLModule.forRoot<ApolloFederationDriverConfig>({
      driver: ApolloFederationDriver,
      typePaths: [graphqlSchema],
      path: '/graphql',
      introspection: true,
    }),
  ],
  controllers: [AutosController, AccountsController, AdminController, HealthController, AutosV2Controller],
  providers: [{ provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: AuthGuard }, V2AccessGuard, ...GRAPHQL_PROVIDERS],
})
export class GatewayModule {}

================================================================================
===== services/apps/gateway/src/graphql/autos.resolvers.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Args, Mutation, Parent, Query, ResolveField, ResolveReference, Resolver } from '@nestjs/graphql';
import { Backends } from '../backends';

const ESTADO_VEHICULO: Record<string, string> = { AVAILABLE: 'DISPONIBLE', RESERVED: 'RESERVADO' };
const ESTADO_ORDEN: Record<string, string> = { CONFIRMED: 'CONFIRMADA', CANCELLED: 'CANCELADA', PENDING: 'PENDIENTE' };
const ESTADO_ORDEN_INV: Record<string, string> = { CONFIRMADA: 'CONFIRMED', CANCELADA: 'CANCELLED', PENDIENTE: 'PENDING' };
const CIUDADES: Record<number, string> = { 1: 'Quito', 2: 'Guayaquil', 3: 'Cuenca' };

// ── Traducción de los mensajes gRPC (inglés/snake_case) al esquema GraphQL (español/camelCase) ────
export function toVehiculo(v: any) {
  return {
    id: v.vehicle_id,
    marca: v.make,
    modelo: v.model,
    tipo: v.car_type,
    transmision: v.transmission,
    puertas: v.doors,
    maletas: v.bag_capacity,
    asientos: v.seats,
    precioPorDia: v.price_per_day,
    imagenUrl: v.image_url || null,
    estado: ESTADO_VEHICULO[v.status] ?? 'DISPONIBLE',
    edadMinima: v.min_driver_age,
    _depotId: v.depot_id,
    _supplierId: v.supplier_id,
  };
}

export function toAgencia(d: any) {
  return {
    id: String(d.depot_id),
    nombre: d.name,
    ciudadId: d.city_id,
    ciudad: CIUDADES[d.city_id] ?? 'Ecuador',
    aeropuerto: d.airport || null,
    puntuacion: d.score || null,
  };
}

export function toOrden(o: any) {
  return {
    id: o.order_id,
    codigo: o.locator,
    estado: ESTADO_ORDEN[o.status] ?? 'PENDIENTE',
    vehiculo: toVehiculo(o.vehicle),
    total: o.total_price,
    moneda: o.currency,
    extras: o.extras ?? [],
    recogida: o.route?.pickup?.datetime || null,
    entrega: o.route?.dropoff?.datetime || null,
    clienteId: o.customer_id || null,
    fechaCreacion: o.creation_date,
  };
}

@Injectable()
class Lookups {
  constructor(readonly backends: Backends) {}

  async vehiculoPorId(id: string) {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('GetVehicles', { vehicle_ids: [id] });
    return r.vehicles[0] ? toVehiculo(r.vehicles[0]) : null;
  }

  async agenciaPorId(id: string | number) {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListDepots', {});
    const d = r.depots.find((x: any) => String(x.depot_id) === String(id));
    return d ? toAgencia(d) : null;
  }

  async ordenPorId(id: string) {
    try {
      return toOrden(await this.backends.booking.call('GetOrder', { order_id: id }));
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (e?.getStatus?.() === 404) return null;
      throw e;
    }
  }
}

// ── Raíz del esquema ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@Resolver()
export class AutosQueryResolver {
  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends, private readonly lookups: Lookups) {}

  @Query('vehiculos')
  async vehiculos(
    @Args('ciudadId') ciudadId?: number,
    @Args('aeropuerto') aeropuerto?: string,
    @Args('tipos') tipos?: string[],
    @Args('transmision') transmision?: string[],
  ) {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('Search', {
      pickup_location: { airport: aeropuerto ?? '', city_id: ciudadId ?? 0 },
      car_types: tipos ?? [],
      transmission: transmision ?? [],
    });
    return r.vehicles.map(toVehiculo);
  }

  @Query('vehiculo')
  vehiculo(@Args('id') id: string) {
    return this.lookups.vehiculoPorId(id);
  }

  @Query('agencias')
  async agencias() {
    return (await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListDepots', {})).depots.map(toAgencia);
  }

  @Query('orden')
  orden(@Args('id') id: string) {
    return this.lookups.ordenPorId(id);
  }

  @Query('ordenes')
  async ordenes(@Args('estado') estado?: string, @Args('clienteId') clienteId?: string) {
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ListOrders', {
      status: estado ? ESTADO_ORDEN_INV[estado] : '',
      customer_id: clienteId ?? '',
    });
    return r.orders.map(toOrden);
  }

  @Mutation('cancelarOrden')
  async cancelarOrden(@Args('id') id: string) {
    await this.backends.booking.call('CancelOrder', { order_id: id });
    return this.lookups.ordenPorId(id);
  }
}

// ── Entidades federadas (@key): otros subgrafos pueden extenderlas ──────────────
@Resolver('Vehiculo')
export class VehiculoResolver {
  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends, private readonly lookups: Lookups) {}

  @ResolveField('agencia')
  agencia(@Parent() v: any) {
    return this.lookups.agenciaPorId(v._depotId);
  }

  @ResolveField('proveedor')
  async proveedor(@Parent() v: any) {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListSuppliers', { supplier_ids: [v._supplierId] });
    const s = r.suppliers[0];
    return { id: String(v._supplierId), nombre: s?.name ?? `Proveedor ${v._supplierId}` };
  }

  @ResolveReference()
  resolveReference(ref: { __typename: string; id: string }) {
    return this.lookups.vehiculoPorId(ref.id);
  }
}

@Resolver('Agencia')
export class AgenciaResolver {
  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends, private readonly lookups: Lookups) {}

  @ResolveField('vehiculos')
  async vehiculos(@Parent() a: any) {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('GetVehicles', {});
    return r.vehicles.filter((v: any) => String(v.depot_id) === String(a.id)).map(toVehiculo);
  }

  @ResolveReference()
  resolveReference(ref: { __typename: string; id: string }) {
    return this.lookups.agenciaPorId(ref.id);
  }
}

@Resolver('Orden')
export class OrdenResolver {
  constructor(private readonly lookups: Lookups) {}

  @ResolveReference()
  resolveReference(ref: { __typename: string; id: string }) {
    return this.lookups.ordenPorId(ref.id);
  }
}

export const GRAPHQL_PROVIDERS = [Lookups, AutosQueryResolver, VehiculoResolver, AgenciaResolver, OrdenResolver];

================================================================================
===== services/apps/gateway/src/main.ts =====
================================================================================
import { ValidationPipe } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { DocumentBuilder, SwaggerModule } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'fs';
import { load as loadYaml } from 'js-yaml';
import { join } from 'path';
import { NextFunction, Request, Response } from 'express';
import { CORRELATION_HEADER, protoDir, runWithCorrelation, SERVICE_VERSION, StructuredLogger } from '@autos/common';
import { enforceAdmin } from './common/auth';
import { ProblemDetailsFilter } from './common/problem-details.filter';
import { WebhookPayloadDto } from './dto/autos/webhook.dto';
import { GatewayModule } from './gateway.module';

async function bootstrap() {
  const logger = new StructuredLogger('api-gateway');
  const app = await NestFactory.create(GatewayModule, { logger });

  const allowedOrigins = process.env.CORS_ORIGINS?.split(',').map((o) => o.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  app.enableCors({
    origin: allowedOrigins && allowedOrigins.length > 0 ? allowedOrigins : true,
    exposedHeaders: [CORRELATION_HEADER, 'Idempotent-Replayed'],
  });

  // Alias de rutas del contrato v2/v3 (todas apuntan a la misma implementación):
  //   /api/v3/autos/*  →  /api/v2/autos/*        (ruta base canónica del contrato v3)
  //   /autos/*         →  /api/v2/autos/*        (Booking monta {baseUrlRest}/autos/health sin prefijo)
  //   /reservas[/…]    →  /api/v2/autos/reservas (la Guía §4 describe POST /reservas)
  // El error ProblemDetails conserva la URL original en `instance` (p. ej. "/reservas").
  app.use((req: Request, _res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const u = req.url;
    if (u.startsWith('/api/v3/')) req.url = '/api/v2/' + u.slice('/api/v3/'.length);
    else if (u === '/autos' || u.startsWith('/autos/') || u.startsWith('/autos?')) req.url = '/api/v2' + u;
    else if (u === '/reservas' || u.startsWith('/reservas/') || u.startsWith('/reservas?')) req.url = '/api/v2/autos' + u;
    next();
  });

  // /api/v1 (contrato original) y /api/v2 (contrato del agregador) cuelgan de /api; /graphql, /health y /warmup quedan en la raíz.
  app.setGlobalPrefix('api', { exclude: ['graphql', 'health', 'health/deep', 'warmup'] });

  // Correlation ID: se acepta el que envía el cliente o se genera uno; viaja a los microservicios (metadata
  // gRPC) y a los eventos, y se devuelve en la respuesta. Además se registra cada petición en JSON.
  app.use((req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const cid = (req.headers[CORRELATION_HEADER] as string | undefined)?.slice(0, 64) || randomUUID();
    res.setHeader(CORRELATION_HEADER, cid);
    const started = Date.now();
    res.on('finish', () => {
      if (req.path === '/health') return;
      logger.event('info', `${req.method} ${req.originalUrl.split('?')[0]} ${res.statusCode} ${Date.now() - started}ms`, 'http', {
        method: req.method,
        path: req.originalUrl.split('?')[0],
        status: res.statusCode,
        duration_ms: Date.now() - started,
        idempotency_key: req.headers['idempotency-key'],
      });
    });
    runWithCorrelation(cid, () => next());
  });

  app.useGlobalPipes(new ValidationPipe({ whitelist: true, transform: true, forbidNonWhitelisted: true }));
  app.useGlobalFilters(new ProblemDetailsFilter());

  const config = new DocumentBuilder()
    .setTitle('GDS Autos Core API')
    .setDescription(
      'API Gateway del dominio Autos del Booking Prototipo. Fachada REST (contrato v1 en /api/v1; contrato oficial v3 en /api/v3, documentado en /api/docs-v3) sobre los microservicios ' +
        'Catálogo, Reservas, Pagos e Identidad (gRPC). También publica GraphQL federado en /graphql. ' +
        (enforceAdmin() ? 'Las rutas /admin exigen un JWT de ADMIN (POST /admin/auth/login).' : ''),
    )
    .setVersion(SERVICE_VERSION)
    .addBearerAuth()
    .build();

  const document = SwaggerModule.createDocument(app, config, { extraModels: [WebhookPayloadDto] });

  // @nestjs/swagger no tiene decorador para 'callbacks' de OpenAPI: se inyecta para documentar el POST que el
  // servidor hace a la URL del webhook (fiel a autos-openapi.yaml).
  const webhooksPost = document.paths['/api/v1/webhooks']?.post;
  if (webhooksPost) {
    webhooksPost.callbacks = {
      carEvent: {
        '{$request.body#/url}': {
          post: {
            requestBody: { content: { 'application/json': { schema: { $ref: '#/components/schemas/WebhookPayloadDto' } } } },
            responses: { '200': { description: 'Evento recibido exitosamente' } },
          },
        },
      },
    };
  }
  SwaggerModule.setup('api/docs', app, document, { swaggerOptions: { persistAuthorization: true } });

  // Contrato v2 del agregador: se publica el archivo tal cual (única fuente de verdad), sin regenerarlo desde el código.
  for (const [version, route] of [['v3', 'api/docs-v3'], ['v2', 'api/docs-v2']] as const) {
    const file = join(protoDir(), '..', `autos-openapi-${version}.yaml`);
    if (!existsSync(file)) continue;
    SwaggerModule.setup(route, app, loadYaml(readFileSync(file, 'utf8')) as any, {
      customSiteTitle: `Autos API ${version} · contrato oficial`,
      yamlDocumentUrl: `${route}/openapi.yaml`,
      swaggerOptions: { persistAuthorization: true },
    });
  }

  const port = Number(process.env.PORT ?? 3000);
  await app.listen(port, '0.0.0.0');
  logger.log(`api-gateway listo en :${port} · REST /api/v1 · Swagger /api/docs · GraphQL /graphql`, 'bootstrap');
}
bootstrap();

================================================================================
===== services/apps/gateway/src/rest/accounts.controller.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Body, Controller, Get, HttpCode, HttpStatus, Post, Req } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Backends } from '../backends';
import { Roles } from '../common/auth';
import { orderToRaw, profileToRest } from '../common/mappers';
import { AdminLoginDto, AdminLoginResponseDto } from '../dto/admin/admin-login.dto';
import { CustomerAuthResponseDto, CustomerProfileDto } from '../dto/customers/customer-profile.dto';
import { LoginCustomerDto } from '../dto/customers/login.dto';
import { RegisterCustomerDto } from '../dto/customers/register.dto';

/** Cuenta de cliente y login de administrador: fachada sobre el microservicio de Identidad. */
@Controller('v1')
export class AccountsController {
  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends) {}

  @Post('auth/register')
  @ApiTags('Cuenta de cliente')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Registrar una cuenta de cliente' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, type: CustomerAuthResponseDto })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  async register(@Body() dto: RegisterCustomerDto) {
    const r = await this.backends.identity.call<any>('RegisterCustomer', {
      email: dto.email,
      password: dto.password,
      first_name: dto.first_name,
      last_name: dto.last_name,
      phone_number: dto.phone_number ?? '',
    });
    return { token: r.token, profile: profileToRest(r.profile) };
  }

  @Post('auth/login')
  @ApiTags('Cuenta de cliente')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Iniciar sesión como cliente' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, type: CustomerAuthResponseDto })
  async login(@Body() dto: LoginCustomerDto) {
    const r = await this.backends.identity.call<any>('LoginCustomer', { login: dto.email, password: dto.password });
    return { token: r.token, profile: profileToRest(r.profile) };
  }

  @Get('auth/me')
  @Roles('CUSTOMER')
  @ApiTags('Cuenta de cliente')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Obtener el perfil del cliente autenticado (null si no hay sesión)' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, type: CustomerProfileDto })
  async me(@Req() req: any) {
    if (req.principal?.role !== 'CUSTOMER') return null;
    return profileToRest(await this.backends.identity.call('GetCustomer', { id: req.principal.subject }));
  }

  @Get('me/orders')
  @Roles('CUSTOMER')
  @ApiTags('Cuenta de cliente')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Listar las reservas del cliente autenticado ([] si no hay sesión)' })
  async myOrders(@Req() req: any) {
    if (req.principal?.role !== 'CUSTOMER') return [];
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ListOrders', { customer_id: req.principal.subject });
    return r.orders.map(orderToRaw);
  }

  @Post('admin/auth/login')
  @ApiTags('Administración')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Iniciar sesión como administrador' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, type: AdminLoginResponseDto })
  async adminLogin(@Body() dto: AdminLoginDto): Promise<AdminLoginResponseDto> {
    return this.backends.identity.call('LoginAdmin', { login: dto.username, password: dto.password });
  }
}

================================================================================
===== services/apps/gateway/src/rest/admin.controller.ts =====
================================================================================
import {
  Body, Controller, Delete, Get, HttpCode, HttpStatus, Param, ParseEnumPipe, ParseUUIDPipe, Post, Put, Query,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { ApiOperation, ApiParam, ApiQuery, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Backends } from '../backends';
import { Roles } from '../common/auth';
import { depotToAdmin, orderToRaw, presentFields, userToRest, vehicleToAdmin } from '../common/mappers';
import { CreateDepotAdminDto, UpdateDepotAdminDto } from '../dto/admin/depot-admin.dto';
import { CreateUserAdminDto, USER_ROLES, UserAdminResponseDto, type UserRole } from '../dto/admin/user-admin.dto';
import { CreateVehicleAdminDto, UpdateVehicleAdminDto } from '../dto/admin/vehicle-admin.dto';
import { OrderStatus } from '../dto/enums';

const vehicleInput = (d: Partial<CreateVehicleAdminDto>) => ({
  vehicle_id: d.vehicle_id ?? '',
  make: d.make ?? '',
  model: d.model ?? '',
  car_type: d.car_type ?? '',
  transmission: d.transmission ?? '',
  doors: d.doors ?? 0,
  bag_capacity: d.bag_capacity ?? 0,
  seats: d.seats ?? 0,
  price_per_day: d.price_per_day ?? 0,
  supplier_id: d.supplier_id ?? 0,
  depot_id: d.depot_id ?? 0,
  image_url: d.image_url ?? '',
  status: d.status ?? '',
  min_driver_age: d.min_driver_age ?? 0,
});

const depotInput = (d: Partial<CreateDepotAdminDto>) => ({
  depot_id: d.depot_id ?? 0,
  name: d.name ?? '',
  city_id: d.city_id ?? 0,
  airport: d.airport ?? '',
  score: d.score ?? 0,
});

/**
 * Backoffice de administración (no forma parte del contrato público). Fachada sobre Catálogo (autos y
 * agencias), Reservas (órdenes, entregas de webhooks) e Identidad (usuarios). Con AUTH_ENFORCE_ADMIN=true
 * exige un JWT de ADMIN (ver common/auth.ts).
 */
@Controller('v1/admin')
@ApiTags('Administración')
@Roles('ADMIN')
export class AdminController {
  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends) {}

  // ── Vehículos ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  @Get('vehicles')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Listar todos los vehículos del catálogo' })
  async listVehicles() {
    return (await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('AdminListVehicles', {})).vehicles.map(vehicleToAdmin);
  }

  @Post('vehicles')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Registrar un nuevo vehículo en el catálogo' })
  async createVehicle(@Body() dto: CreateVehicleAdminDto) {
    return vehicleToAdmin(await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminCreateVehicle', vehicleInput(dto)));
  }

  @Put('vehicles/:id')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Actualizar un vehículo existente' })
  async updateVehicle(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string, @Body() dto: UpdateVehicleAdminDto) {
    return vehicleToAdmin(
      await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminUpdateVehicle', { id, patch: vehicleInput(dto), fields: presentFields(dto) }),
    );
  }

  @Post('vehicles/:id/release')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Marcar un vehículo como DISPONIBLE (libera una reserva)' })
  async releaseVehicle(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string) {
    return vehicleToAdmin(await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminSetVehicleStatus', { id, status: 'AVAILABLE' }));
  }

  @Post('vehicles/:id/reserve')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Marcar un vehículo como RESERVADO (bloquea nuevas reservas)' })
  async reserveVehicle(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string) {
    return vehicleToAdmin(await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminSetVehicleStatus', { id, status: 'RESERVED' }));
  }

  @Delete('vehicles/:id')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Eliminar un vehículo del catálogo' })
  async deleteVehicle(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string) {
    await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminDeleteVehicle', { id });
  }

  // ── Agencias ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  @Get('depots')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Listar todas las agencias (depots)' })
  async listDepots() {
    return (await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('AdminListDepots', {})).depots.map(depotToAdmin);
  }

  @Post('depots')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Registrar una nueva agencia' })
  async createDepot(@Body() dto: CreateDepotAdminDto) {
    return depotToAdmin(await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminCreateDepot', depotInput(dto)));
  }

  @Put('depots/:id')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Actualizar una agencia' })
  async updateDepot(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string, @Body() dto: UpdateDepotAdminDto) {
    return depotToAdmin(
      await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminUpdateDepot', { id, patch: depotInput(dto), fields: presentFields(dto) }),
    );
  }

  @Delete('depots/:id')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Eliminar una agencia' })
  async deleteDepot(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string) {
    await this.backends.catalog.call('AdminDeleteDepot', { id });
  }

  // ── Órdenes (solo lectura) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  @Get('orders')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Listar todas las órdenes (filtro opcional por estado)' })
  @ApiQuery({ name: 'status', enum: OrderStatus, required: false })
  async listOrders(@Query('status') status?: OrderStatus) {
    return (await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ListOrders', { status: status ?? '' })).orders.map(orderToRaw);
  }

  @Get('orders/:id')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Detalle administrativo de una orden' })
  async getOrder(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string) {
    return orderToRaw(await this.backends.booking.call('GetOrder', { order_id: id }));
  }

  // ── Stock hacia Booking Prototipo (gRPC) ─────────────────────────────────
  @Post('inventory/sync')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Sincronizar todo el stock de la flota con Booking Prototipo',
    description:
      'Envía el estado de cada auto por gRPC (inventory.InventoryStockService/StreamInventoryChanges). Requiere INVENTORY_GRPC_URL; sin ella responde enabled=false.',
  })
  syncInventory() {
    return this.backends.booking.call('SyncInventory', {});
  }

  // ── Observabilidad de eventos ────────────────────────────────────────────
  @Get('webhook-deliveries')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Entregas de webhooks (DELIVERED y FAILED tras agotar los reintentos)' })
  async deliveries() {
    return (await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ListWebhookDeliveries', {})).deliveries;
  }

  // ── Usuarios (administradores y clientes) ────────────────────────────────
  @Get('users')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Listar usuarios (administradores y clientes)' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, type: [UserAdminResponseDto] })
  async listUsers() {
    return (await this.backends.identity.call<any>('ListUsers', {})).users.map(userToRest);
  }

  @Post('users')
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Crear un usuario administrador o cliente',
    description:
      'role=ADMIN requiere username + password. role=CUSTOMER requiere email, password, first_name y last_name. La cuenta creada puede iniciar sesión de inmediato.',
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, type: UserAdminResponseDto })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Username o correo ya registrado' })
  async createUser(@Body() dto: CreateUserAdminDto) {
    return userToRest(
      await this.backends.identity.call('CreateUser', {
        role: dto.role,
        username: dto.username ?? '',
        email: dto.email ?? '',
        password: dto.password,
        first_name: dto.first_name ?? '',
        last_name: dto.last_name ?? '',
        phone_number: dto.phone_number ?? '',
      }),
    );
  }

  @Delete('users/:role/:id')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Eliminar un usuario' })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'role', enum: USER_ROLES })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'id', format: 'uuid' })
  async deleteUser(
    @Param('role', new ParseEnumPipe(USER_ROLES)) role: UserRole,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string,
  ) {
    await this.backends.identity.call('DeleteUser', { role, id });
  }
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/rest/autos.controller.ts =====
