import {
  Body, Controller, Delete, Get, Header, Headers, HttpCode, HttpStatus, Param, ParseUUIDPipe, Post, Req, UseGuards, UseInterceptors,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { ApiHeader, ApiOperation, ApiParam, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
import { Backends } from '../backends';
import { IdempotencyInterceptor, IdempotencyKeyGuard } from '../common/idempotency';
import { orderToDetail, vehicleToDetails, webhookToRest } from '../common/mappers';
import { CarConstantsRequestDto, CarConstantsResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/constants.dto';
import { DepotScoresRequestDto, DepotScoresResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/depot-scores.dto';
import { DepotsRequestDto, DepotsResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/depots.dto';
import { CarDetailsRequestDto, CarDetailsResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/details.dto';
import { OrderCreateRequestDto } from '../dto/autos/order-create.dto';
import { OrderDetailDto } from '../dto/autos/order-detail.dto';
import { OrderHoldRequestDto, OrderHoldResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/order-hold.dto';
import { OrderModifyRequestDto } from '../dto/autos/order-modify.dto';
import { OrderPreviewRequestDto, OrderPreviewResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/order-preview.dto';
import { PaymentRequestDto, PaymentResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/payment.dto';
import { CarSearchRequestDto, CarSearchResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/search.dto';
import { SuppliersRequestDto, SuppliersResponseDto } from '../dto/autos/suppliers.dto';
import { WebhookSubscriptionDto } from '../dto/autos/webhook.dto';

/**
 * Contrato REST público de Autos (autos-openapi.yaml, ruta /api/v1). Es una fachada: valida la petición y
 * la reparte por gRPC a Catálogo (autos, agencias, proveedores) o Reservas (búsqueda, órdenes, pagos, webhooks).
 */
@Controller('v1')
export class AutosController {
  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends) {}

  // ══ Búsqueda y Catálogo ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Post('search')
  @ApiTags('Búsqueda y Catálogo')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Búsqueda de renta de vehículos' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'X-Affiliate-Id', required: true })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Vehículos encontrados', type: CarSearchResponseDto })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Petición inválida' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @Header('Cache-Control', 'no-store')
  async search(@Headers('X-Affiliate-Id') _affiliateId: string, @Body() dto: CarSearchRequestDto): Promise<CarSearchResponseDto> {
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('Search', {
      booker_country: dto.booker.country,
      currency: dto.currency,
      driver: dto.driver,
      route: dto.route,
      filters: dto.filters ?? {},
      maximum_results: dto.maximum_results ?? 100,
    });
    return {
      request_id: r.request_id,
      data: r.data,
      metadata: { total_results: r.total_results, next_page: null },
      search_token: r.search_token,
    };
  }

  @Post('details')
  @ApiTags('Búsqueda y Catálogo')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Obtener especificaciones y características de los vehículos' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'X-Affiliate-Id', required: true })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Detalles de los vehículos solicitados', type: CarDetailsResponseDto })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @Header('Cache-Control', 'no-store')
  async details(@Headers('X-Affiliate-Id') _a: string, @Body() _dto: CarDetailsRequestDto): Promise<CarDetailsResponseDto> {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('GetVehicles', {});
    return { request_id: randomUUID(), data: r.vehicles.map(vehicleToDetails) };
  }

  // ══ Agencias y Proveedores ════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Post('depots')
  @ApiTags('Información de Agencias y Proveedores')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Consultar lista de agencias de renta (puntos de recogida y entrega)' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'X-Affiliate-Id', required: true })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Lista de agencias (depots)', type: DepotsResponseDto })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @Header('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=3600')
  async depots(@Headers('X-Affiliate-Id') _a: string, @Body() _dto: DepotsRequestDto): Promise<DepotsResponseDto> {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListDepots', {});
    return {
      request_id: randomUUID(),
      data: r.depots.map((d: any) => ({
        depot_id: d.depot_id,
        name: d.name,
        location: { city_id: d.city_id, ...(d.airport ? { airport: d.airport } : {}) },
      })),
      metadata: { total_results: r.depots.length },
    };
  }

  @Post('depots/reviews/scores')
  @ApiTags('Información de Agencias y Proveedores')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Obtener puntuaciones y reseñas de las agencias' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'X-Affiliate-Id', required: true })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Puntuaciones desglosadas por agencia', type: DepotScoresResponseDto })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @Header('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=600')
  async depotScores(@Headers('X-Affiliate-Id') _a: string, @Body() _dto: DepotScoresRequestDto): Promise<DepotScoresResponseDto> {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListDepots', {});
    return {
      request_id: randomUUID(),
      data: r.depots.map((d: any) => ({ depot_id: d.depot_id, score: d.score })),
      metadata: { total_results: r.depots.length },
    };
  }

  @Post('suppliers')
  @ApiTags('Información de Agencias y Proveedores')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Listar proveedores de renta de autos' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'X-Affiliate-Id', required: true })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Lista de proveedores de renta de autos', type: SuppliersResponseDto })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @Header('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=3600')
  async suppliers(@Headers('X-Affiliate-Id') _a: string, @Body() dto: SuppliersRequestDto): Promise<SuppliersResponseDto> {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListSuppliers', { supplier_ids: dto.suppliers ?? [] });
    return { request_id: randomUUID(), data: r.suppliers };
  }

  // ══ Componentes comunes ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Post('constants')
  @ApiTags('Componentes Comunes')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Consultar constantes del sistema (políticas, seguros, servicios extra)' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'X-Affiliate-Id', required: true })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Constantes del sistema', type: CarConstantsResponseDto })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @Header('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=86400')
  async constants(@Headers('X-Affiliate-Id') _a: string, @Body() dto: CarConstantsRequestDto): Promise<CarConstantsResponseDto> {
    const r = await this.backends.catalog.call<any>('GetConstants', { constants: dto.constants ?? [] });
    return { request_id: randomUUID(), data: JSON.parse(r.data_json) };
  }

  // ══ Órdenes (reservas) ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Post('orders/hold')
  @ApiTags('Gestión de Órdenes (Reservas)')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Bloquear temporalmente el vehículo y precio (Hold)' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Vehículo bloqueado exitosamente', type: OrderHoldResponseDto })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Conflicto (auto no disponible o edad no permitida)' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  hold(@Body() dto: OrderHoldRequestDto): Promise<OrderHoldResponseDto> {
    return this.backends.booking.call('Hold', { vehicle_id: dto.vehicle_id, search_token: dto.search_token });
  }

  @Post('orders/preview')
  @ApiTags('Gestión de Órdenes (Reservas)')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Previsualizar la orden de renta antes de confirmar' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Detalles de la orden previsualizada y precios finales', type: OrderPreviewResponseDto })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  async preview(@Body() dto: OrderPreviewRequestDto): Promise<OrderPreviewResponseDto> {
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('Preview', {
      vehicle_id: dto.vehicle_id,
      search_token: dto.search_token,
      hold_id: dto.hold_id ?? '',
      extras: dto.extras ?? [],
    });
    return {
      request_id: r.request_id,
      data: {
        order_preview_id: r.order_preview_id,
        total_price: r.total_price,
        currency: r.currency,
        breakdown: r.breakdown,
      },
    };
  }

  @Post('payments')
  @ApiTags('Pagos (simulado)')
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Procesar un pago simulado (obligatorio antes de crear la orden)',
    description:
      'Simula una pasarela de pago. El monto se toma del order_preview_id. Si queda APPROVED, usa payment_reference en /orders/create. Tarjetas de prueba: 4242424242424242 aprobada; 4000000000000002 rechazada (fondos insuficientes).',
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: 'Pago aprobado', type: PaymentResponseDto })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 402, description: 'Pago rechazado' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Vehículo ya reservado o edad no permitida' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  pay(@Body() dto: PaymentRequestDto): Promise<PaymentResponseDto> {
    return this.backends.booking.call('Pay', { order_preview_id: dto.order_preview_id, card: dto.card });
  }

  @Post('orders/create')
  @ApiTags('Gestión de Órdenes (Reservas)')
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Crear orden/reserva de renta de vehículo',
    description:
      'Opcionalmente, envía un token de cliente válido (POST /auth/register o POST /auth/login) para ligar la reserva a tu cuenta. También se puede reservar sin cuenta. Además exige un pago aprobado (POST /payments).',
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 402, description: 'Pago requerido o no aprobado' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'Idempotency-Key', required: true, description: 'UUID v4: repetir la misma clave devuelve la misma respuesta sin duplicar la reserva' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: 'Orden creada exitosamente', type: OrderDetailDto })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Petición inválida' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Conflicto (auto no disponible)' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  @UseGuards(IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async createOrder(@Req() req: any, @Body() dto: OrderCreateRequestDto): Promise<OrderDetailDto> {
    // Si llega un JWT de cliente, la orden queda ligada a esa cuenta; si no, es reserva de invitado.
    const customerId = req.principal?.role === 'CUSTOMER' ? req.principal.subject : '';
    const o = await this.backends.booking.call('CreateOrder', {
      order_preview_id: dto.order_preview_id,
      payment_reference: dto.payment_reference,
      driver_details: dto.driver_details,
      customer_id: customerId,
    });
    return orderToDetail(o) as unknown as OrderDetailDto;
  }

  @Get('orders/:orderId')
  @ApiTags('Gestión de Órdenes (Reservas)')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Obtener detalles de la orden' })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'orderId', type: 'string', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Detalles completos de la orden', type: OrderDetailDto })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Orden no encontrada' })
  async getOrder(@Param('orderId', ParseUUIDPipe) orderId: string): Promise<OrderDetailDto> {
    return orderToDetail(await this.backends.booking.call('GetOrder', { order_id: orderId })) as unknown as OrderDetailDto;
  }

  @Post('orders/:orderId/modify')
  @ApiTags('Gestión de Órdenes (Reservas)')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Modificar una orden existente' })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'orderId', type: 'string', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'Idempotency-Key', required: true, description: 'UUID v4 para evitar modificaciones duplicadas' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Orden modificada', type: OrderDetailDto })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Conflicto' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async modifyOrder(@Param('orderId', ParseUUIDPipe) orderId: string, @Body() dto: OrderModifyRequestDto): Promise<OrderDetailDto> {
    const o = await this.backends.booking.call('ModifyOrder', {
      order_id: orderId,
      extras_to_add: dto.extras_to_add ?? [],
      extras_to_remove: dto.extras_to_remove ?? [],
      route: dto.route ?? null,
    });
    return orderToDetail(o) as unknown as OrderDetailDto;
  }

  @Post('orders/:orderId/cancel')
  @ApiTags('Gestión de Órdenes (Reservas)')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Cancelar una orden de renta' })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'orderId', type: 'string', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiHeader({ name: 'Idempotency-Key', required: true, description: 'UUID v4 para evitar cancelaciones duplicadas' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Cancelación procesada' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Conflicto' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async cancelOrder(@Param('orderId', ParseUUIDPipe) orderId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.backends.booking.call('CancelOrder', { order_id: orderId });
  }

  // ══ Webhooks ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Get('webhooks')
  @ApiTags('Webhooks')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Listar suscripciones a eventos' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Suscripciones activas' })
  async listWebhooks() {
    return (await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ListWebhooks', {})).webhooks.map(webhookToRest);
  }

  @Post('webhooks')
  @ApiTags('Webhooks')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Registrar un nuevo webhook' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: 'Webhook registrado' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  async createWebhook(@Body() dto: WebhookSubscriptionDto) {
    return webhookToRest(
      await this.backends.booking.call('CreateWebhook', { url: dto.url, events: dto.events, secret: dto.secret ?? '' }),
    );
  }

  @Delete('webhooks/:id')
  @ApiTags('Webhooks')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Eliminar suscripción de webhook' })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'id', type: 'string', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 204, description: 'Suscripción eliminada' })
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
  async deleteWebhook(@Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) id: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.backends.booking.call('DeleteWebhook', { id });
  }
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/rest/health.controller.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Controller, Get } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ApiExcludeController } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { SERVICE_VERSION } from '@autos/common';
import { Backends } from '../backends';

/**
 * /health: el gateway está vivo. /health/deep (alias /warmup): consulta a los cuatro microservicios.
 * En un plan gratis donde cada servicio se duerme por separado, llamar a /warmup antes de una demo
 * despierta toda la cadena y confirma que cada pieza responde.
 */
@Controller()
@ApiExcludeController()
export class HealthController {
  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends) {}

  @Get('health')
  health() {
    return { status: 'ok', service: 'api-gateway', version: SERVICE_VERSION, uptime_s: Math.round(process.uptime()) };
  }

  @Get(['health/deep', 'warmup'])
  async deep() {
    const probe = async (name: string, fn: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
      const started = Date.now();
      try {
        await fn();
        return { service: name, status: 'up', ms: Date.now() - started };
      } catch (err: any) {
        // Un error de dominio (404/401…) significa que el servicio respondió: está vivo.
        const code = err?.getResponse?.()?.code;
        const down = code === 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE' || code === 'UPSTREAM_ERROR';
        return { service: name, status: down ? 'down' : 'up', ms: Date.now() - started, ...(down ? { error: code } : {}) };
      }
    };
    const services = await Promise.all([
      probe('catalog-service', () => this.backends.catalog.call('ListSuppliers', {})),
      probe('booking-service', () => this.backends.booking.call('ListWebhooks', {})),
      probe('payments-service', () => this.backends.payments.call('GetPayment', { payment_reference: 'health-check' })),
      probe('identity-service', () => this.backends.identity.call('VerifyToken', { token: 'health-check' })),
    ]);
    return { status: services.every((s) => s.status === 'up') ? 'ok' : 'degraded', service: 'api-gateway', services };
  }
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/v2/autos-v2.controller.ts =====
================================================================================
import {
  Body, Controller, Get, Headers, HttpCode, HttpException, HttpStatus, Param, Post, Put, Query, Req, Res, UseGuards, UseInterceptors,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { ApiExcludeController } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import {
  driverUnderage, InvalidParam, invalidParams, MIN_LEGAL_AGE, needsAgeSurcharge, normalizeCoverage, notFound, rentalDays, SURCHARGE_PER_DAY,
} from '@autos/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
import { Backends } from '../backends';
import { V2AccessGuard, V2Roles } from '../common/auth';
import { IdempotencyInterceptor, IdempotencyKeyGuard } from '../common/idempotency';
import {
  CATEGORIES, CatalogData, checkTrip, coverageOptions, cooldownResponse, fleetItem, isDate, isEmail, isInt, isText, locationMatches,
  orderToV2, promoToV2, resolveLocation, throwIfBadDates, throwIfInvalid, TRANSMISSIONS, transmissionOf, categoryOf, vehicleCard, ESTADO,
} from './support';

const VERSION = '3.0.0';
const COVERAGE_INPUT = ['BASIC', 'BASICA', 'TOTAL', 'PREMIUM'];

/**
 * Contrato REST v2 del agregador Booking Prototipo (contracts/autos-openapi-v2.yaml, ruta /api/v2).
 * Es una fachada sobre los microservicios por gRPC. El contrato se publica tal cual en /api/docs/v2; este
 * controlador no se documenta con decoradores propios para que la única fuente de verdad sea el archivo.
 */
@Controller('v2/autos')
@ApiExcludeController()
export class AutosV2Controller {
  private snapshot?: CatalogData;

  constructor(private readonly backends: Backends) {}

  // ══ Lectura del catálogo con tolerancia a fallos (último dato válido como respaldo) ════════════
  /**
   * Catálogo completo (autos, agencias, proveedores). Si Catálogo no responde (timeout o Circuit Breaker
   * abierto) se sirve la última copia válida y se avisa con la cabecera X-Data-Source: fallback-cache.
   */
  private async catalogData(res?: any): Promise<CatalogData> {
    try {
      const [v, d, s] = await Promise.all([
        this.backends.catalog.call<any>('GetVehicles', {}),
        this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListDepots', {}),
        this.backends.catalog.call<any>('ListSuppliers', {}),
      ]);
      this.snapshot = { vehicles: v.vehicles, depots: d.depots, suppliers: s.suppliers };
      return this.snapshot;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof HttpException && err.getStatus() >= 502 && this.snapshot) {
        res?.setHeader('X-Data-Source', 'fallback-cache');
        return this.snapshot;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  private vehicleOr404(cat: CatalogData, id: string) {
    const v = cat.vehicles.find((x) => x.vehicle_id === id || x.id === id);
    if (!v) throw notFound('Vehículo no encontrado', `Vehículo "${id}" no encontrado en la flota.`);
    return v;
  }

  private base(req: any) {
    return `${req.protocol}://${req.get('host')}/api/v2`;
  }

  // ══ Telemetría ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Get('health')
  async health() {
    const probe = async (fn: () => Promise<any>) => {
      const started = Date.now();
      try {
        return { up: true, ms: Date.now() - started, value: await fn() };
      } catch (err: any) {
        // Un error de dominio (404/401…) significa que el servicio respondió: está vivo.
        return { up: !(err instanceof HttpException) || err.getStatus() < 502, ms: Date.now() - started, value: null };
      }
    };
    const [catalog, booking, payments, identity] = await Promise.all([
      probe(() => this.backends.catalog.call('GetVehicles', {})),
      probe(() => this.backends.booking.call('ListWebhooks', {})),
      probe(() => this.backends.payments.call('GetPayment', { payment_reference: 'health-check' })),
      probe(() => this.backends.identity.call('VerifyToken', { token: 'health-check' })),
    ]);
    const states = [this.backends.catalog, this.backends.booking, this.backends.payments, this.backends.identity].map((c) => c.breakerState);
    const circuit = states.includes('OPEN') ? 'OPEN' : states.includes('HALF_OPEN') ? 'HALF_OPEN' : 'CLOSED';
    const core = catalog.up && booking.up;
    const status = !core ? 'DOWN' : payments.up && identity.up ? 'HEALTHY' : 'DEGRADED';

    if (status === 'DOWN') {
      throw new HttpException(
        {
          type: 'https://booking.epn.edu.ec/errors/service-unavailable',
          title: 'Nodo de autos no disponible',
          status: HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
          detail: `Agencia de alquiler no disponible (Circuit Breaker ${circuit === 'OPEN' ? 'abierto' : 'en recuperación'}): ${!catalog.up ? 'catálogo' : 'reservas'} no responde.`,
          code: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE',
        },
        HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
      );
    }
    const vehicles: any[] = catalog.value?.vehicles ?? [];
    return {
      status,
      nodo_id: process.env.NODE_ID ?? 'NODE_AUT_LOCAL',
      nombre_proveedor: process.env.NODE_NAME ?? 'RodadoEc (Flota Partner)',
      vertical: 'AUTOS',
      version: VERSION,
      uptime_seconds: Math.round(process.uptime()),
      latencia_ms: Math.max(catalog.ms, booking.ms, payments.ms, identity.ms),
      circuit_breaker_estado: circuit,
      cooldown_activo_count: vehicles.filter((v) => v.status === 'CLEANING').length,
      total_vehiculos: vehicles.length,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
  }

  @Get('eventos')
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard)
  async eventos(@Query('limit') limitRaw?: string, @Query('after') after?: string) {
    const limit = limitRaw === undefined ? 10 : Number(limitRaw);
    if (!isInt(limit, 1, 100)) {
      throw invalidParams('Parámetros de consulta inválidos.', [{ name: 'limit', reason: 'Debe ser un entero entre 1 y 100.' }]);
    }
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ListEvents', { limit, after: after ?? '' });
    return {
      events: r.events.map((e: any) => ({
        specversion: e.specversion,
        type: e.type,
        source: e.source,
        id: e.id,
        time: e.time,
        correlation_id: e.correlation_id,
        datacontenttype: e.datacontenttype,
        data: JSON.parse(e.data_json),
      })),
      metadata: { total: r.total, timestamp: r.timestamp },
    };
  }

  // ══ Promociones B2B ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Get('promociones/contrato')
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard)
  async getPromo() {
    return promoToV2(await this.backends.booking.call('GetPromoContract', {}));
  }

  @Put('promociones/contrato')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async putPromo(@Body() b: any) {
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (typeof b?.permite_promociones !== 'boolean') bad.push({ name: 'permite_promociones', reason: 'Es obligatorio y debe ser booleano.' });
    const pct = b?.porcentaje_descuento_maximo;
    if (typeof pct !== 'number' || pct < 0 || pct > 100) bad.push({ name: 'porcentaje_descuento_maximo', reason: 'Es obligatorio y debe ser un número entre 0 y 100.' });
    if (b?.destinos_habilitados !== undefined && !(Array.isArray(b.destinos_habilitados) && b.destinos_habilitados.every(isText))) {
      bad.push({ name: 'destinos_habilitados', reason: 'Debe ser un arreglo de nombres de ciudad.' });
    }
    if (b?.vigencia_hasta !== undefined && !isDate(b.vigencia_hasta)) bad.push({ name: 'vigencia_hasta', reason: 'Debe ser una fecha YYYY-MM-DD.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);
    const saved = await this.backends.booking.call('SetPromoContract', {
      allows_promotions: b.permite_promociones,
      max_discount_percentage: b.porcentaje_descuento_maximo,
      enabled_destinations: b.destinos_habilitados ?? [],
      valid_until: b.vigencia_hasta ?? '',
      combo_conditions: b.condiciones_combo ?? '',
    });
    return {
      success: true,
      mensaje: 'Acuerdo B2B de promociones y combos actualizado exitosamente.',
      contrato: promoToV2(saved),
    };
  }

  // ══ Catálogo y búsqueda ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Get()
  async list(@Res({ passthrough: true }) res: any, @Query('category') category = 'ALL', @Query('transmission') transmission = 'ALL', @Query('limit') limitRaw?: string) {
    const limit = limitRaw === undefined ? 10 : Number(limitRaw);
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (category !== 'ALL' && !(CATEGORIES as readonly string[]).includes(category)) bad.push({ name: 'category', reason: `Valores admitidos: ALL, ${CATEGORIES.join(', ')}.` });
    if (transmission !== 'ALL' && !(TRANSMISSIONS as readonly string[]).includes(transmission)) bad.push({ name: 'transmission', reason: 'Valores admitidos: ALL, MANUAL, AUTOMATICO.' });
    if (!isInt(limit, 1, 500)) bad.push({ name: 'limit', reason: 'Debe ser un entero positivo.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);

    const cat = await this.catalogData(res);
    const matches = cat.vehicles.filter(
      (v) => v.status === 'AVAILABLE' && (category === 'ALL' || categoryOf(v) === category) && (transmission === 'ALL' || transmissionOf(v) === transmission),
    );
    return { total: matches.length, limit, data: matches.slice(0, limit).map((v) => vehicleCard(v, cat)) };
  }

  @Post('search')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  async search(@Body() b: any, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: any) {
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (!isText(b?.pickup_location)) bad.push({ name: 'pickup_location', reason: 'Es obligatorio (ciudad o código IATA).' });
    const dateIssues = checkTrip({ pickupDate: b?.pickup_date, pickupTime: b?.pickup_time, dropoffDate: b?.dropoff_date, dropoffTime: b?.dropoff_time }, bad, true);
    if (b?.driver_age !== undefined && !isInt(b.driver_age, 0, 120)) bad.push({ name: 'driver_age', reason: 'Debe ser un entero entre 0 y 120.' });
    const category = b?.category ?? 'ALL';
    const transmission = b?.transmission ?? 'ALL';
    if (category !== 'ALL' && !(CATEGORIES as readonly string[]).includes(category)) bad.push({ name: 'category', reason: `Valores admitidos: ALL, ${CATEGORIES.join(', ')}.` });
    if (transmission !== 'ALL' && !(TRANSMISSIONS as readonly string[]).includes(transmission)) bad.push({ name: 'transmission', reason: 'Valores admitidos: ALL, MANUAL, AUTOMATICO.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);
    throwIfBadDates(dateIssues);

    const age: number = b.driver_age ?? 30;
    if (age < MIN_LEGAL_AGE) throw driverUnderage(age);

    const cat = await this.catalogData(res);
    const loc = resolveLocation(b.pickup_location, cat.depots);
    if (!loc) {
      throw invalidParams('La ubicación de recogida no pertenece a nuestra red.', [
        { name: 'pickup_location', reason: 'Ubicación no reconocida. Usa una ciudad (Quito, Guayaquil, Cuenca) o un código IATA (UIO, GYE, CUE).' },
      ]);
    }
    const days = rentalDays(b.pickup_date, b.dropoff_date);
    // Excluye los autos que no se pueden rentar: RESERVADO, ENTREGADO, EN_MANTENIMIENTO y EN_LIMPIEZA (cooldown de 2 h).
    const found = cat.vehicles.filter(
      (v) =>
        v.status === 'AVAILABLE' &&
        v.min_driver_age <= age &&
        locationMatches(v, loc, cat.depots) &&
        (category === 'ALL' || categoryOf(v) === category) &&
        (transmission === 'ALL' || transmissionOf(v) === transmission),
    );
    const surcharge = needsAgeSurcharge(age);
    return {
      request_id: `req-autos-${randomUUID().slice(0, 6)}`,
      metadata: {
        total_results: found.length,
        surcharge_applied: surcharge,
        surcharge_amount: surcharge ? SURCHARGE_PER_DAY * days : 0,
        pickup_location: loc.airport ?? b.pickup_location,
        pickup_date: b.pickup_date,
        dropoff_date: b.dropoff_date,
      },
      data: found.map((v) => vehicleCard(v, cat, { days, driverAge: age })),
    };
  }

  @Get('flota')
  async flota(@Res({ passthrough: true }) res: any) {
    const cat = await this.catalogData(res);
    return cat.vehicles.map(fleetItem);
  }

  // ══ Reservas ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  @Get('orders')
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard)
  async listOrders(@Req() req: any) {
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ListOrders', {});
    return r.orders.map((o: any) => orderToV2(this.shape(o), this.base(req)));
  }

  @Post('orders/create')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  createOrder(@Req() req: any, @Body() b: any, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: any) {
    return this.create(req, b, res);
  }

  /** Alias de compatibilidad de /autos/orders/create. */
  @Post('reservas')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  createReserva(@Req() req: any, @Body() b: any, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: any) {
    return this.create(req, b, res);
  }

  private async create(req: any, b: any, res: any) {
    // Contrato v3: `driver` en inglés es el canónico; `conductor` y los nombres en español se aceptan como alias.
    const raw = b?.driver ?? b?.conductor;
    const c = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {};
    const norm = {
      vehicle_id: b?.vehicle_id ?? b?.auto_id,
      pickup_date: b?.pickup_date ?? b?.fecha_recogida,
      dropoff_date: b?.dropoff_date ?? b?.fecha_devolucion,
      pickup_time: b?.pickup_time ?? b?.hora_recogida,
      dropoff_time: b?.dropoff_time ?? b?.hora_devolucion,
      pickup_location: b?.pickup_location ?? b?.lugar_recogida,
      dropoff_location: b?.dropoff_location ?? b?.lugar_devolucion,
      coverage: b?.coverage_type ?? b?.tipo_cobertura ?? b?.cobertura,
      name: c.full_name ?? c.nombre ?? c.nombre_completo,
      email: c.email ?? c.correo,
      phone: c.phone ?? c.telefono,
      license: c.license_number ?? c.licencia_conducir,
      license_country: c.country_license ?? c.pais_licencia,
      age: c.age ?? c.edad ?? c.edad_conductor ?? c.driver_age,
      flight: c.flight_number ?? c.numero_vuelo,
    };
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (!isText(norm.vehicle_id)) bad.push({ name: 'vehicle_id', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
    const dateIssues = checkTrip({ pickupDate: norm.pickup_date, pickupTime: norm.pickup_time, dropoffDate: norm.dropoff_date, dropoffTime: norm.dropoff_time }, bad, false);
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') bad.push({ name: 'driver', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
    else {
      if (!isText(norm.name)) bad.push({ name: 'driver.full_name', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
      if (!isEmail(norm.email)) bad.push({ name: 'driver.email', reason: 'Es obligatorio y debe ser un correo válido.' });
      if (!isText(norm.phone)) bad.push({ name: 'driver.phone', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
      if (!isText(norm.license)) bad.push({ name: 'driver.license_number', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
      // age es obligatoria en el contrato v3; ser menor de 18 es un error semántico (422), no de formato.
      if (!isInt(norm.age, 0, 120)) bad.push({ name: 'driver.age', reason: 'Es obligatoria y debe ser un entero entre 0 y 120.' });
    }
    if (norm.coverage !== undefined && !COVERAGE_INPUT.includes(norm.coverage)) bad.push({ name: 'coverage_type', reason: 'Valores admitidos: BASIC, BASICA, TOTAL.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);
    throwIfBadDates(dateIssues);

    // Las ubicaciones son opcionales y de texto libre: si se reconocen se guardan resueltas.
    const depots = norm.pickup_location || norm.dropoff_location ? (await this.catalogData(res)).depots : [];
    const loc = (t?: string) => (t ? resolveLocation(t, depots) ?? {} : {});
    const customerId = req.principal?.role === 'CUSTOMER' ? req.principal.subject : '';

    const order = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('CreateOrderV2', {
      vehicle_id: norm.vehicle_id,
      pickup_date: norm.pickup_date,
      pickup_time: norm.pickup_time ?? '',
      dropoff_date: norm.dropoff_date,
      dropoff_time: norm.dropoff_time ?? '',
      pickup_location: loc(norm.pickup_location),
      dropoff_location: loc(norm.dropoff_location ?? norm.pickup_location),
      coverage_type: normalizeCoverage(norm.coverage),
      driver: {
        full_name: norm.name,
        email: norm.email,
        phone: norm.phone,
        license: norm.license,
        license_country: norm.license_country ?? '',
        age: norm.age ?? 0,
        flight_number: norm.flight ?? '',
      },
      customer_id: customerId,
    });
    return orderToV2(this.shape(order), this.base(req));
  }

  @Post('reservas/:id/checkin')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async checkin(@Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: any) {
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (!isText(b?.documento_identidad)) bad.push({ name: 'documento_identidad', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
    if (b?.fecha_vencimiento_licencia !== undefined && !isDate(b.fecha_vencimiento_licencia)) bad.push({ name: 'fecha_vencimiento_licencia', reason: 'Debe ser una fecha YYYY-MM-DD.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('CheckinDigital', {
      order_id: id,
      document_id: b.documento_identidad,
      license_number: b.numero_licencia ?? '',
      license_expiry: b.fecha_vencimiento_licencia ?? '',
    });
    return {
      reserva_id: r.pnr,
      pnr: r.pnr,
      checkin_status: r.checkin_status,
      fast_track_pickup: r.fast_track_pickup,
      mensaje: 'Check-in digital exitoso. Presenta tu código QR en el mostrador para retiro express.',
      voucher_qr_data: r.voucher_qr_data,
      requisitos: ['Licencia de conducir vigente (original)', 'Documento de identidad', 'Código QR del voucher digital'],
    };
  }

  @Post('orders/:id/checkin-entrega')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async checkinEntrega(@Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: any) {
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (!isText(b?.licencia_conducir)) bad.push({ name: 'licencia_conducir', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
    if (!isInt(b?.km_salida, 0)) bad.push({ name: 'km_salida', reason: 'Es obligatorio y debe ser un entero mayor o igual a 0.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);
    const o = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('DeliverOrder', {
      order_id: id,
      license: b.licencia_conducir,
      km_salida: b.km_salida,
      technician: b.tecnico_entrega ?? '',
    });
    return {
      success: true,
      order_id: o.locator,
      pnr: o.locator,
      estado_orden: o.status,
      km_salida: o.km_salida,
      licencia_verificada: b.licencia_conducir,
      mensaje: 'Llaves entregadas al conductor. Auto en tránsito (Fase ENTREGADO).',
    };
  }

  @Post('orders/:id/checkin-devolucion')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async checkinDevolucion(@Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: any) {
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (!isInt(b?.km_retorno, 0)) bad.push({ name: 'km_retorno', reason: 'Es obligatorio y debe ser un entero mayor o igual a 0.' });
    if (b?.nivel_combustible !== undefined && !isInt(b.nivel_combustible, 0, 100)) bad.push({ name: 'nivel_combustible', reason: 'Debe ser un porcentaje entero de 0 a 100.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ReturnOrder', {
      order_id: id,
      km_retorno: b.km_retorno,
      fuel_level: b.nivel_combustible ?? 0,
      notes: b.observaciones ?? '',
    });
    return {
      success: true,
      order_id: r.order.locator,
      pnr: r.order.locator,
      estado_orden: r.order.status,
      km_retorno: r.order.km_retorno,
      km_recorridos: r.km_driven,
      cooldown: cooldownResponse(r),
      alerta_mantenimiento: r.maintenance_alert || 'Kilometraje en rango normal.',
    };
  }

  @Get('orders/:orderId')
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard)
  async getOrder(@Req() req: any, @Param('orderId') orderId: string) {
    return orderToV2(this.shape(await this.backends.booking.call('GetOrder', { order_id: orderId })), this.base(req));
  }

  @Post('orders/:orderId/cancel')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async cancel(@Param('orderId') orderId: string, @Body() b: any) {
    if (b?.solicitante !== undefined && !['CLIENTE', 'SAGA_ORCHESTRATOR', 'ADMIN'].includes(b.solicitante)) {
      throw invalidParams('Parámetros inválidos.', [{ name: 'solicitante', reason: 'Valores admitidos: CLIENTE, SAGA_ORCHESTRATOR, ADMIN.' }]);
    }
    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('CancelOrderV2', { order_id: orderId });
    return {
      order_id: r.order.locator,
      pnr: r.order.locator,
      status: r.order.status,
      auto_id: r.vehicle_id,
      refund: { ...r.refund },
      inventory_restoration: { status: 'RESTORED', vehicle_id: r.vehicle_id, new_state: ESTADO[r.vehicle_state] ?? 'DISPONIBLE' },
      message: 'Orden de renta cancelada exitosamente. Vehículo liberado y reembolso procesado.',
    };
  }

  // ══ Flota y mantenimiento (las rutas con :id van al final) ═════════════════
  @Get(':id/coberturas')
  async coberturas(@Param('id') id: string, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: any) {
    return coverageOptions(this.vehicleOr404(await this.catalogData(res), id));
  }

  @Get(':id')
  async detail(@Param('id') id: string, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: any) {
    const cat = await this.catalogData(res);
    return vehicleCard(this.vehicleOr404(cat, id), cat);
  }

  @Post(':id/devolucion')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async devolucion(@Param('id') id: string) {
    return cooldownResponse(await this.backends.booking.call<any>('ReturnVehicle', { vehicle_id: id }));
  }

  @Post(':id/mantenimiento/registro')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @V2Roles('MANTENIMIENTO', 'ADMIN')
  @UseGuards(V2AccessGuard, IdempotencyKeyGuard)
  @UseInterceptors(IdempotencyInterceptor)
  async mantenimiento(@Param('id') id: string, @Body() b: any, @Headers('x-role') _role?: string) {
    const bad: InvalidParam[] = [];
    if (!isInt(b?.kilometraje_registrado, 0)) bad.push({ name: 'kilometraje_registrado', reason: 'Es obligatorio y debe ser un entero mayor o igual a 0.' });
    if (!isInt(b?.nivel_combustible, 0, 100)) bad.push({ name: 'nivel_combustible', reason: 'Es obligatorio y debe ser un porcentaje entero de 0 a 100.' });
    if (!isText(b?.tecnico_id)) bad.push({ name: 'tecnico_id', reason: 'Es obligatorio.' });
    if (b?.nuevo_proximo_mantenimiento_km !== undefined && !isInt(b.nuevo_proximo_mantenimiento_km, 1)) bad.push({ name: 'nuevo_proximo_mantenimiento_km', reason: 'Debe ser un entero positivo.' });
    throwIfInvalid(bad);

    const r = await this.backends.booking.call<any>('RegisterMaintenance', {
      vehicle_id: id,
      technician_id: b.tecnico_id,
      odometer_km: b.kilometraje_registrado,
      fuel_level: b.nivel_combustible,
      body_condition: b.condiciones_carroceria ?? '',
      tires_condition: b.estado_neumaticos ?? '',
      notes: b.observaciones ?? '',
      maintenance_done: !!b.mantenimiento_realizado,
      new_next_maintenance_km: b.nuevo_proximo_mantenimiento_km ?? 0,
    });
    const v = r.vehicle;
    return {
      auto_id: v.vehicle_id,
      marca_modelo: `${v.make} ${v.model}`,
      kilometraje_actual: v.odometer_km,
      proximo_mantenimiento_km: v.next_maintenance_km,
      mantenimiento_requerido: r.alert,
      alerta_preventiva: r.alert
        ? `ALERTA PREVENTIVA: El vehículo ha superado el límite de ${v.next_maintenance_km} km. Mantenimiento requerido.`
        : 'Sin alertas: el kilometraje está dentro del umbral preventivo.',
      nivel_combustible: `${b.nivel_combustible}%`,
      bitacora_id: r.log_id,
      timestamp: r.timestamp,
    };
  }

  /** Normaliza un Order gRPC (sin nulos): el reembolso solo existe si tiene id. */
  private shape(o: any) {
    return { ...o, refund: o.refund?.refund_id ? o.refund : null, pricing: o.pricing?.days ? o.pricing : null };
  }
}

===== services/apps/gateway/src/v2/support.ts =====

import {
  computePricing, COVERAGE_RULES, Coverage, InvalidParam, invalidDates, invalidParams, needsAgeSurcharge, normalizeCoverage, rentalDays,
} from '@autos/common';

/**
 * Soporte del contrato REST v2 (autos-openapi-v2.yaml): traducción de los mensajes gRPC (inglés, 6 estados
 * internos) a los esquemas en español del contrato, y validación de entradas con `invalid_params` (RFC 7807).
 */

// ── Máquina de estados ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const ESTADO: Record<string, string> = {
  AVAILABLE: 'DISPONIBLE',
  RESERVED: 'RESERVADO',
  READY_FOR_DELIVERY: 'LISTO_PARA_ENTREGAR',
  DELIVERED: 'ENTREGADO',
  CLEANING: 'EN_LIMPIEZA',
  MAINTENANCE: 'EN_MANTENIMIENTO',
};

export const CATEGORIES = ['ECONOMICO', 'COMPACTO', 'SUV', 'PREMIUM', 'VAN'] as const;
export const TRANSMISSIONS = ['MANUAL', 'AUTOMATICO'] as const;

export function categoryOf(v: any): string {
  const t = String(v.car_type ?? '').toLowerCase();
  if (t.includes('suv')) return 'SUV';
  if (t.includes('van') || t.includes('minivan')) return 'VAN';
  if (t.includes('compact')) return 'COMPACTO';
  return v.price_per_day >= 60 ? 'PREMIUM' : 'ECONOMICO'; // sedanes: por tarifa
}

export const transmissionOf = (v: any) => (/auto/i.test(String(v.transmission)) ? 'AUTOMATICO' : 'MANUAL');

const ASSETS = () => (process.env.PUBLIC_ASSETS_URL ?? '').replace(/\/$/, '');
const absolute = (url: string) => (!url ? '' : /^https?:\/\//.test(url) ? url : `${ASSETS()}${url}`);

const normalize = (s: string) => s.normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '').toLowerCase();

// ── Fechas y horas ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const DATE_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
const SLOT_RE = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):(00|30)$/;

export const isDate = (s: unknown): s is string =>
  typeof s === 'string' && DATE_RE.test(s) && new Date(`${s}T00:00:00Z`).toISOString().startsWith(s);
export const isSlot = (s: unknown): s is string => typeof s === 'string' && SLOT_RE.test(s);

/** Fecha de hoy en Ecuador (YYYY-MM-DD). */
export const todayEcuador = () => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Guayaquil' }).format(new Date());

/**
 * Valida las fechas de una búsqueda o reserva. Los errores de formato van a `bad` (→ 400 MALFORMED_REQUEST);
 * los semánticos (pasado, devolución anterior a la recogida) se devuelven aparte (→ 422 INVALID_DATES, que no
 * dispara compensaciones de Saga en Booking).
 */
export function checkTrip(b: { pickupDate: any; pickupTime?: any; dropoffDate: any; dropoffTime?: any }, bad: InvalidParam[], requireTimes: boolean): InvalidParam[] {
  const semantic: InvalidParam[] = [];
  const pd = { name: 'pickup_date', ok: isDate(b.pickupDate) };
  const dd = { name: 'dropoff_date', ok: isDate(b.dropoffDate) };
  if (!pd.ok) bad.push({ name: pd.name, reason: 'Debe ser una fecha válida en formato YYYY-MM-DD.' });
  if (!dd.ok) bad.push({ name: dd.name, reason: 'Debe ser una fecha válida en formato YYYY-MM-DD.' });
  for (const [name, val] of [['pickup_time', b.pickupTime], ['dropoff_time', b.dropoffTime]] as const) {
    if (val === undefined && !requireTimes) continue;
    if (!isSlot(val)) bad.push({ name, reason: 'Debe ser una franja de 30 minutos en formato HH:00 o HH:30.' });
  }
  // ALLOW_PAST_DATES=true (pruebas de integración con los ejemplos del contrato, cuyas fechas ya pasaron) omite esta regla.
  const allowPast = /^(1|true)$/i.test(process.env.ALLOW_PAST_DATES ?? '');
  if (pd.ok && !allowPast && b.pickupDate < todayEcuador()) semantic.push({ name: 'pickup_date', reason: 'La fecha de recogida no puede ser en el pasado.' });
  if (pd.ok && dd.ok) {
    if (b.dropoffDate < b.pickupDate) semantic.push({ name: 'dropoff_date', reason: 'La fecha de devolución no puede ser anterior a la de recogida.' });
    else if (b.dropoffDate === b.pickupDate && isSlot(b.pickupTime) && isSlot(b.dropoffTime) && b.dropoffTime <= b.pickupTime) {
      semantic.push({ name: 'dropoff_time', reason: 'La hora de devolución debe ser posterior a la de recogida el mismo día.' });
    }
  }
  return semantic;
}

export function throwIfBadDates(semantic: InvalidParam[]) {
  if (semantic.length) throw invalidDates(semantic);
}

export function throwIfInvalid(bad: InvalidParam[]) {
  if (bad.length) throw invalidParams('Uno o más campos no cumplen con el contrato.', bad);
}

export const isInt = (n: unknown, min = 0, max = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER): n is number =>
  typeof n === 'number' && Number.isInteger(n) && n >= min && n <= max;
export const isEmail = (s: unknown): s is string => typeof s === 'string' && /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(s);
export const isText = (s: unknown): s is string => typeof s === 'string' && s.trim().length > 0;

// ── Ubicaciones ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const CITY_NAMES: Record<string, number> = { quito: 1, guayaquil: 2, cuenca: 3 };

/** Resuelve el texto libre del contrato (IATA, ciudad o nombre de agencia) a {airport?, city_id?}. */
export function resolveLocation(text: string, depots: any[]): { airport?: string; city_id?: number } | null {
  const iata = /\b(UIO|GYE|CUE)\b/i.exec(text)?.[1]?.toUpperCase();
  if (iata && depots.some((d) => d.airport === iata)) return { airport: iata };
  const n = normalize(text);
  const depot = depots.find((d) => n.includes(normalize(d.name)) || normalize(d.name).includes(n));
  if (depot) return { city_id: depot.city_id };
  for (const [name, id] of Object.entries(CITY_NAMES)) if (n.includes(name)) return { city_id: id };
  return null;
}

export const locationMatches = (v: any, loc: { airport?: string; city_id?: number } | null, depots: any[]) => {
  if (!loc) return true;
  const d = depots.find((x) => x.depot_id === v.depot_id);
  if (!d) return false;
  return loc.airport ? d.airport === loc.airport : d.city_id === loc.city_id;
};

// ── Mapeadores a los esquemas del contrato ──────────────────────────────────
export interface CatalogData {
  vehicles: any[];
  depots: any[];
  suppliers: any[];
}

export function vehicleCard(v: any, cat: CatalogData, opts: { days?: number; driverAge?: number; coverage?: Coverage } = {}) {
  const depot = cat.depots.find((d) => d.depot_id === v.depot_id);
  const supplier = cat.suppliers.find((s) => s.supplier_id === v.supplier_id);
  const days = opts.days ?? 1;
  const total = opts.days
    ? computePricing({ dailyPrice: v.price_per_day, days, coverage: opts.coverage ?? 'BASICA', driverAge: opts.driverAge ?? 30 }).total
    : v.price_per_day;
  const category = categoryOf(v);
  const transmission = transmissionOf(v);
  const photo = absolute(v.image_url);
  const mantenimiento_requerido = v.odometer_km >= v.next_maintenance_km;
  return {
    vehicle_id: v.vehicle_id,
    marca: v.make,
    modelo: v.model,
    categoria: category,
    precio_diario: v.price_per_day,
    precio_total: total,
    moneda: 'USD',
    transmision: transmission,
    asientos: v.seats,
    puertas: v.doors,
    maletas_grandes: v.bag_capacity,
    maletas_pequenas: v.bag_capacity > 0 ? 1 : 0,
    aire_acondicionado: true,
    kilometraje_ilimitado: true,
    politica_combustible: 'LLENO_A_LLENO',
    cancelacion_gratuita: true,
    proveedor: {
      nombre: supplier?.name ?? `Proveedor ${v.supplier_id}`,
      rating: Number(((depot?.score ?? 0) * 2).toFixed(1)),
      ubicacion: depot?.name ?? '',
    },
    estado: ESTADO[v.status] ?? 'DISPONIBLE',
    kilometraje_actual: v.odometer_km,
    proximo_mantenimiento_km: v.next_maintenance_km,
    mantenimiento_requerido,
    foto_url: photo,
    images: photo ? [photo] : [],
    // alias canónicos (el contrato publica ambos nombres)
    auto_id: v.vehicle_id,
    id: v.vehicle_id,
    brand: v.make,
    model: v.model,
    category,
    daily_price: v.price_per_day,
    total_price_usd: total,
    currency: 'USD',
    transmission,
    seats: v.seats,
    doors: v.doors,
    air_conditioning: true,
    unlimited_mileage: true,
    fuel_policy: 'Lleno a Lleno (Full-to-Full)',
    free_cancellation: true,
    // contrato v3
    total_price: total,
    status: ESTADO[v.status] ?? 'DISPONIBLE',
    ...(v.status === 'CLEANING' && v.available_from
      ? {
          cleaning_cooldown: {
            cooldown_horas: 2,
            cooldown_minutos: 120,
            activo: new Date(v.available_from).getTime() > Date.now(),
            inicio_cooldown: new Date(new Date(v.available_from).getTime() - 120 * 60_000).toISOString(),
            fin_cooldown: new Date(v.available_from).toISOString(),
            proximo_estado: 'DISPONIBLE',
          },
        }
      : {}),
  };
}

export function fleetItem(v: any) {
  const cleaning = v.status === 'CLEANING' && !!v.available_from;
  const ms = cleaning ? new Date(v.available_from).getTime() - Date.now() : 0;
  return {
    id: v.vehicle_id,
    marca: v.make,
    modelo: v.model,
    categoria: categoryOf(v),
    precio_diario: v.price_per_day,
    estado_operativo: ESTADO[v.status] ?? 'DISPONIBLE',
    cooldown_activo: cleaning && ms > 0,
    segundos_restantes_cooldown: Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000)),
    disponible_estimado_at: cleaning ? v.available_from : null,
    kilometraje_actual: v.odometer_km,
    umbral_mantenimiento_km: v.next_maintenance_km,
    alerta_mantenimiento: v.odometer_km >= v.next_maintenance_km,
    foto_url: absolute(v.image_url),
  };
}

export function coverageOptions(v: any) {
  const options = [
    {
      tipo: 'BASICA' as const,
      nombre: 'Protección Básica (Incluida)',
      descripcion: `Franquicia de $${COVERAGE_RULES.BASICA.franchise.toFixed(2)} ante daños y depósito de garantía retenido en tarjeta de crédito.`,
      beneficios: ['Responsabilidad civil obligatoria', 'Kilometraje ilimitado', 'Cancelación gratuita'],
    },
    {
      tipo: 'TOTAL' as const,
      nombre: 'Cobertura Total / Protección Premium',
      descripcion: 'Elimina totalmente la franquicia ante daños en carrocería, cristales, neumáticos, bajos y pérdida de llaves.',
      beneficios: ['Franquicia $0.00', 'Sin depósito de garantía', 'Cristales, neumáticos y bajos', 'Pérdida de llaves', 'Kilometraje ilimitado'],
    },
  ];
  return {
    auto_id: v.vehicle_id,
    vehiculo: `${v.make} ${v.model}`,
    opciones: options.map((o) => ({
      ...o,
      costo_adicional_diario: COVERAGE_RULES[o.tipo].dailyCost,
      franquicia: COVERAGE_RULES[o.tipo].franchise,
      deposito_garantia: COVERAGE_RULES[o.tipo].deposit,
    })),
  };
}

const orderStatus = (s: string) => s;

export function orderToV2(o: any, base: string) {
  const cancelled = o.status === 'CANCELLED';
  const autoId = o.vehicle?.vehicle_id ?? '';
  const refund = o.refund?.refund_id ? { ...o.refund } : null;
  const pricing = o.pricing ?? { days: 1, base: o.total_price, coverage: 0, surcharge: 0 };
  const status = orderStatus(o.status);
  const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
    order_id: o.locator,
    pnr: o.locator,
    status,
    auto_id: autoId,
    tipo_cobertura: o.coverage_type || 'BASICA',
    deposito_garantia: o.deposit ?? 0,
    total_price: {
      currency: o.currency,
      total: o.total_price,
      desglose: { dias: pricing.days, tarifa_base: pricing.base, cobertura: pricing.coverage, recargo_edad: pricing.surcharge },
    },
    voucher_qr_data: `BOOKING-CAR-${o.locator}-${autoId}-${status}`,
    vehicle_id: autoId,
    voucher_qr_payload: `${base}/autos/orders/${o.locator}`,
    checklist_mostrador: [
      'Licencia de conducir vigente (original)',
      'Documento de identidad',
      o.deposit > 0 ? `Tarjeta de crédito para el depósito de garantía ($${Number(o.deposit).toFixed(2)})` : 'Sin depósito de garantía (Cobertura Total)',
    ],
    refund,
    _links: {
      self: { href: `${base}/autos/orders/${o.locator}` },
      ...(o.status === 'CONFIRMED'
        ? {
            checkin: { href: `${base}/autos/reservas/${o.locator}/checkin`, method: 'POST' },
            cancel: { href: `${base}/autos/orders/${o.locator}/cancel`, method: 'POST' },
          }
        : {}),
    },
    codigo_reserva: o.locator,
    estado: status,
    moneda: o.currency,
    precio_total_monto: o.total_price,
  };
  if (cancelled && refund) {
    body.reembolso_id = refund.refund_id;
    body.monto_reembolsado = refund.amount;
  }
  if (o.status === 'COMPLETADA' && o.returned_at) {
    const start = new Date(o.returned_at).getTime();
    const end = start + 120 * 60_000;
    body.cooldown_info = {
      cooldown_horas: 2,
      cooldown_minutos: 120,
      activo: Date.now() < end,
      inicio_cooldown: new Date(start).toISOString(),
      fin_cooldown: new Date(end).toISOString(),
      proximo_estado: 'DISPONIBLE',
    };
  }
  return body;
}

export function cooldownResponse(r: { vehicle: any; cooldown_hours: number; available_from: string }) {
  const v = r.vehicle;
  return {
    auto_id: v.vehicle_id,
    vehiculo: `${v.make} ${v.model}`,
    estado: ESTADO[v.status] ?? 'EN_LIMPIEZA',
    cooldown_horas: r.cooldown_hours,
    disponible_desde: r.available_from,
    mensaje: `Vehículo en ciclo de limpieza y desinfección. Estará disponible en ${r.cooldown_hours} horas.`,
  };
}

export function promoToV2(p: any) {
  return {
    proveedor_codigo: p.provider_code,
    nombre_socio: p.partner_name,
    vertical: p.vertical,
    permite_promociones: p.allows_promotions,
    porcentaje_descuento_maximo: p.max_discount_percentage,
    destinos_habilitados: p.enabled_destinations ?? [],
    ...(p.valid_until ? { vigencia_hasta: p.valid_until } : {}),
    condiciones_combo: p.combo_conditions ?? '',
  };
}

export { needsAgeSurcharge, normalizeCoverage, rentalDays };

import { Column, CreateDateColumn, Entity, PrimaryGeneratedColumn } from 'typeorm';

/** Administrador creado desde el panel (convive con el administrador de arranque definido por entorno). */
@Entity('admins')
export class AdminUser {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 60, unique: true })
  username: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 200 })
  password_hash: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100, nullable: true })
  first_name: string | null;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100, nullable: true })
  last_name: string | null;

  @CreateDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' })
  created_at: Date;
}

@Entity('customers')
export class Customer {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 180, unique: true })
  email: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 200 })
  password_hash: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100 })
  first_name: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100 })
  last_name: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 30, nullable: true })
  phone_number: string | null;

  @CreateDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' })
  created_at: Date;
}

================================================================================
===== services/apps/identity/src/identity.controller.ts =====
================================================================================
import { GrpcMethod } from '@nestjs/microservices';
import { GrpcController } from '@autos/common';
import { IdentityService } from './identity.service';

const S = 'IdentityService';

/** Implementación del contrato contracts/proto/autos/identity/v1/identity.proto */
@GrpcController()
export class IdentityController {
  constructor(private readonly identity: IdentityService) {}

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'RegisterCustomer')
  registerCustomer(req: any) {
    return this.identity.registerCustomer(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'LoginCustomer')
  loginCustomer(req: any) {
    return this.identity.loginCustomer(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'GetCustomer')
  getCustomer(req: any) {
    return this.identity.getCustomer(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'LoginAdmin')
  loginAdmin(req: any) {
    return this.identity.loginAdmin(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'VerifyToken')
  verifyToken(req: any) {
    return this.identity.verifyToken(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'ListUsers')
  listUsers() {
    return this.identity.listUsers();
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'CreateUser')
  createUser(req: any) {
    return this.identity.createUser(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'DeleteUser')
  async deleteUser(req: any) {
    await this.identity.deleteUser(req);
    return {};
  }
}

import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { databaseModule, healthModuleFor } from '@autos/common';
import { AdminUser, Customer } from './entities/identity.entities';
import { IdentityController } from './identity.controller';
import { IdentityService } from './identity.service';
import { PasswordService } from './password.service';

@Module({
  imports: [
    databaseModule('identity', [AdminUser, Customer]),
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([AdminUser, Customer]),
    healthModuleFor('identity-service'),
  ],
  controllers: [IdentityController],
  providers: [IdentityService, PasswordService],
})
export class IdentityModule {}

================================================================================
===== services/apps/identity/src/identity.service.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
import { badRequest, conflict, JwtTokens, notFound, unauthorized } from '@autos/common';
import { AdminUser, Customer } from './entities/identity.entities';
import { PasswordService } from './password.service';

const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const USERNAME_RE = /^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{3,60}$/;

const envAdminUsername = () => process.env.ADMIN_USERNAME || 'admin';
const envAdminPassword = () => process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD || 'rodadoec2026';

function safeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  const x = Buffer.from(a);
  const y = Buffer.from(b);
  return x.length === y.length && timingSafeEqual(x, y);
}

export interface UserAccountMsg {
  id: string;
  role: 'ADMIN' | 'CUSTOMER';
  login: string;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
  phone_number: string;
  created_at: string;
  protected: boolean;
}

@Injectable()
export class IdentityService {
  private readonly tokens = new JwtTokens();

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(AdminUser) private readonly admins: Repository<AdminUser>,
    @InjectRepository(Customer) private readonly customers: Repository<Customer>,
    private readonly passwords: PasswordService,
  ) {}

  // ── Clientes ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  async registerCustomer(req: {
    email: string;
    password: string;
    first_name: string;
    last_name: string;
    phone_number?: string;
  }) {
    const email = req.email.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (await this.customers.findOneBy({ email })) {
      throw conflict('Ya existe una cuenta registrada con ese correo.');
    }
    const saved = await this.customers.save(
      this.customers.create({
        email,
        password_hash: this.passwords.hash(req.password),
        first_name: req.first_name,
        last_name: req.last_name,
        phone_number: req.phone_number || null,
      }),
    );
    return { token: this.tokens.signCustomer(saved.id), profile: this.profile(saved) };
  }

  async loginCustomer(req: { login: string; password: string }) {
    const customer = await this.customers.findOneBy({ email: req.login.trim().toLowerCase() });
    if (!customer || !this.passwords.verify(req.password, customer.password_hash)) {
      throw unauthorized('Correo o contraseña incorrectos.');
    }
    return { token: this.tokens.signCustomer(customer.id), profile: this.profile(customer) };
  }

  async getCustomer(req: { id: string }) {
    const customer = await this.customers.findOneBy({ id: req.id });
    if (!customer) throw unauthorized('Cuenta no encontrada.');
    return this.profile(customer);
  }

  // ── Administradores ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  async loginAdmin(req: { login: string; password: string }) {
    // Se evalúan ambas comparaciones sin cortocircuito para no filtrar cuál falló.
    const validUser = safeEqual(req.login, envAdminUsername());
    const validPass = safeEqual(req.password, envAdminPassword());
    if (validUser && validPass) return { token: this.tokens.signAdmin(req.login) };

    const admin = await this.admins.findOneBy({ username: req.login.trim().toLowerCase() });
    if (!admin || !this.passwords.verify(req.password, admin.password_hash)) {
      throw unauthorized('Usuario o contraseña incorrectos.');
    }
    return { token: this.tokens.signAdmin(admin.username) };
  }

  verifyToken(req: { token: string }) {
    const principal = this.tokens.verify(req.token);
    if (!principal) throw unauthorized('Sesión inválida o expirada.');
    return principal;
  }

  // ── Gestión de usuarios (panel Admin) ───────────────────────────────────
  async listUsers(): Promise<{ users: UserAccountMsg[] }> {
    const [admins, customers] = await Promise.all([
      this.admins.find({ order: { created_at: 'ASC' } }),
      this.customers.find({ order: { created_at: 'ASC' } }),
    ]);
    return {
      users: [
        this.account('', 'ADMIN', envAdminUsername(), '', '', '', '', true),
        ...admins.map((a) =>
          this.account(a.id, 'ADMIN', a.username, a.first_name, a.last_name, '', a.created_at.toISOString(), false),
        ),
        ...customers.map((c) =>
          this.account(c.id, 'CUSTOMER', c.email, c.first_name, c.last_name, c.phone_number, c.created_at.toISOString(), false),
        ),
      ],
    };
  }

  async createUser(req: {
    role: string;
    username?: string;
    email?: string;
    password: string;
    first_name?: string;
    last_name?: string;
    phone_number?: string;
  }): Promise<UserAccountMsg> {
    if (req.role === 'ADMIN') {
      if (!req.username || !USERNAME_RE.test(req.username)) {
        throw badRequest('username es obligatorio (3–60 caracteres: letras, números, punto, guion o guion bajo).');
      }
      const username = req.username.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (username === envAdminUsername().toLowerCase() || (await this.admins.findOneBy({ username }))) {
        throw conflict('Ya existe un administrador con ese username.');
      }
      const saved = await this.admins.save(
        this.admins.create({
          username,
          password_hash: this.passwords.hash(req.password),
          first_name: req.first_name || null,
          last_name: req.last_name || null,
        }),
      );
      return this.account(saved.id, 'ADMIN', saved.username, saved.first_name, saved.last_name, '', saved.created_at.toISOString(), false);
    }

    if (req.role === 'CUSTOMER') {
      if (!req.email || !EMAIL_RE.test(req.email)) throw badRequest('email válido es obligatorio para crear un cliente.');
      if (!req.first_name || !req.last_name) {
        throw badRequest('first_name y last_name son obligatorios para crear un cliente.');
      }
      const email = req.email.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (await this.customers.findOneBy({ email })) throw conflict('Ya existe una cuenta registrada con ese correo.');
      const saved = await this.customers.save(
        this.customers.create({
          email,
          password_hash: this.passwords.hash(req.password),
          first_name: req.first_name,
          last_name: req.last_name,
          phone_number: req.phone_number || null,
        }),
      );
      return this.account(saved.id, 'CUSTOMER', saved.email, saved.first_name, saved.last_name, saved.phone_number, saved.created_at.toISOString(), false);
    }
    throw badRequest('role debe ser ADMIN o CUSTOMER.');
  }

  async deleteUser(req: { role: string; id: string }): Promise<void> {
    const repo = req.role === 'ADMIN' ? this.admins : req.role === 'CUSTOMER' ? this.customers : null;
    if (!repo) throw badRequest('role debe ser ADMIN o CUSTOMER.');
    const result = await (repo as Repository<any>).delete(req.id);
    if (!result.affected) throw notFound('Usuario no encontrado', `Usuario "${req.id}" no encontrado`);
  }

  // ── helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  private profile(c: Customer) {
    return {
      id: c.id,
      email: c.email,
      first_name: c.first_name,
      last_name: c.last_name,
      phone_number: c.phone_number ?? '',
    };
  }

  private account(
    id: string,
    role: 'ADMIN' | 'CUSTOMER',
    login: string,
    first_name: string | null,
    last_name: string | null,
    phone_number: string | null,
    created_at: string,
    isProtected: boolean,
  ): UserAccountMsg {
    return {
      id,
      role,
      login,
      first_name: first_name ?? '',
      last_name: last_name ?? '',
      phone_number: phone_number ?? '',
      created_at,
      protected: isProtected,
    };
  }
}

===== services/apps/identity/src/password.service.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomBytes, scryptSync, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto';

/** Hash de contraseñas con scrypt nativo de Node. Formato: "<salt-hex>:<hash-hex>". */
@Injectable()
export class PasswordService {
  hash(plain: string): string {
    const salt = randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
    return `${salt}:${scryptSync(plain, salt, 64).toString('hex')}`;
  }

  verify(plain: string, stored: string): boolean {
    const [salt, hash] = stored.split(':');
    if (!salt || !hash) return false;
    const derived = scryptSync(plain, salt, 64);
    const expected = Buffer.from(hash, 'hex');
    return derived.length === expected.length && timingSafeEqual(derived, expected);
  }
}

===== services/apps/payments/src/entities/payment.entity.ts =====

import { Column, CreateDateColumn, Entity, PrimaryGeneratedColumn, ValueTransformer } from 'typeorm';

class NumericTransformer implements ValueTransformer {
  to(data: number | null): number | null {
    return data;
  }
  from(data: string | null): number | null {
    return data ? parseFloat(data) : null;
  }
}

export enum PaymentStatus {
  APPROVED = 'APPROVED',
  DECLINED = 'DECLINED',
  REFUNDED = 'REFUNDED',
}

/** Pago simulado. Nunca se guarda el número completo de la tarjeta ni el CVV: solo marca y últimos 4. */
@Entity('payments')
export class Payment {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 30, unique: true })
  reference: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 20 })
  status: PaymentStatus;

  @Column('numeric', { precision: 10, scale: 2, transformer: new NumericTransformer() })
  amount: number;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 3 })
  currency: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 50 })
  order_preview_id: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 20 })
  card_brand: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 4 })
  card_last4: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 100 })
  cardholder_name: string;

  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 200, nullable: true })
  decline_reason: string | null;

  // Se llena cuando el pago se consume en una orden (un pago = una orden).
  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 50, nullable: true })
  order_id: string | null;

  @CreateDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' })
  created_at: Date;
}

import { GrpcMethod } from '@nestjs/microservices';
import { GrpcController } from '@autos/common';
import { PaymentsService } from './payments.service';

const S = 'PaymentsService';

/** Implementación del contrato contracts/proto/autos/payments/v1/payments.proto */
@GrpcController()
export class PaymentsController {
  constructor(private readonly payments: PaymentsService) {}

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'Charge')
  charge(req: any) {
    return this.payments.charge(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'GetPayment')
  getPayment(req: any) {
    return this.payments.getPayment(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'Consume')
  consume(req: any) {
    return this.payments.consume(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'Refund')
  refund(req: any) {
    return this.payments.refund(req);
  }

  @GrpcMethod(S, 'RefundByOrder')
  async refundByOrder(req: any) {
    await this.payments.refundByOrder(req);
    return {};
  }
}

===== services/apps/payments/src/payments.service.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
import { IsNull, Repository } from 'typeorm';
import { notFound, paymentDeclined, paymentRequired } from '@autos/common';
import { Payment, PaymentStatus } from './entities/payment.entity';

interface Card {
  holder_name: string;
  number: string;
  expiry_month: number;
  expiry_year: number;
  cvv: string;
}

function luhnValid(number: string): boolean {
  let sum = 0;
  let double = false;
  for (let i = number.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    let d = Number(number[i]);
    if (double) {
      d *= 2;
      if (d > 9) d -= 9;
    }
    sum += d;
    double = !double;
  }
  return sum % 10 === 0;
}

function cardBrand(number: string): string {
  if (number.startsWith('4')) return 'VISA';
  if (/^(5[1-5]|2[2-7])/.test(number)) return 'MASTERCARD';
  if (/^3[47]/.test(number)) return 'AMEX';
  return 'CARD';
}

export function toPaymentMsg(p: Payment) {
  return {
    payment_reference: p.reference,
    status: p.status,
    amount: p.amount,
    currency: p.currency,
    card_brand: p.card_brand,
    card_last4: p.card_last4,
    created_at: p.created_at.toISOString(),
    order_id: p.order_id ?? '',
    order_preview_id: p.order_preview_id,
    decline_reason: p.decline_reason ?? '',
  };
}

/**
 * Pasarela de pago SIMULADA (no mueve dinero). Reglas de prueba:
 *  - número inválido (Luhn) o tarjeta vencida → rechazado
 *  - 4000 0000 0000 0002 → rechazado (fondos insuficientes) · 4000 0000 0000 0069 → bloqueada
 *  - cualquier otra tarjeta válida → aprobado
 */
@Injectable()
export class PaymentsService {
  constructor(@InjectRepository(Payment) private readonly payments: Repository<Payment>) {}

  async charge(req: { order_preview_id: string; amount: number; currency: string; card?: Card; channel?: string }) {
    // Canal B2B (contrato v2): Booking liquida con la tienda, no hay tarjeta que evaluar.
    const b2b = req.channel === 'B2B';
    const card: Card = b2b ? { holder_name: 'BOOKING B2B', number: '0000000000000000', expiry_month: 12, expiry_year: 2099, cvv: '000' } : (req.card as Card);
    const reason = b2b ? null : this.evaluateCard(card);
    const saved = await this.payments.save(
      this.payments.create({
        reference: `PAY-${randomUUID().slice(0, 10).toUpperCase()}`,
        status: reason ? PaymentStatus.DECLINED : PaymentStatus.APPROVED,
        amount: req.amount,
        currency: req.currency || 'USD',
        order_preview_id: req.order_preview_id,
        card_brand: b2b ? 'B2B' : cardBrand(card.number),
        card_last4: card.number.slice(-4),
        cardholder_name: card.holder_name,
        decline_reason: reason,
        order_id: null,
      }),
    );
    if (reason) throw paymentDeclined(reason);
    return toPaymentMsg(saved);
  }

  async getPayment(req: { payment_reference: string }) {
    const p = await this.payments.findOneBy({ reference: req.payment_reference });
    if (!p) throw notFound('Pago no encontrado', `No existe un pago con referencia "${req.payment_reference}".`);
    return toPaymentMsg(p);
  }

  /** Atómico: solo un pago APROBADO y sin usar puede ligarse a una orden. */
  async consume(req: { payment_reference: string; order_id: string }) {
    const r = await this.payments.update(
      { reference: req.payment_reference, status: PaymentStatus.APPROVED, order_id: IsNull() },
      { order_id: req.order_id },
    );
    if (!r.affected) {
      throw paymentRequired('El pago no existe, no está aprobado o ya se usó en otra orden.');
    }
    return this.getPayment({ payment_reference: req.payment_reference });
  }

  async refund(req: { payment_reference: string }) {
    await this.payments.update({ reference: req.payment_reference }, { status: PaymentStatus.REFUNDED });
    return this.getPayment(req);
  }

  async refundByOrder(req: { order_id: string }) {
    await this.payments.update({ order_id: req.order_id }, { status: PaymentStatus.REFUNDED });
  }

  private evaluateCard(card: Card): string | null {
    if (!luhnValid(card.number)) return 'Número de tarjeta inválido.';
    const now = new Date();
    const expired =
      card.expiry_year < now.getFullYear() ||
      (card.expiry_year === now.getFullYear() && card.expiry_month < now.getMonth() + 1);
    if (expired) return 'La tarjeta está vencida.';
    if (card.number === '4000000000000002') return 'Fondos insuficientes.';
    if (card.number === '4000000000000069') return 'Tarjeta bloqueada por el emisor.';
    return null;
  }
}
