===== services/libs/common/src/bootstrap.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Controller, Get, Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { MicroserviceOptions } from '@nestjs/microservices';
import { ensureSchema } from './database';
import { grpcServerOptions } from './grpc';
import { StructuredLogger } from './logger';

export const SERVICE_VERSION = '1.1.0';

export function healthModuleFor(service: string) {
  @Controller()
  class HealthController {
    @Get('health')
    health() {
      return { status: 'ok', service, version: SERVICE_VERSION, uptime_s: Math.round(process.uptime()) };
    }
  }
  @Module({ controllers: [HealthController] })
  class HealthModule {}
  return HealthModule;
}

interface ServiceOptions {
  name: string;
  module: any;
  grpcPackage: string;
  protoFile: string;
  defaultHttpPort: number;
  defaultGrpcPort: number;
  /** schema de Postgres del servicio (se crea si no existe) */
  schema?: string;
}

/**
 * Arranque común de un microservicio: servidor gRPC (contrato .proto) + un puerto HTTP mínimo con /health
 * (lo usan Render, docker-compose y los monitores de disponibilidad).
 */
export async function bootstrapService(o: ServiceOptions): Promise<void> {
  const logger = new StructuredLogger(o.name);
  if (o.schema) await ensureSchema(o.schema);

  const app = await NestFactory.create(o.module, { logger });
  // GRPC_ON_PORT=true (Render): la plataforma expone un único puerto por servicio y termina TLS en su borde,
  // así que el servidor gRPC escucha directamente en $PORT y no se levanta el HTTP de /health.
  const grpcOnPort = /^(1|true)$/i.test(process.env.GRPC_ON_PORT ?? '') && !!process.env.PORT;
  const grpcPort = grpcOnPort ? process.env.PORT! : (process.env.GRPC_PORT ?? o.defaultGrpcPort);
  app.connectMicroservice<MicroserviceOptions>(grpcServerOptions(o.grpcPackage, o.protoFile, grpcPort), {
    inheritAppConfig: true,
  });
  await app.startAllMicroservices();

  if (grpcOnPort) {
    await app.init();
    logger.log(`${o.name} listo: gRPC :${grpcPort} (modo puerto único)`, 'bootstrap');
    return;
  }

  const httpPort = Number(process.env.PORT ?? o.defaultHttpPort);
  await app.listen(httpPort, '0.0.0.0');
  logger.log(`${o.name} listo: gRPC :${grpcPort} · HTTP /health :${httpPort}`, 'bootstrap');
}

================================================================================
===== services/libs/common/src/correlation.ts =====
================================================================================
import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'async_hooks';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';

/**
 * Correlation ID (Semana 6): identificador único que viaja con cada petición a través del gateway,
 * los microservicios (metadata gRPC) y los eventos, para poder reconstruir el recorrido completo.
 */
export const CORRELATION_HEADER = 'x-correlation-id';

interface Ctx {
  correlationId: string;
}

const storage = new AsyncLocalStorage<Ctx>();

export function runWithCorrelation<T>(correlationId: string | undefined, fn: () => T): T {
  return storage.run({ correlationId: correlationId || randomUUID() }, fn);
}

export function currentCorrelationId(): string | undefined {
  return storage.getStore()?.correlationId;
}

================================================================================
===== services/libs/common/src/database.ts =====
================================================================================
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Client } from 'pg';

function sslOption() {
  const on = /^(1|true)$/i.test(process.env.DATABASE_SSL ?? '') || process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
  return on ? { rejectUnauthorized: false } : false;
}

/**
 * Cada microservicio es dueño de su propio schema de Postgres (aislamiento de datos: ningún servicio
 * lee las tablas de otro; se hablan por gRPC). Una sola base permite usar el plan gratis de Render.
 */
export async function ensureSchema(schema: string): Promise<void> {
  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
  if (!url) throw new Error('DATABASE_URL no está definida');
  const client = new Client({ connectionString: url, ssl: sslOption() });
  await client.connect();
  try {
    await client.query(`CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS "${schema}"`);
  } finally {
    await client.end();
  }
}

export function databaseModule(schema: string, entities: Function[]) {
  return TypeOrmModule.forRoot({
    type: 'postgres',
    url: process.env.DATABASE_URL,
    schema,
    entities,
    synchronize: true, // prototipo académico: sin migraciones
    ssl: sslOption(),
    retryAttempts: 10,
    retryDelay: 3000,
  });
}

================================================================================
===== services/libs/common/src/grpc.ts =====
================================================================================
import {
  applyDecorators,
  ArgumentsHost,
  CallHandler,
  Catch,
  Controller,
  ExecutionContext,
  HttpException,
  HttpStatus,
  Injectable,
  NestInterceptor,
  UseFilters,
  UseInterceptors,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClientGrpc, GrpcOptions, RpcException, Transport } from '@nestjs/microservices';
import { ChannelCredentials, Metadata, status } from '@grpc/grpc-js';
import { existsSync } from 'fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'path';
import { firstValueFrom, Observable, throwError, timeout } from 'rxjs';
import { CORRELATION_HEADER, currentCorrelationId, runWithCorrelation } from './correlation';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Contratos .proto
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Carpeta contracts/proto: se busca hacia arriba (repo, imagen Docker) o se toma de PROTO_DIR. */
export function protoDir(): string {
  if (process.env.PROTO_DIR) return process.env.PROTO_DIR;
  let dir = __dirname;
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const candidate = join(dir, 'contracts', 'proto');
    if (existsSync(candidate)) return candidate;
    dir = dirname(dir);
  }
  return join(process.cwd(), '..', 'contracts', 'proto');
}

function loader() {
  return { keepCase: true, longs: String, enums: String, defaults: true, oneofs: true, arrays: true, includeDirs: [protoDir()] };
}

export function grpcServerOptions(pkg: string, protoFile: string, port: number | string): GrpcOptions {
  return {
    transport: Transport.GRPC,
    options: {
      package: pkg,
      protoPath: join(protoDir(), protoFile),
      url: `0.0.0.0:${port}`,
      loader: loader(),
    },
  };
}

export function grpcClientOptions(pkg: string, protoFile: string, url: string): GrpcOptions {
  const tls = /^(1|true)$/i.test(process.env.GRPC_TLS ?? '');
  return {
    transport: Transport.GRPC,
    options: {
      package: pkg,
      protoPath: join(protoDir(), protoFile),
      url,
      loader: loader(),
      ...(tls ? { credentials: ChannelCredentials.createSsl() } : {}),
      channelOptions: { 'grpc.keepalive_time_ms': 30000, 'grpc.keepalive_permit_without_calls': 1 },
    },
  };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Errores: ProblemDetails (application/problem+json) <-> gRPC status
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function httpToGrpc(httpStatus: number): number {
  switch (httpStatus) {
    case 400:
      return status.INVALID_ARGUMENT;
    case 401:
      return status.UNAUTHENTICATED;
    case 402:
      return status.FAILED_PRECONDITION;
    case 403:
      return status.PERMISSION_DENIED;
    case 404:
      return status.NOT_FOUND;
    case 409:
      return status.ABORTED;
    case 503:
      return status.UNAVAILABLE;
    default:
      return status.INTERNAL;
  }
}

/** Server: convierte cualquier error en un RpcException que lleva el ProblemDetails como JSON. */
export function toRpcException(e: unknown): RpcException {
  if (e instanceof RpcException) return e;
  if (e instanceof HttpException) {
    const httpStatus = e.getStatus();
    const body = e.getResponse();
    const problem =
      typeof body === 'object' && body !== null
        ? { ...(body as object), status: httpStatus }
        : { type: 'about:blank', title: String(body), status: httpStatus, detail: String(body), code: 'ERROR' };
    return new RpcException({ code: httpToGrpc(httpStatus), message: JSON.stringify(problem) });
  }
  const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
  return new RpcException({
    code: status.INTERNAL,
    message: JSON.stringify({
      type: 'about:blank',
      title: 'Error interno',
      status: 500,
      detail: message,
      code: 'INTERNAL',
    }),
  });
}

/** Client: reconstruye la HttpException (ProblemDetails) a partir del error gRPC. */
export function fromRpcError(err: any): HttpException {
  if (err instanceof HttpException) return err;
  const raw: string | undefined = typeof err?.details === 'string' ? err.details : err?.message;
  if (raw) {
    try {
      const problem = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (problem && typeof problem === 'object' && problem.status) {
        return new HttpException(problem, Number(problem.status));
      }
    } catch {
      /* no era JSON: se trata como error de transporte */
    }
  }
  const unavailable = err?.code === status.UNAVAILABLE || err?.code === status.DEADLINE_EXCEEDED || err?.name === 'TimeoutError';
  const httpStatus = unavailable ? HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE : HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY;
  return new HttpException(
    {
      type: 'https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/service-unavailable',
      title: unavailable ? 'Servicio no disponible' : 'Error en un servicio interno',
      status: httpStatus,
      detail: err?.name === 'TimeoutError' ? 'El microservicio no respondió dentro del tiempo de tolerancia.' : err?.details || err?.message || 'Fallo la comunicación con un microservicio.',
      code: unavailable ? 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE' : 'UPSTREAM_ERROR',
    },
    httpStatus,
  );
}

@Catch()
export class GrpcProblemFilter {
  catch(exception: unknown, _host: ArgumentsHost): Observable<never> {
    return throwError(() => toRpcException(exception).getError());
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Correlation ID en el servidor gRPC
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Injectable()
export class CorrelationRpcInterceptor implements NestInterceptor {
  intercept(context: ExecutionContext, next: CallHandler): Observable<any> {
    const md = context.switchToRpc().getContext() as Metadata;
    const cid = md?.get?.(CORRELATION_HEADER)?.[0] as string | undefined;
    return new Observable((subscriber) => {
      const sub = runWithCorrelation(cid, () => next.handle().subscribe(subscriber));
      return () => sub.unsubscribe();
    });
  }
}

/** Decorador de clase para los controladores gRPC: filtro de errores + correlation id. */
export const GrpcController = () =>
  applyDecorators(Controller(), UseFilters(GrpcProblemFilter), UseInterceptors(CorrelationRpcInterceptor));

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Cliente gRPC tipado con correlation id y errores ya traducidos
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type BreakerState = 'CLOSED' | 'OPEN' | 'HALF_OPEN';

const callTimeoutMs = () => Number(process.env.GRPC_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 5000);
const BREAKER_THRESHOLD = 5; // fallos de transporte seguidos que abren el circuito
const BREAKER_OPEN_MS = 10_000;

/**
 * Cliente gRPC con tolerancia perimetral: cada llamada expira a los 5 s (GRPC_TIMEOUT_MS) y un Circuit
 * Breaker corta las llamadas a un servicio caído (falla rápido con 503 en vez de colgarse) hasta que se
 * recupere. Un error de dominio (404, 409…) prueba que el servicio responde, así que no cuenta como fallo.
 */
export class GrpcCaller {
  private svc: any;
  private failures = 0;
  private openUntil = 0;

  constructor(
    private readonly client: ClientGrpc,
    private readonly serviceName: string,
  ) {}

  get breakerState(): BreakerState {
    if (!this.openUntil) return 'CLOSED';
    return Date.now() < this.openUntil ? 'OPEN' : 'HALF_OPEN';
  }

  async call<T = any>(method: string, data: object = {}): Promise<T> {
    if (this.breakerState === 'OPEN') {
      throw new HttpException(
        {
          type: 'https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/service-unavailable',
          title: 'Servicio no disponible',
          status: HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
          detail: `Circuit Breaker abierto: ${this.serviceName} no responde; se reintentará en unos segundos.`,
          code: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE',
        },
        HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
      );
    }
    this.svc ??= this.client.getService<any>(this.serviceName);
    const metadata = new Metadata();
    const cid = currentCorrelationId();
    if (cid) metadata.set(CORRELATION_HEADER, cid);
    try {
      const result = await firstValueFrom<T>(this.svc[method](data, metadata).pipe(timeout(callTimeoutMs())));
      this.failures = 0;
      this.openUntil = 0;
      return result;
    } catch (err) {
      const http = fromRpcError(err);
      if (http.getStatus() >= 502) {
        if (++this.failures >= BREAKER_THRESHOLD) this.openUntil = Date.now() + BREAKER_OPEN_MS;
      } else {
        this.failures = 0;
        this.openUntil = 0;
      }
      throw http;
    }
  }
}

===== services/libs/common/src/jwt.ts =====

import { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';

// Mismos secretos y valores por defecto de desarrollo que el monolito original.
export const adminJwtSecret = () => process.env.ADMIN_JWT_SECRET || 'dev-only-insecure-secret-rodadoec';
export const customerJwtSecret = () =>
  process.env.CUSTOMER_JWT_SECRET || 'dev-only-insecure-secret-rodadoec-customers';

export type Role = 'ADMIN' | 'CUSTOMER';

export interface Principal {
  role: Role;
  subject: string;
}

/**
 * JWT estándar (RFC 7519, HS256). Admin y cliente usan secretos distintos: un token de uno nunca
 * sirve para el otro. Lo usan Identidad (emitir/verificar) y el Gateway (verificar sin viaje de red).
 */
export class JwtTokens {
  private readonly admin = new JwtService({ secret: adminJwtSecret() });
  private readonly customer = new JwtService({ secret: customerJwtSecret() });

  signAdmin(username: string): string {
    return this.admin.sign({ sub: username, role: 'admin' }, { expiresIn: '12h' });
  }

  signCustomer(customerId: string): string {
    return this.customer.sign({ sub: customerId, role: 'customer' }, { expiresIn: '30d' });
  }

  verify(token: string | undefined): Principal | null {
    if (!token) return null;
    try {
      const p = this.admin.verify<{ sub: string }>(token);
      return { role: 'ADMIN', subject: p.sub };
    } catch {
      /* no es de admin: se prueba como cliente */
    }
    try {
      const p = this.customer.verify<{ sub: string }>(token);
      return { role: 'CUSTOMER', subject: p.sub };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
}

================================================================================
===== services/libs/common/src/logger.ts =====
================================================================================
import { LoggerService } from '@nestjs/common';
import { appendFile, mkdirSync } from 'fs';
import { join } from 'path';
import { currentCorrelationId } from './correlation';

/**
 * Logs estructurados en JSON (una línea por evento) con el correlation_id de la petición en curso.
 * Formato pensado para enviarse a un agregador (ELK, Loki, CloudWatch).
 *
 * Con LOG_DIR definido, cada línea se agrega además a un archivo físico `<LOG_DIR>/<servicio>.log`
 * (requisito de trazabilidad de la rúbrica RDA2: correlation_id e idempotency_key en logs de archivo).
 */
export class StructuredLogger implements LoggerService {
  private readonly file?: string;

  constructor(private readonly service: string) {
    const dir = process.env.LOG_DIR;
    if (dir) {
      try {
        mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
        this.file = join(dir, `${service}.log`);
      } catch {
        /* sin archivo: se sigue escribiendo a la consola */
      }
    }
  }

  /** Evento estructurado con campos propios (p. ej. idempotency_key, status, latencia). */
  event(level: string, message: string, context: string, extra: Record<string, unknown>) {
    this.write(level, message, context, extra);
  }

  private write(level: string, message: unknown, context?: string, extra?: Record<string, unknown>) {
    const line = {
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level,
      service: this.service,
      correlation_id: currentCorrelationId(),
      context,
      message: typeof message === 'string' ? message : JSON.stringify(message),
      ...extra,
    };
    const out = JSON.stringify(line);
    if (this.file) appendFile(this.file, out + '\n', () => undefined);
    if (level === 'error') process.stderr.write(out + '\n');
    else process.stdout.write(out + '\n');
  }

  log(message: unknown, context?: string) {
    this.write('info', message, context);
  }
  error(message: unknown, trace?: string, context?: string) {
    this.write('error', message, context, trace ? { trace } : undefined);
  }
  warn(message: unknown, context?: string) {
    this.write('warn', message, context);
  }
  debug(message: unknown, context?: string) {
    if (process.env.LOG_DEBUG) this.write('debug', message, context);
  }
  verbose(message: unknown, context?: string) {
    if (process.env.LOG_DEBUG) this.write('verbose', message, context);
  }
}

================================================================================
===== services/libs/common/src/pricing.ts =====
================================================================================
/**
 * Reglas de precio del contrato REST v2 (coberturas y recargo por edad), compartidas por el gateway
 * (búsqueda y coberturas) y el servicio de Reservas (cobro de la orden) para que ambos cuadren al centavo.
 */
export type Coverage = 'BASICA' | 'TOTAL';

export const COVERAGE_RULES: Record<Coverage, { dailyCost: number; franchise: number; deposit: number }> = {
  // Protección básica (incluida): franquicia de $1,200 y depósito retenido en tarjeta de crédito.
  BASICA: { dailyCost: 0, franchise: 1200, deposit: 1000 },
  // Cobertura total / premium: sin franquicia ni depósito.
  TOTAL: { dailyCost: 18, franchise: 0, deposit: 0 },
};

/** Recargo diario para conductores jóvenes (<25) o seniors (>70). */
export const SURCHARGE_PER_DAY = 8;
export const MIN_LEGAL_AGE = 18;

export const needsAgeSurcharge = (age: number) => age < 25 || age > 70;

const round2 = (n: number) => Number(n.toFixed(2));

/** Días de renta entre dos fechas YYYY-MM-DD (mínimo 1). */
export function rentalDays(pickupDate: string, dropoffDate: string): number {
  const ms = new Date(`${dropoffDate}T00:00:00Z`).getTime() - new Date(`${pickupDate}T00:00:00Z`).getTime();
  return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(ms / 86_400_000));
}

export function normalizeCoverage(value?: string): Coverage {
  return value === 'TOTAL' || value === 'PREMIUM' ? 'TOTAL' : 'BASICA';
}

export function computePricing(p: { dailyPrice: number; days: number; coverage: Coverage; driverAge: number }) {
  const base = round2(p.dailyPrice * p.days);
  const coverage = round2(COVERAGE_RULES[p.coverage].dailyCost * p.days);
  const surcharge = needsAgeSurcharge(p.driverAge) ? round2(SURCHARGE_PER_DAY * p.days) : 0;
  return {
    days: p.days,
    base,
    coverage,
    surcharge,
    total: round2(base + coverage + surcharge),
    deposit: COVERAGE_RULES[p.coverage].deposit,
  };
}

import { HttpException, HttpStatus } from '@nestjs/common';

/** Códigos de error del dominio (enum del contrato autos-openapi.yaml, más los de pago). */
export enum ProblemCode {
  VALIDATION_FAILED = 'VALIDATION_FAILED',
  CAR_NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE = 'CAR_NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE',
  PRICE_CHANGED = 'PRICE_CHANGED',
  DEPOT_CLOSED = 'DEPOT_CLOSED',
  DRIVER_AGE_RESTRICTION = 'DRIVER_AGE_RESTRICTION',
  BOOKING_NOT_CONFIRMED = 'BOOKING_NOT_CONFIRMED',
  CANCELLATION_NOT_ALLOWED = 'CANCELLATION_NOT_ALLOWED',
  RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED = 'RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED',
  PAYMENT_REFERENCE_INVALID = 'PAYMENT_REFERENCE_INVALID',
  PAYMENT_NOT_AUTHORIZED = 'PAYMENT_NOT_AUTHORIZED',
  PAYMENT_DECLINED = 'PAYMENT_DECLINED',
  PAYMENT_REQUIRED = 'PAYMENT_REQUIRED',
}

/** Construye una excepción con el formato application/problem+json del contrato. */
export function problem(
  status: HttpStatus,
  code: ProblemCode | string,
  title: string,
  detail: string,
  type?: string,
): HttpException {
  return new HttpException(
    {
      type:
        type ?? `https://api.booking-hub.com/errors/${String(code).toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, '-')}`,
      title,
      status,
      detail,
      code,
    },
    status,
  );
}

// ── Errores del contrato REST v2 (RFC 7807 con invalid_params) ─────────────
const V2_ERRORS = 'https://booking.epn.edu.ec/errors';

export interface InvalidParam {
  name: string;
  reason: string;
}

/** 400 con la lista de campos que no pasaron la validación (invalid_params). */
export function invalidParams(detail: string, params: InvalidParam[]): HttpException {
  return new HttpException(
    {
      type: `${V2_ERRORS}/validation-failed`,
      title: 'Petición inválida',
      status: HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
      detail,
      code: 'MALFORMED_REQUEST',
      invalid_params: params,
    },
    HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
  );
}

export const unprocessable = (code: string, title: string, detail: string) =>
  problem(HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY, code, title, detail, `${V2_ERRORS}/${code.toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, '-')}`);

export const driverUnderage = (age: number) =>
  unprocessable('DRIVER_UNDERAGE', 'Conductor menor de edad', `El conductor tiene ${age} años: la renta de vehículos exige ser mayor de 18 años.`);

export const driverBelowVehicleMinimum = (vehicleId: string, minAge: number, age: number) =>
  unprocessable(
    'DRIVER_AGE_RESTRICTION',
    'Edad del conductor no permitida',
    `El vehículo "${vehicleId}" exige un conductor de al menos ${minAge} años; el conductor indicado tiene ${age}.`,
  );

export const vehicleInCleaningCooldown = (vehicleId: string, availableFromIso: string) =>
  problem(
    HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
    'VEHICLE_IN_CLEANING_COOLDOWN',
    'Vehículo en Cooldown de Limpieza Obligatorio (2 Horas)',
    `El vehículo ${vehicleId} se encuentra en fase de limpieza y desinfección obligatoria de 120 minutos tras su retorno. Estará disponible nuevamente a las ${availableFromIso}.`,
    `${V2_ERRORS}/cooldown-activo`,
  );

export const vehicleInMaintenance = (vehicleId: string) =>
  problem(
    HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
    'VEHICLE_IN_MAINTENANCE',
    'Vehículo en mantenimiento',
    `El vehículo ${vehicleId} está en mantenimiento preventivo y no se puede reservar hasta que el técnico lo habilite.`,
    `${V2_ERRORS}/vehicle-in-maintenance`,
  );

export const invalidStateTransition = (detail: string) =>
  problem(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, 'INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION', 'Transición de estado no permitida', detail, `${V2_ERRORS}/invalid-state-transition`);

/**
 * 409 canónico del contrato v3 (Guía §4.3): el ÚNICO código que activa la compensación LIFO del orquestador
 * de Sagas de Booking es 409 con code VEHICLE_UNAVAILABLE. `reason` precisa el motivo (cooldown, reservado…).
 */
export const vehicleUnavailable = (vehicleId: string, reason: string, detail: string) =>
  new HttpException(
    {
      type: `${V2_ERRORS}/vehicle-unavailable`,
      title: 'Vehículo No Disponible',
      status: HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
      detail,
      code: 'VEHICLE_UNAVAILABLE',
      reason,
      vehicle_id: vehicleId,
    },
    HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
  );

/** 422: errores semánticos de fechas (no disparan compensaciones de Saga). */
export const invalidDates = (params: InvalidParam[]) =>
  new HttpException(
    {
      type: `${V2_ERRORS}/invalid-dates`,
      title: 'Fechas inválidas',
      status: HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
      detail: 'Las fechas de la reserva no son válidas.',
      code: 'INVALID_DATES',
      invalid_params: params,
    },
    HttpStatus.UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
  );

===== services/libs/common/src/ttl-cache.ts =====
================================================================================
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

interface Entry {
  value: unknown;
  expiresAt: number;
}

/** Caché en memoria con expiración (search_token, hold_id, order_preview_id). */
@Injectable()
export class TtlCache {
  private readonly store = new Map<string, Entry>();

  set(key: string, value: unknown, ttlMs: number): void {
    this.store.set(key, { value, expiresAt: Date.now() + ttlMs });
  }

  get<T>(key: string): T | undefined {
    const entry = this.store.get(key);
    if (!entry) return undefined;
    if (entry.expiresAt < Date.now()) {
      this.store.delete(key);
      return undefined;
    }
    return entry.value as T;
  }

  has(key: string): boolean {
    return this.get(key) !== undefined;
  }

  delete(key: string): void {
    this.store.delete(key);
  }
}

===== services/test/consume-brokers.js =====
================================================================================
/**
 * Consumidor de prueba de los brokers del agregador: imprime los CloudEvents que el nodo de Autos publica.
 *   node test/consume-brokers.js [segundos]        (por defecto 20)
 * RabbitMQ: exchange booking.events (topic), cola efímera con binding "auto.#"
 * Kafka:    topic topic-ordenes-b2b, desde el inicio
 * Variables: RABBITMQ_URL (amqp://guest:guest@localhost:5672), KAFKA_BROKERS (localhost:9092)
 */
const amqp = require('amqplib');
const { Kafka } = require('kafkajs');

const seconds = Number(process.argv[2] || 20);
const rabbitUrl = process.env.RABBITMQ_URL || 'amqp://guest:guest@localhost:5672';
const kafkaBrokers = (process.env.KAFKA_BROKERS || 'localhost:9092').split(',');
const counts = { rabbit: 0, kafka: 0 };

async function rabbit() {
  const conn = await amqp.connect(rabbitUrl);
  const ch = await conn.createChannel();
  await ch.assertExchange('booking.events', 'topic', { durable: true });
  const { queue } = await ch.assertQueue('', { exclusive: true });
  await ch.bindQueue(queue, 'booking.events', 'auto.#');
  await ch.consume(queue, (m) => {
    counts.rabbit++;
    const ev = JSON.parse(m.content.toString());
    console.log(`RABBIT  rk=${m.fields.routingKey}  ${ev.type}  id=${ev.id}  auto=${ev.data.auto_id}  specversion=${ev.specversion}  ct=${m.properties.contentType}`);
    ch.ack(m);
  });
  return () => conn.close();
}

async function kafka() {
  const k = new Kafka({ clientId: 'consumer-prueba', brokers: kafkaBrokers });
  const consumer = k.consumer({ groupId: `prueba-${Date.now()}` });
  await consumer.connect();
  await consumer.subscribe({ topic: 'topic-ordenes-b2b', fromBeginning: true });
  await consumer.run({
    eachMessage: async ({ message }) => {
      counts.kafka++;
      const ev = JSON.parse(message.value.toString());
      console.log(`KAFKA   key=${message.key}  ${ev.type}  id=${ev.id}  ce_type=${message.headers.ce_type}`);
    },
  });
  return () => consumer.disconnect();
}

(async () => {
  const stops = [];
  stops.push(await rabbit().catch((e) => { console.log('RabbitMQ no disponible:', e.message); return () => {}; }));
  stops.push(await kafka().catch((e) => { console.log('Kafka no disponible:', e.message); return () => {}; }));
  console.log(`Escuchando ${seconds} s…`);
  setTimeout(async () => {
    console.log(`TOTAL  rabbit=${counts.rabbit}  kafka=${counts.kafka}`);
    for (const s of stops) await s().catch(() => {});
    process.exit(0);
  }, seconds * 1000);
})();

===== services/test/contract.ts =====
================================================================================
import Ajv from 'ajv';
import addFormats from 'ajv-formats';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';
import { load } from 'js-yaml';
import { join } from 'path';
import { http, Res } from './helpers';

/**
 * Verificador de conformidad con el contrato oficial autos-openapi-v3.yaml: cada respuesta real del gateway
 * se valida contra el esquema que el contrato declara para ese endpoint y código de estado.
 */
const spec: any = load(readFileSync(join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'contracts', 'autos-openapi-v3.yaml'), 'utf8'));

const ajv = new Ajv({ strict: false, allErrors: true });
addFormats(ajv);
for (const f of ['float', 'double', 'int32', 'int64']) ajv.addFormat(f, true);
ajv.addSchema({ components: spec.components }, 'contract');

const deref = (node: any): any => (node?.$ref ? node.$ref.replace('#/', '').split('/').reduce((o: any, k: string) => o[k], spec) : node);

/** Operaciones del contrato, como "GET /autos/{id}", para comprobar que las pruebas las ejercitan todas. */
export const CONTRACT_OPERATIONS: string[] = Object.entries<any>(spec.paths).flatMap(([p, item]) =>
  Object.keys(item)
    .filter((m) => ['get', 'post', 'put', 'delete', 'patch'].includes(m))
    .map((m) => `${m.toUpperCase()} ${p}`),
);

const exercised = new Set<string>();
export const exercisedOperations = () => exercised;

export function schemaErrors(schemaName: string, data: unknown): string[] {
  const validate = ajv.compile({ $ref: `contract#/components/schemas/${schemaName}` });
  return validate(data) ? [] : (validate.errors ?? []).map((e) => `${e.instancePath || '/'} ${e.message}`);
}

/**
 * Llama al gateway (/api/v2) y valida la respuesta contra el contrato.
 *   template: la ruta tal como aparece en el contrato ("/autos/{id}"), para registrar la operación.
 *   Devuelve la respuesta; si el contrato no declara ese código de estado o el cuerpo no cumple, falla la prueba.
 */
export async function v2(
  method: string,
  template: string,
  params: Record<string, string> = {},
  opts: { body?: unknown; headers?: Record<string, string>; query?: Record<string, string | number> } = {},
): Promise<Res> {
  const path = template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, k) => encodeURIComponent(params[k]));
  const qs = opts.query ? `?${new URLSearchParams(Object.entries(opts.query).map(([k, v]) => [k, String(v)]))}` : '';
  const res = await http(method, `/api/v3${path}${qs}`, opts.body, opts.headers);
  exercised.add(`${method.toUpperCase()} ${template}`);

  const op = spec.paths[template]?.[method.toLowerCase()];
  if (!op) throw new Error(`El contrato no define ${method} ${template}`);
  const declared = deref(op.responses?.[String(res.status)]);
  if (!declared) {
    throw new Error(
      `${method} ${template} respondió ${res.status}, un código que el contrato no declara (declara: ${Object.keys(op.responses).join(', ')}). Cuerpo: ${JSON.stringify(res.body)}`,
    );
  }
  const media = declared.content?.['application/json'] ?? declared.content?.['application/problem+json'];
  if (media?.schema) {
    // Los $ref del contrato ("#/components/...") apuntan al documento registrado como 'contract'.
    const validate = ajv.compile(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(media.schema).replace(/"#\/components/g, '"contract#/components')));
    if (!validate(res.body)) {
      const errs = (validate.errors ?? []).map((e) => `${e.instancePath || '/'} ${e.message}`).join('; ');
      throw new Error(`${method} ${template} (${res.status}) no cumple el contrato: ${errs}\nCuerpo: ${JSON.stringify(res.body)}`);
    }
  }
  return res;
}

===== services/test/gateway.integration.ts =====
================================================================================
import { api, DECLINED_CARD, DRIVER, http, idem, OK_CARD, previewAndPay } from './helpers';

/**
 * Pruebas de integración de extremo a extremo: golpean el API Gateway real y atraviesan los cuatro
 * microservicios por gRPC (Catálogo, Reservas, Pagos, Identidad). Requieren el sistema levantado
 * (docker compose up, o GATEWAY_URL apuntando a Render).
 */
describe('Gateway · salud y trazabilidad', () => {
  it('/warmup confirma que los 4 microservicios responden', async () => {
    const r = await http('GET', '/warmup');
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.status).toBe('ok');
    expect(r.body.services.map((s: any) => s.service).sort()).toEqual([
      'booking-service',
      'catalog-service',
      'identity-service',
      'payments-service',
    ]);
  });

  it('devuelve X-Correlation-Id y respeta el que envía el cliente', async () => {
    const r = await http('GET', '/health', undefined, { 'X-Correlation-Id': 'test-corr-123' });
    expect(r.headers.get('x-correlation-id')).toBe('test-corr-123');
    const r2 = await http('GET', '/health');
    expect(r2.headers.get('x-correlation-id')).toMatch(/[0-9a-f-]{8,}/);
  });
});

describe('Catálogo (REST → gRPC)', () => {
  it('/details lista al menos 25 vehículos distintos', async () => {
    const r = await api('POST', '/details', {});
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(new Set(r.body.data.map((v: any) => v.vehicle_id)).size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(25);
  });

  it('/depots, /suppliers y /constants responden', async () => {
    for (const p of ['/depots', '/suppliers', '/constants']) expect((await api('POST', p, {})).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it('valida el cuerpo con ProblemDetails (400 VALIDATION_FAILED)', async () => {
    const r = await api('POST', '/orders/hold', {});
    expect(r.status).toBe(400);
    expect(r.body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_FAILED');
  });
});

describe('Saga de reserva (Reservas → Catálogo + Pagos)', () => {
  it('rechaza crear la orden sin pago aprobado (402)', async () => {
    const { previewId } = await previewAndPay(DECLINED_CARD);
    const r = await api(
      'POST',
      '/orders/create',
      { order_preview_id: previewId, payment_reference: 'NO-EXISTE', driver_details: DRIVER },
      idem(),
    );
    expect(r.status).toBe(402);
  });

  it('un pago rechazado devuelve 402 PAYMENT_DECLINED', async () => {
    const { pay } = await previewAndPay(DECLINED_CARD);
    expect(pay.status).toBe(402);
    expect(pay.body.code).toBe('PAYMENT_DECLINED');
  });

  it('flujo feliz: pago → orden → auto RESERVADO → no se reserva de nuevo → cancelar libera el auto', async () => {
    const ctx = await previewAndPay(OK_CARD);
    expect(ctx.pay.status).toBe(201);
    expect(ctx.pay.body.status).toBe('APPROVED');

    const key = idem();
    const body = {
      order_preview_id: ctx.previewId,
      payment_reference: ctx.pay.body.payment_reference,
      driver_details: DRIVER,
    };
    const create = await api('POST', '/orders/create', body, key);
    expect(create.status).toBe(201);
    expect(create.body.status).toBe('CONFIRMED');
    const order = create.body;

    // Idempotencia real: misma clave → misma respuesta, sin duplicar.
    const replay = await api('POST', '/orders/create', body, key);
    expect(replay.body.order_id).toBe(order.order_id);
    expect(replay.headers.get('idempotent-replayed')).toBe('true');

    // El auto queda RESERVED y no admite otro hold.
    const details = await api('POST', '/details', {});
    expect(details.body.data.find((v: any) => v.vehicle_id === ctx.car.vehicle_id).status).toBe('RESERVED');
    const again = await api('POST', '/orders/hold', { vehicle_id: ctx.car.vehicle_id, search_token: ctx.token });
    expect(again.status).toBe(409);
    expect(again.body.code).toBe('CAR_NO_LONGER_AVAILABLE');

    // Cancelar compensa: el auto vuelve a AVAILABLE.
    const cancel = await api('POST', `/orders/${order.order_id}/cancel`, undefined, idem());
    expect(cancel.status).toBe(200);
    const after = await api('POST', '/details', {});
    expect(after.body.data.find((v: any) => v.vehicle_id === ctx.car.vehicle_id).status).toBe('AVAILABLE');
    expect((await api('GET', `/orders/${order.order_id}`)).body.status).toBe('CANCELLED');
  });
});

describe('Identidad y seguridad', () => {
  const email = `it-${Date.now()}@test.com`;
  let token: string;

  it('registra un cliente y devuelve un JWT', async () => {
    const r = await api('POST', '/auth/register', { email, password: 'clave12345', first_name: 'It', last_name: 'Test' });
    expect(r.status).toBe(201);
    token = r.body.token;
    expect(token.split('.')).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it('inicia sesión con credenciales válidas y rechaza las inválidas', async () => {
    expect((await api('POST', '/auth/login', { email, password: 'clave12345' })).status).toBe(201);
    expect((await api('POST', '/auth/login', { email, password: 'incorrecta' })).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it('/auth/me reconoce el JWT del cliente y no un token manipulado', async () => {
    const ok = await api('GET', '/auth/me', undefined, { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` });
    expect(ok.status).toBe(200);
    expect(ok.body.email).toBe(email);
    const bad = await api('GET', '/auth/me', undefined, { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}x` });
    expect(bad.body?.email).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('el admin inicia sesión y obtiene un JWT', async () => {
    const r = await api('POST', '/admin/auth/login', {
      username: process.env.ADMIN_USERNAME ?? 'admin',
      password: process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD ?? 'rodadoec2026',
    });
    expect([200, 201]).toContain(r.status);
    expect(r.body.token).toBeDefined();
  });
});

describe('Administración', () => {
  it('lista vehículos, agencias, órdenes y usuarios', async () => {
    for (const p of ['/admin/vehicles', '/admin/depots', '/admin/orders', '/admin/users']) {
      const r = await api('GET', p);
      // 401 solo si AUTH_ENFORCE_ADMIN=true y no se envía token.
      expect([200, 401]).toContain(r.status);
      if (r.status === 200) expect(Array.isArray(r.body)).toBe(true);
    }
  });
});

describe('GraphQL federado', () => {
  const gql = (query: string, variables?: unknown) => http('POST', '/graphql', { query, variables });

  it('expone el SDL del subgrafo (_service)', async () => {
    const r = await gql('{ _service { sdl } }');
    expect(r.body.data._service.sdl).toContain('type Vehiculo');
  });

  it('resuelve vehículos con agencia y proveedor (llamadas gRPC encadenadas)', async () => {
    const r = await gql('{ vehiculos(ciudadId: 2) { id marca agencia { nombre ciudad } proveedor { nombre } } }');
    expect(r.body.errors).toBeUndefined();
    expect(r.body.data.vehiculos.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(r.body.data.vehiculos[0].agencia.ciudad).toBe('Guayaquil');
  });

  it('resuelve entidades federadas (_entities) para que otro subgrafo extienda Vehiculo', async () => {
    const all = await gql('{ vehiculos { id } }');
    const id = all.body.data.vehiculos[0].id;
    const r = await gql('query($r:[_Any!]!){ _entities(representations:$r){ ... on Vehiculo { id marca } } }', {
      r: [{ __typename: 'Vehiculo', id }],
    });
    expect(r.body.data._entities[0].id).toBe(id);
  });
});

describe('Webhooks', () => {
  it('registra y elimina una suscripción', async () => {
    const c = await api('POST', '/webhooks', {
      url: 'https://example.com/hook',
      events: ['CAR_ORDER_CONFIRMED'],
      secret: 'integration-secret',
    });
    expect(c.status).toBe(201);
    expect((await api('DELETE', `/webhooks/${c.body.id}`)).status).toBeLessThan(300);
  });
});

===== services/test/helpers.ts =====
================================================================================
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';

/** URL base del API Gateway bajo prueba (local por defecto; puede apuntar a Render con GATEWAY_URL). */
export const BASE = (process.env.GATEWAY_URL ?? 'http://localhost:3000').replace(/\/$/, '');

export interface Res<T = any> { status: number; body: T; headers: Headers }

export async function http<T = any>(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}): Promise<Res<T>> {
  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Affiliate-Id': '1001', ...headers },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await r.text();
  let parsed: any = null;
  try { parsed = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null; } catch { parsed = text; }
  return { status: r.status, body: parsed, headers: r.headers };
}

export const api = (method: string, path: string, body?: unknown, headers?: Record<string, string>) =>
  http(method, `/api/v1${path}`, body, headers);

export const idem = () => ({ 'Idempotency-Key': randomUUID() });

export const OK_CARD = { holder_name: 'Test User', number: '4242424242424242', expiry_month: 12, expiry_year: 2030, cvv: '123' };
export const DECLINED_CARD = { ...OK_CARD, number: '4000000000000002' };
export const DRIVER = { first_name: 'Test', last_name: 'User', email: 'test@example.com', phone_number: '0999999999' };

/** Ruta de 3 días con fechas únicas por ejecución, para no chocar con otras reservas. */
export function route(daysAhead = 400) {
  const start = new Date(Date.now() + daysAhead * 86400000 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 300) * 86400000);
  const end = new Date(start.getTime() + 3 * 86400000);
  return {
    pickup: { datetime: start.toISOString(), location: { city_id: 2 } },
    dropoff: { datetime: end.toISOString(), location: { city_id: 2 } },
  };
}

/** Busca y bloquea un auto libre; devuelve lo necesario para continuar el flujo. */
export async function holdAvailableCar(driverAge = 30) {
  const r = route();
  const s = await api('POST', '/search', { booker: { country: 'ec' }, currency: 'USD', driver: { age: driverAge }, route: r });
  const car = s.body.data.find((c: any) => c.available !== false) ?? s.body.data[0];
  const h = await api('POST', '/orders/hold', { vehicle_id: car.vehicle_id, search_token: s.body.search_token });
  return { search: s, car, hold: h, token: s.body.search_token };
}

export async function previewAndPay(card = OK_CARD) {
  const ctx = await holdAvailableCar();
  const p = await api('POST', '/orders/preview', { vehicle_id: ctx.car.vehicle_id, search_token: ctx.token, extras: [] });
  const previewId = p.body.data.order_preview_id;
  const pay = await api('POST', '/payments', { order_preview_id: previewId, card });
  return { ...ctx, preview: p, previewId, pay };
}

===== services/test/mock-inventory-server.js =====
================================================================================
/**
 * Servidor de prueba del InventoryStockService de Booking Prototipo (contracts/proto/inventory/inventory.proto).
 * Sirve para verificar localmente que el nodo de Autos empuja su stock por gRPC:
 *   node test/mock-inventory-server.js [puerto]        (por defecto 50999)
 *   INVENTORY_GRPC_URL=127.0.0.1:50999 en el servicio de Reservas
 * Imprime cada mensaje recibido y valida el contrato (fecha YYYY-MM-DD, franja HH:00|HH:30, unidades 0|1).
 */
const grpc = require('@grpc/grpc-js');
const loader = require('@grpc/proto-loader');
const path = require('path');

const def = loader.loadSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'contracts', 'proto', 'inventory', 'inventory.proto'), {
  keepCase: true, longs: String, enums: String, defaults: true, oneofs: true,
});
const { InventoryStockService } = grpc.loadPackageDefinition(def).inventory;
const port = process.argv[2] || 50999;

const valid = (m) => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(m.date) && /^\d{2}:(00|30)$/.test(m.time_slot) && [0, 1].includes(m.available_units);
let streamed = 0;

const server = new grpc.Server();
server.addService(InventoryStockService.service, {
  PushStockUpdate(call, cb) {
    const m = call.request;
    console.log(`PUSH   ${m.service_id} ${m.item_id} ${m.date} ${m.time_slot} unidades=${m.available_units} ${valid(m) ? 'OK' : 'INVÁLIDO'}`);
    cb(null, { success: valid(m), message: valid(m) ? 'stock actualizado' : 'mensaje inválido', processed_at: String(Date.now()) });
  },
  StreamInventoryChanges(call, cb) {
    let total = 0;
    call.on('data', (e) => { total++; streamed++; if (total <= 3) console.log(`STREAM ${e.service_id} ${e.item_id} ${e.date} ${e.time_slot} delta=${e.delta}`); });
    call.on('end', () => { console.log(`STREAM fin: ${total} eventos`); cb(null, { total_events_processed: total, is_synchronized: true, last_sync_hash: 'mock-' + total }); });
  },
});
server.bindAsync(`0.0.0.0:${port}`, grpc.ServerCredentials.createInsecure(), (err) => {
  if (err) throw err;
  console.log(`InventoryStockService (mock) escuchando en :${port}`);
});

================================================================================
===== services/test/v2-contract.integration.ts =====
================================================================================
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
import { api, http } from './helpers';
import { CONTRACT_OPERATIONS, exercisedOperations, schemaErrors, v2 } from './contract';

/**
 * Conformidad con el contrato oficial autos-openapi-v3.yaml. Cada respuesta se valida contra el esquema del
 * contrato (ver contract.ts) y al final se comprueba que se ejercitó TODA operación que el contrato define.
 * Requiere el sistema levantado (docker compose up) y la bandera AUTH_ENFORCE_V2 apagada.
 */
const idem = () => ({ 'Idempotency-Key': randomUUID() });
const day = (offset: number) => new Date(Date.now() + offset * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const PICKUP = day(30);
const DROPOFF = day(33);

const driver = (extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  full_name: 'Carlos Andrés Zambrano',
  email: 'carlos.zambrano@example.com',
  phone: '+593987654321',
  license_number: '1723456789',
  country_license: 'Ecuador',
  age: 32,
  ...extra,
});

const reserva = (vehicleId: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  vehicle_id: vehicleId,
  pickup_date: PICKUP,
  dropoff_date: DROPOFF,
  pickup_time: '10:00',
  dropoff_time: '10:00',
  pickup_location: 'Aeropuerto Mariscal Sucre (UIO), Quito',
  coverage_type: 'TOTAL',
  driver: driver(),
  ...extra,
});

const usedVehicles: string[] = [];
let freeCars: string[] = [];
const nextCar = () => {
  const id = freeCars.shift();
  if (!id) throw new Error('No quedan autos DISPONIBLES para la prueba.');
  usedVehicles.push(id);
  return id;
};

let adminUuids: Record<string, string> = {};

beforeAll(async () => {
  const list = await v2('GET', '/autos', {}, { query: { limit: 500 } });
  freeCars = list.body.data.filter((c: any) => c.estado === 'DISPONIBLE').map((c: any) => c.vehicle_id);
  const admin = await api('GET', '/admin/vehicles');
  adminUuids = Object.fromEntries((admin.body as any[]).map((v) => [v.vehicle_id, v.id]));
});

afterAll(async () => {
  // Deja la flota como estaba: los autos usados vuelven a DISPONIBLE (sin esperar las 2 h de cooldown).
  for (const id of usedVehicles) if (adminUuids[id]) await api('POST', `/admin/vehicles/${adminUuids[id]}/release`);
});

describe('Telemetría y salud', () => {
  it('GET /autos/health: HEALTHY con telemetría del nodo', async () => {
    const r = await v2('GET', '/autos/health');
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.status).toBe('HEALTHY');
    expect(r.body.version).toBe('3.0.0');
    expect(r.body.vertical).toBe('AUTOS');
    expect(r.body.total_vehiculos).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(25);
  });
});

describe('Catálogo y búsqueda', () => {
  it('GET /autos: solo DISPONIBLES, con filtros', async () => {
    const r = await v2('GET', '/autos', {}, { query: { category: 'SUV', transmission: 'AUTOMATICO', limit: 5 } });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.data.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5);
    for (const c of r.body.data) {
      expect(c.categoria).toBe('SUV');
      expect(c.transmision).toBe('AUTOMATICO');
      expect(c.estado).toBe('DISPONIBLE');
    }
  });

  it('GET /autos con filtro inválido responde 400 con invalid_params', async () => {
    // El contrato solo declara 200/500 para este GET: la validación de filtros (400) es un añadido del gateway.
    const r = await http('GET', '/api/v2/autos?category=TANQUE');
    expect(r.status).toBe(400);
    expect(r.body.invalid_params[0].name).toBe('category');
  });

  it('POST /autos/search: franjas de 30 min, metadata y recargo por edad', async () => {
    const body = { pickup_location: 'UIO', pickup_date: PICKUP, pickup_time: '10:00', dropoff_date: DROPOFF, dropoff_time: '10:30', driver_age: 32 };
    const normal = await v2('POST', '/autos/search', {}, { body });
    expect(normal.status).toBe(200);
    expect(normal.body.metadata.surcharge_applied).toBe(false);
    expect(normal.body.metadata.total_results).toBe(normal.body.data.length);

    const joven = await v2('POST', '/autos/search', {}, { body: { ...body, driver_age: 22 } });
    expect(joven.body.metadata.surcharge_applied).toBe(true);
    expect(joven.body.metadata.surcharge_amount).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    if (joven.body.data.length && normal.body.data.length) {
      expect(joven.body.data[0].precio_total).toBeGreaterThan(normal.body.data[0].precio_total);
    }
  });

  it('POST /autos/search: menor de 18 → 422; hora fuera de franja → 400; fecha pasada → 400', async () => {
    const body = { pickup_location: 'Quito', pickup_date: PICKUP, pickup_time: '10:00', dropoff_date: DROPOFF, dropoff_time: '10:00' };
    const menor = await v2('POST', '/autos/search', {}, { body: { ...body, driver_age: 17 } });
    expect(menor.status).toBe(422);
    expect(menor.body.code).toBe('DRIVER_UNDERAGE');

    const franja = await v2('POST', '/autos/search', {}, { body: { ...body, pickup_time: '10:17' } });
    expect(franja.status).toBe(400);
    expect(franja.body.invalid_params.map((p: any) => p.name)).toContain('pickup_time');

    // Errores semánticos de fechas: 422 INVALID_DATES (no activan compensaciones de Saga).
    const pasada = await v2('POST', '/autos/search', {}, { body: { ...body, pickup_date: '2020-01-01' } });
    expect(pasada.status).toBe(422);
    expect(pasada.body.code).toBe('INVALID_DATES');
    expect(pasada.body.invalid_params.map((p: any) => p.name)).toContain('pickup_date');
  });

  it('GET /autos/flota: 6 estados posibles y telemetría de kilometraje', async () => {
    const r = await v2('GET', '/autos/flota');
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(25);
    expect(r.body[0]).toHaveProperty('kilometraje_actual');
  });

  it('GET /autos/{id} y /autos/{id}/coberturas', async () => {
    const id = freeCars[0];
    const detalle = await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id });
    expect(detalle.body.vehicle_id).toBe(id);
    const cob = await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}/coberturas', { id });
    const total = cob.body.opciones.find((o: any) => o.tipo === 'TOTAL');
    expect(total.franquicia).toBe(0);
    expect(total.deposito_garantia).toBe(0);
    expect(cob.body.opciones.find((o: any) => o.tipo === 'BASICA').franquicia).toBe(1200);
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: 'no-existe' })).status).toBe(404);
  });
});

describe('Reservas: ciclo completo con máquina de estados', () => {
  let car: string;
  let pnr: string;

  it('POST /autos/orders/create: reserva con cobertura, PNR de 8 caracteres e idempotencia', async () => {
    car = nextCar();
    const key = idem();
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car), headers: key });
    expect(r.status).toBe(201);
    pnr = r.body.pnr;
    expect(pnr).toMatch(/^CAR[A-Z0-9]{5}$/);
    expect(r.body.order_id).toBe(pnr);
    expect(r.body.status).toBe('CONFIRMED');
    expect(r.body.tipo_cobertura).toBe('TOTAL');
    expect(r.body.deposito_garantia).toBe(0);
    expect(r.body.total_price.total).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const replay = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car), headers: key });
    expect(replay.body.pnr).toBe(pnr);
    expect(replay.headers.get('idempotent-replayed')).toBe('true');
  });

  it('el auto queda RESERVADO y desaparece de las búsquedas; una segunda reserva da 409', async () => {
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.estado).toBe('RESERVADO');
    const lista = await v2('GET', '/autos', {}, { query: { limit: 500 } });
    expect(lista.body.data.map((c: any) => c.vehicle_id)).not.toContain(car);
    const otra = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car), headers: idem() });
    expect(otra.status).toBe(409);
    expect(otra.body.code).toBe('VEHICLE_UNAVAILABLE');
  });

  it('POST /autos/orders/create valida el cuerpo, la edad y exige Idempotency-Key', async () => {
    // Idempotency-Key es obligatoria (parámetro required del contrato): sin ella, 400.
    const sinClave = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(freeCars[0]) });
    expect(sinClave.status).toBe(400);

    const invalida = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: { vehicle_id: freeCars[0] }, headers: idem() });
    expect(invalida.status).toBe(400);
    expect(invalida.body.invalid_params.map((p: any) => p.name)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['pickup_date', 'driver']));

    const menor = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(freeCars[0], { driver: driver({ age: 17 }) }), headers: idem() });
    expect(menor.status).toBe(422);
  });

  it('GET /autos/orders y GET /autos/orders/{orderId} (por PNR)', async () => {
    const lista = await v2('GET', '/autos/orders');
    expect(lista.body.map((o: any) => o.pnr)).toContain(pnr);
    const una = await v2('GET', '/autos/orders/{orderId}', { orderId: pnr });
    expect(una.body.auto_id).toBe(car);
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/orders/{orderId}', { orderId: 'CAR00000' })).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('POST /autos/reservas/{id}/checkin: el auto pasa a LISTO_PARA_ENTREGAR', async () => {
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/reservas/{id}/checkin', { id: pnr }, { body: { documento_identidad: '1723456789', numero_licencia: '1723456789', fecha_vencimiento_licencia: '2030-12-31' }, headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.checkin_status).toBe('CHECKIN_COMPLETED');
    expect(r.body.voucher_qr_data).toContain(pnr);
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.estado).toBe('LISTO_PARA_ENTREGAR');
  });

  it('check-in con licencia vencida → 400', async () => {
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/reservas/{id}/checkin', { id: pnr }, { body: { documento_identidad: '1723456789', fecha_vencimiento_licencia: '2020-01-01' }, headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(400);
  });

  it('no se puede devolver un auto que aún no se entregó (409)', async () => {
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/{id}/checkin-devolucion', { id: pnr }, { body: { km_retorno: 100 }, headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('POST /autos/orders/{id}/checkin-entrega: el auto pasa a ENTREGADO', async () => {
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/{id}/checkin-entrega', { id: pnr }, { body: { licencia_conducir: '1723456789', km_salida: 12500, tecnico_entrega: 'Roberto Paredes' }, headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.estado_orden).toBe('ENTREGADO');
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.estado).toBe('ENTREGADO');
  });

  it('una orden entregada ya no se puede cancelar (409)', async () => {
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/{orderId}/cancel', { orderId: pnr }, { headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('POST /autos/orders/{id}/checkin-devolucion: odómetro, km recorridos y cooldown de 2 h', async () => {
    const menos = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/{id}/checkin-devolucion', { id: pnr }, { body: { km_retorno: 100 }, headers: idem() });
    expect(menos.status).toBe(400); // el odómetro de retorno no puede ser menor que el de salida

    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/{id}/checkin-devolucion', { id: pnr }, { body: { km_retorno: 12950, nivel_combustible: 100 }, headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.estado_orden).toBe('COMPLETADA');
    expect(r.body.km_recorridos).toBe(450);
    expect(r.body.cooldown.estado).toBe('EN_LIMPIEZA');
    expect(r.body.cooldown.cooldown_horas).toBe(2);
  });

  it('cooldown: el auto está EN_LIMPIEZA, fuera de resultados, con cronómetro en la flota y reservarlo da 409 VEHICLE_IN_CLEANING_COOLDOWN', async () => {
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.estado).toBe('EN_LIMPIEZA');
    const lista = await v2('GET', '/autos', {}, { query: { limit: 500 } });
    expect(lista.body.data.map((c: any) => c.vehicle_id)).not.toContain(car);

    const flota = (await v2('GET', '/autos/flota')).body.find((c: any) => c.id === car);
    expect(flota.cooldown_activo).toBe(true);
    expect(flota.segundos_restantes_cooldown).toBeGreaterThan(7000);
    expect(flota.segundos_restantes_cooldown).toBeLessThanOrEqual(7200);

    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car), headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(409);
    expect(r.body.code).toBe('VEHICLE_UNAVAILABLE'); // único código que activa la compensación LIFO en Booking
    expect(r.body.reason).toBe('VEHICLE_IN_CLEANING_COOLDOWN');
    expect(r.body.detail).toMatch(/limpieza/);
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.cleaning_cooldown.activo).toBe(true);

    const orden = (await v2('GET', '/autos/orders/{orderId}', { orderId: pnr })).body;
    expect(orden.status).toBe('COMPLETADA');
    expect(orden.cooldown_info.activo).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('Cancelación con compensación (Saga)', () => {
  it('POST /autos/reservas + cancel: reembolso 100% y el auto vuelve a DISPONIBLE', async () => {
    const car = nextCar();
    const created = await v2('POST', '/autos/reservas', {}, { body: reserva(car, { coverage_type: 'BASICA' }), headers: idem() });
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    expect(created.body.deposito_garantia).toBe(1000);

    const key = idem();
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/{orderId}/cancel', { orderId: created.body.pnr }, { body: { motivo: 'Cambio de itinerario', solicitante: 'SAGA_ORCHESTRATOR' }, headers: key });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.status).toBe('CANCELLED');
    expect(r.body.refund.percentage).toBe(100);
    expect(r.body.refund.amount).toBe(created.body.total_price.total);
    expect(r.body.inventory_restoration.new_state).toBe('DISPONIBLE');
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.estado).toBe('DISPONIBLE');

    const orden = await v2('GET', '/autos/orders/{orderId}', { orderId: created.body.pnr });
    expect(orden.body.reembolso_id).toBe(r.body.refund.refund_id);

    const mal = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/{orderId}/cancel', { orderId: created.body.pnr }, { body: { solicitante: 'EL_VECINO' }, headers: idem() });
    expect(mal.status).toBe(400);
  });

  it('contrato v3: acepta driver en inglés, conductor con alias en español y coverage_type BASIC', async () => {
    const car = nextCar();
    const ingles = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car, { coverage_type: 'BASIC' }), headers: idem() });
    expect(ingles.status).toBe(201);
    expect(ingles.body.tipo_cobertura).toBe('BASICA');
    expect(ingles.body.vehicle_id).toBe(car);
    expect(ingles.body.voucher_qr_payload).toContain(ingles.body.pnr);

    const car2 = nextCar();
    const { driver: _d, coverage_type: _c, ...sinDriver } = reserva(car2) as any;
    const espanol = await v2('POST', '/autos/reservas', {}, {
      body: { ...sinDriver, conductor: { nombre: 'Ana Pérez', correo: 'ana@example.com', telefono: '0999999999', licencia_conducir: 'A123', edad: 40 } },
      headers: idem(),
    });
    expect(espanol.status).toBe(201);
  });

  it('driver sin age → 400; menor de 18 → 422', async () => {
    const car = freeCars[0];
    const sinEdad = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car, { driver: driver({ age: undefined }) }), headers: idem() });
    expect(sinEdad.status).toBe(400);
    expect(sinEdad.body.invalid_params.map((p: any) => p.name)).toContain('driver.age');
  });

  it('fechas inválidas en la reserva → 422 INVALID_DATES (no 400)', async () => {
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(freeCars[0], { pickup_date: DROPOFF, dropoff_date: PICKUP }), headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(422);
    expect(r.body.code).toBe('INVALID_DATES');
  });

  it('Idempotency-Key reutilizada con otro cuerpo → 400 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT', async () => {
    const car = nextCar();
    const key = idem();
    expect((await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car), headers: key })).status).toBe(201);
    const otro = await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car, { driver: driver({ full_name: 'Otra Persona' }) }), headers: key });
    expect(otro.status).toBe(400);
    expect(otro.body.code).toBe('IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT');
  });

  it('POST /autos/{id}/devolucion: devolución directa con cooldown de 2 h', async () => {
    const car = nextCar();
    await v2('POST', '/autos/orders/create', {}, { body: reserva(car), headers: idem() });
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/{id}/devolucion', { id: car }, { headers: idem() });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.estado).toBe('EN_LIMPIEZA');
    expect(r.body.cooldown_horas).toBe(2);
    expect((await v2('POST', '/autos/{id}/devolucion', { id: car }, { headers: idem() })).status).toBe(409);
  });
});

describe('Mantenimiento preventivo (RBAC)', () => {
  it('un rol CLIENTE no puede registrar mantenimiento (403)', async () => {
    const car = nextCar();
    const r = await v2('POST', '/autos/{id}/mantenimiento/registro', { id: car }, {
      body: { kilometraje_registrado: 500, nivel_combustible: 80, tecnico_id: 'tec-01' },
      headers: { ...idem(), 'X-Role': 'CLIENTE' },
    });
    expect(r.status).toBe(403);
  });

  it('MANTENIMIENTO registra el odómetro; al superar el umbral hay alerta, el auto sale de circulación y se emite el evento', async () => {
    const car = nextCar();
    const headers = () => ({ ...idem(), 'X-Role': 'MANTENIMIENTO' });
    const alerta = await v2('POST', '/autos/{id}/mantenimiento/registro', { id: car }, {
      body: { kilometraje_registrado: 10250, nivel_combustible: 100, tecnico_id: 'tec-rosero-01', estado_neumaticos: 'BUENO' },
      headers: headers(),
    });
    expect(alerta.status).toBe(200);
    expect(alerta.body.mantenimiento_requerido).toBe(true);
    expect(alerta.body.alerta_preventiva).toContain('ALERTA PREVENTIVA');
    expect(alerta.body.bitacora_id).toMatch(/^BIT-AUTO-/);
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.estado).toBe('EN_MANTENIMIENTO');

    const hecho = await v2('POST', '/autos/{id}/mantenimiento/registro', { id: car }, {
      body: { kilometraje_registrado: 10250, nivel_combustible: 100, tecnico_id: 'tec-rosero-01', mantenimiento_realizado: true, nuevo_proximo_mantenimiento_km: 15000 },
      headers: headers(),
    });
    expect(hecho.body.mantenimiento_requerido).toBe(false);
    expect(hecho.body.proximo_mantenimiento_km).toBe(15000);
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/{id}', { id: car })).body.estado).toBe('DISPONIBLE');
  });

  it('valida el cuerpo (400)', async () => {
    // El contrato no declara 400 para esta operación: se valida igualmente el cuerpo con invalid_params.
    const r = await http('POST', `/api/v2/autos/${freeCars[0]}/mantenimiento/registro`, { nivel_combustible: 250 }, { ...idem(), 'X-Role': 'ADMIN' });
    expect(r.status).toBe(400);
    expect(r.body.invalid_params.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  });
});

describe('Rutas que Booking puede usar como baseUrlRest', () => {
  it('/api/v3, /api/v2 y la raíz (/autos, /reservas) llegan a la misma implementación', async () => {
    for (const base of ['/api/v3', '/api/v2', '']) {
      const h = await http('GET', `${base}/autos/health`);
      expect(h.status).toBe(200);
      expect(h.body.status).toBe('HEALTHY');
    }
    // POST /reservas en la raíz (Guía §4): 400 por cuerpo vacío demuestra que la ruta existe y valida.
    const r = await http('POST', '/reservas', {}, idem());
    expect(r.status).toBe(400);
    expect(r.body.instance).toBe('/reservas');
    expect(r.body.invalid_params.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

describe('Acuerdo B2B de promociones (Cátedra RDA2 §9)', () => {
  it('GET y PUT /autos/promociones/contrato', async () => {
    const antes = await v2('GET', '/autos/promociones/contrato');
    expect(antes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(antes.body.vertical).toBe('AUTOS');

    const r = await v2('PUT', '/autos/promociones/contrato', {}, {
      body: { permite_promociones: true, porcentaje_descuento_maximo: 12.5, destinos_habilitados: ['Quito', 'Cuenca'], vigencia_hasta: '2027-06-30', condiciones_combo: 'Combo auto + hotel' },
      headers: idem(),
    });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body.contrato.porcentaje_descuento_maximo).toBe(12.5);
    expect((await v2('GET', '/autos/promociones/contrato')).body.destinos_habilitados).toEqual(['Quito', 'Cuenca']);

    const mal = await v2('PUT', '/autos/promociones/contrato', {}, { body: { permite_promociones: 'si', porcentaje_descuento_maximo: 150 }, headers: idem() });
    expect(mal.status).toBe(400);
  });
});

describe('Feed Outbox de eventos CloudEvents', () => {
  it('GET /autos/eventos: eventos del ciclo de vida con correlation_id y paginación por cursor', async () => {
    const r = await v2('GET', '/autos/eventos', {}, { query: { limit: 100 } });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    const types = new Set(r.body.events.map((e: any) => e.type));
    expect(types).toContain('ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.order.confirmed');
    expect(types).toContain('ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.order.cancelled');
    expect(types).toContain('ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.cooldown.started');
    expect(types).toContain('ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.mantenimiento.alerta');

    const schemaOf: Record<string, string> = {
      'ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.order.confirmed': 'CloudEventAutoOrderConfirmed',
      'ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.order.cancelled': 'CloudEventAutoOrderCancelled',
      'ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.cooldown.started': 'CloudEventAutoCleaningCooldownStarted',
      'ec.edu.epn.booking.autos.mantenimiento.alerta': 'CloudEventAutoMantenimientoAlerta',
    };
    for (const e of r.body.events) {
      const name = schemaOf[e.type];
      if (name) expect({ type: e.type, errors: schemaErrors(name, e) }).toEqual({ type: e.type, errors: [] });
    }

    const primero = await v2('GET', '/autos/eventos', {}, { query: { limit: 1 } });
    const siguiente = await v2('GET', '/autos/eventos', {}, { query: { limit: 100, after: primero.body.events[0].id } });
    expect(siguiente.body.events.map((e: any) => e.id)).not.toContain(primero.body.events[0].id);
  });
});

describe('Cobertura total del contrato', () => {
  it('las pruebas ejercitaron TODAS las operaciones que define autos-openapi-v3.yaml', () => {
    const faltan = CONTRACT_OPERATIONS.filter((op) => !exercisedOperations().has(op));
    expect(faltan).toEqual([]);
    expect(CONTRACT_OPERATIONS.length).toBe(19);
  });
});

================================================================================
===== contracts/graphql/autos.graphql =====
================================================================================
# Subgrafo federado GraphQL del dominio Autos (Reto 3 - Apollo Federation). Versión del contrato: ver CHANGELOG.md.
# version: 1.0.0
#
# Se publica en POST /graphql del API Gateway de Autos. Un gateway central (Apollo Gateway/Router) lo
# compone con los subgrafos de los otros dominios del Booking (Alojamientos, Atracciones, Vuelos).
# Las entidades llevan @key para que otros subgrafos puedan extenderlas (por ejemplo, un subgrafo de
# Reservas globales que agregue campos a `Orden`).
#
# Convenciones: camelCase en español (igual que el esquema de ejemplo de Atracciones).
# Compatibilidad: solo se agregan campos/tipos opcionales (MINOR). Quitar o renombrar un campo es MAJOR
# y exige publicar un esquema nuevo (autos.v2.graphql); mientras tanto un campo viejo se marca @deprecated.

enum EstadoVehiculo {
  DISPONIBLE
  RESERVADO
}

enum EstadoOrden {
  CONFIRMADA
  CANCELADA
  PENDIENTE
}

type Proveedor {
  id: ID!
  nombre: String!
}

type Agencia @key(fields: "id") {
  id: ID!
  nombre: String!
  ciudadId: Int!
  ciudad: String!
  aeropuerto: String
  puntuacion: Float
  vehiculos: [Vehiculo!]!
}

type Vehiculo @key(fields: "id") {
  id: ID!
  marca: String!
  modelo: String!
  tipo: String!
  transmision: String!
  puertas: Int!
  maletas: Int!
  asientos: Int!
  precioPorDia: Float!
  imagenUrl: String
  estado: EstadoVehiculo!
  edadMinima: Int!
  agencia: Agencia!
  proveedor: Proveedor!
}

type Orden @key(fields: "id") {
  id: ID!
  codigo: String!
  estado: EstadoOrden!
  vehiculo: Vehiculo!
  total: Float!
  moneda: String!
  extras: [String!]!
  recogida: String
  entrega: String
  clienteId: ID
  fechaCreacion: String!
}

extend type Query {
  "Autos de la ciudad o aeropuerto indicados. Sin filtros devuelve todo el catálogo."
  vehiculos(ciudadId: Int, aeropuerto: String, tipos: [String!], transmision: [String!]): [Vehiculo!]!
  vehiculo(id: ID!): Vehiculo
  agencias: [Agencia!]!
  orden(id: ID!): Orden
  ordenes(estado: EstadoOrden, clienteId: ID): [Orden!]!
}

extend type Mutation {
  "Cancela una orden confirmada (libera el auto y reembolsa el pago simulado)."
  cancelarOrden(id: ID!): Orden!
}

================================================================================
===== contracts/proto/autos/booking/v1/booking.proto =====
================================================================================
// Servicio de Reservas: orquesta el flujo de compra (búsqueda -> hold -> preview -> pago -> orden).
// Actúa como orquestador de una saga: pago aprobado -> reserva atómica del auto -> orden, con
// compensaciones (reembolso del pago / liberación del auto) si un paso falla.
syntax = "proto3";

package autos.booking.v1;

import "autos/common/v1/common.proto";

service BookingService {
  rpc Search(SearchRequest) returns (SearchResponse);
  rpc Hold(HoldRequest) returns (HoldResponse);
  rpc Preview(PreviewRequest) returns (PreviewResponse);
  rpc Pay(PayRequest) returns (PaymentResult);
  rpc CreateOrder(CreateOrderRequest) returns (Order);
  rpc GetOrder(OrderRef) returns (Order);
  rpc ModifyOrder(ModifyOrderRequest) returns (Order);
  rpc CancelOrder(OrderRef) returns (autos.common.v1.Empty);
  rpc ListOrders(ListOrdersRequest) returns (OrderList);

  rpc ListWebhooks(autos.common.v1.Empty) returns (WebhookList);
  rpc CreateWebhook(CreateWebhookRequest) returns (Webhook);
  rpc DeleteWebhook(WebhookRef) returns (autos.common.v1.Empty);
  // Registro de entregas de webhooks (éxitos y fallos tras agotar los reintentos): hace de "dead letter" consultable.
  rpc ListWebhookDeliveries(autos.common.v1.Empty) returns (WebhookDeliveryList);

  // ── Contrato REST v2 (autos-openapi-v2.yaml) ──────────────────────────
  // Reserva directa B2B: sin hold/preview; la liquidación se simula internamente en Pagos (canal B2B).
  rpc CreateOrderV2(CreateOrderV2Request) returns (Order);
  rpc CancelOrderV2(OrderRef) returns (CancelResult);
  // Check-in 1 (digital express): valida licencia y deja el auto LISTO_PARA_ENTREGAR.
  rpc CheckinDigital(CheckinDigitalRequest) returns (CheckinDigitalResult);
  // Check-in 2 (mostrador): odómetro de salida, el auto pasa a ENTREGADO.
  rpc DeliverOrder(DeliverOrderRequest) returns (Order);
  // Check-in 3 (patio): odómetro de retorno; la orden se completa y el auto entra en cooldown de limpieza de 2 h.
  rpc ReturnOrder(ReturnOrderRequest) returns (ReturnResult);
  // Devolución directa del vehículo (sin pasar por la orden).
  rpc ReturnVehicle(ReturnVehicleRequest) returns (ReturnResult);
  rpc RegisterMaintenance(RegisterMaintenanceRequest) returns (MaintenanceResult);
  // Feed Transactional Outbox de eventos CloudEvents.
  rpc ListEvents(ListEventsRequest) returns (EventList);
  // Acuerdo B2B de promociones y combos.
  rpc GetPromoContract(autos.common.v1.Empty) returns (PromoContract);
  rpc SetPromoContract(PromoContract) returns (PromoContract);
  // Sincronización masiva del stock de la flota hacia Booking Prototipo (inventory.StreamInventoryChanges).
  rpc SyncInventory(autos.common.v1.Empty) returns (SyncResult);
}

message SearchFilters {
  repeated string car_types = 1;
  repeated string transmission = 2;
}

message SearchRequest {
  string booker_country = 1;
  string currency = 2;
  autos.common.v1.Driver driver = 3;
  autos.common.v1.Route route = 4;
  SearchFilters filters = 5;
  int32 maximum_results = 6; // 0 = 100
}

message SearchResult {
  string vehicle_id = 1;
  double price = 2;
  int32 supplier_id = 3;
  bool available = 4;
  int32 min_driver_age = 5;
  int32 depot_id = 6;
}

message SearchResponse {
  string request_id = 1;
  repeated SearchResult data = 2;
  int32 total_results = 3;
  string search_token = 4;
}

message HoldRequest {
  string vehicle_id = 1;
  string search_token = 2;
}

message HoldResponse {
  string hold_id = 1;
  string expires_at = 2;
  string status = 3; // HELD | FAILED
}

message PreviewRequest {
  string vehicle_id = 1;
  string search_token = 2;
  string hold_id = 3;
  repeated string extras = 4;
}

message PriceBreakdown {
  double base_price = 1;
  double extras_price = 2;
  int32 days = 3;
}

message PreviewResponse {
  string request_id = 1;
  string order_preview_id = 2;
  double total_price = 3;
  string currency = 4;
  PriceBreakdown breakdown = 5;
}

message Card {
  string holder_name = 1;
  string number = 2;
  int32 expiry_month = 3;
  int32 expiry_year = 4;
  string cvv = 5;
}

message PayRequest {
  string order_preview_id = 1;
  Card card = 2;
}

message PaymentResult {
  string payment_reference = 1;
  string status = 2;
  double amount = 3;
  string currency = 4;
  string card_brand = 5;
  string card_last4 = 6;
  string created_at = 7;
}

message CreateOrderRequest {
  string order_preview_id = 1;
  string payment_reference = 2;
  autos.common.v1.DriverDetails driver_details = 3;
  string customer_id = 4; // vacío = reserva de invitado
}

message Order {
  string order_id = 1;
  string locator = 2;
  string status = 3; // CONFIRMED | CANCELLED | PENDING | ENTREGADO | COMPLETADA
  autos.common.v1.Vehicle vehicle = 4;
  autos.common.v1.Route route = 5;
  repeated string extras = 6;
  double total_price = 7;
  string currency = 8;
  string creation_date = 9;
  string customer_id = 10;
  string payment_reference = 11;
  autos.common.v1.DriverDetails driver_details = 12;
  // Campos del contrato v2 (vacío / 0 = no definido)
  string coverage_type = 13;     // BASICA | TOTAL
  double deposit = 14;           // depósito de garantía retenido
  PricingV2 pricing = 15;
  int32 km_salida = 16;
  int32 km_retorno = 17;
  string checkin_at = 18;
  string delivered_at = 19;
  string returned_at = 20;
  RefundInfo refund = 21;        // presente solo si la orden se canceló
}

message PricingV2 {
  int32 days = 1;
  double base = 2;
  double coverage = 3;
  double surcharge = 4;
}

message RefundInfo {
  string refund_id = 1;
  double amount = 2;
  string currency = 3;
  int32 percentage = 4;
  string status = 5;
}

message DriverV2 {
  string full_name = 1;
  string email = 2;
  string phone = 3;
  string license = 4;
  string license_country = 5;
  int32 age = 6;
  string flight_number = 7;
}

message CreateOrderV2Request {

  string dropoff_date = 4;
  string dropoff_time = 5;
  autos.common.v1.Location pickup_location = 6;
  autos.common.v1.Location dropoff_location = 7;
  string coverage_type = 8;   // BASICA | TOTAL
  DriverV2 driver = 9;
  string customer_id = 10;
}

message CancelResult {

  RefundInfo refund = 2;

}
